*Rowzatul Zannat,Abdullah Al Shafi,K. M. Azharul Hasan,Atia Shahnaz Ipa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BanglaMed-QA，一个专为孟加拉语医学领域设计的问答系统。该系统构建了包含506种疾病、9个类别、4,493个问答对的知识库，结合领域特定的词根词典、同义词集和词性标注技术，采用SVM进行问题分类，并通过多种相似度度量（余弦、Jaccard、BM25、Levenshtein）及软/硬投票方法进行查询匹配。系统在自动评估中达到95%的F1分数，人工满意度评分为0.9/1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语等低资源语言的医学问答系统研究非常匮乏，主要受限于数据集不足和缺乏针对这些语言定制的系统。这导致孟加拉语使用者难以获取可靠的医疗健康信息，存在显著的信息获取鸿沟。

Method: 首先构建包含4,493个问答对、覆盖506种疾病和9个类别的结构化医学知识库；其次提出领域特定的词根词典和同义词集以提升语义理解，并利用词性标注进行指代消解；采用监督机器学习（SVM效果最佳）进行问题分类；使用余弦、Jaccard、BM25和Levenshtein多种相似度度量，结合软投票和硬投票方法进行查询匹配。

Result: 自动评估中达到95%的F1分数，人工评估获得0.9/1.0的平均满意度评分，验证了系统在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: BanglaMed-QA成功证明了在低资源语言中构建高质量医学问答系统的可行性，有望缩小孟加拉语使用者的医疗信息差距，为类似低资源语言的医学QA研究提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Medical question answering (QA) systems have become crucial tools for providing reliable health information. But they remain very unexplored for low-resource languages like Bangla due to limited datasets and systems tailored to these languages. To address this, we introduce BanglaMed-QA, a robust QA system specifically designed for the Bangla medical domain. The process begins with building a structured medical knowledge base that includes 4,493 QA pairs in 9 categories under 506 diseases. To improve semantic comprehension, domain-specific root word dictionaries and synonym sets are proposed, in addition to part-of-speech tagging for anaphora resolution. We adopt supervised machine learning models in which SVM is found to be the best model to categorize questions. Multiple similarity metrics, including cosine, Jaccard, BM25, and Levenshtein, are applied with soft and hard voting methods for query matching. The performance of the QA system has been evaluated in two aspects, with a 95% F1 score in an automated evaluation and an average human satisfaction rating of 0.9 out of 1.0. This validates the real-world application of BanglaMed-QA in closing the healthcare information gap for Bangla speakers.

</details>


### [45] [PersonaForge: Realistic Multi-Turn User Simulation for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28378)
*Hanglong Lv,Dawei Zhu,Lei Li,Bowen Ye,Huaqiu Liu,Yifan Song,Bofei Gao,Weimin Xiong,Jinhao Dong,Chenhong He,Lingpeng Kong,Qi Liu,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaForge，一个用于合成真实多轮用户-智能体交互的用户模拟框架，并构建了PersonaForge-Bench基准。实验表明基于该框架训练的Qwen3.5-27B在综合得分上提升4.1%，尤其在任务完成和响应质量维度上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的智能体训练数据和基准大多假设信息完整、单轮的查询，但通过对16K真实会话的分析发现75.9%的交互是多轮的，用户的真实使用方式与现有训练和评估方式之间存在显著鸿沟。

Method: PersonaForge框架包含三个核心组件：四维角色空间（persona space）、基于真实用户统计校准的SOUL驱动行为控制、以及基于真实种子查询的反向深度构建（Reverse Deep Construction）。基于该框架构建了6.3K条训练数据集和138个任务的人工标注基准PersonaForge-Bench，覆盖20多个专业领域并采用四维评分。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-27B上，PersonaForge训练将综合得分提升4.1%，四个维度均有增益，其中任务完成（+6.0%）和响应质量（+6.8%）提升最大。训练后的智能体使用更少的轮次和工具调用，交互效率更高；消融实验验证了SOUL组件和自适应模拟的贡献。

Conclusion: PersonaForge和PersonaForge-Bench为在真实多轮用户交互场景下训练和评估智能体奠定了基础，弥补了现有训练数据和基准与真实使用模式之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agentic workflow executors, yet existing training data and benchmarks largely assume informationally complete, single-turn queries. Our analysis of 16K real-world sessions shows that 75.9% of interactions are multi-turn, revealing a substantial gap between how users interact with agents and how such systems are trained and evaluated. We introduce \textbf{PersonaForge}, a user simulation framework for synthesizing realistic multi-turn user--agent interactions. PersonaForge combines a four-dimensional persona space, SOUL-driven behavioral control calibrated to real-user statistics, and Reverse Deep Construction grounded in authentic seed queries. Using PersonaForge, we construct a 6.3K-record training dataset and \textbf{PersonaForge-Bench}, a manually annotated 138-task benchmark spanning over 20 professional domains with four-dimensional scoring. Experiments on Qwen3.5-27B show that PersonaForge training improves the composite score by +4.1%, with gains across all four dimensions and the largest improvements in Task Completion (+6.0%) and Response Quality (+6.8%). Further analyses show that PersonaForge-trained agents use fewer turns and tool calls, suggesting improved interaction efficiency, while ablations confirm the contribution of SOUL components and adaptive simulation. Together, PersonaForge and PersonaForge-Bench establish a foundation for training and evaluating agents under realistic multi-turn user interaction.

</details>


### [46] [When Linguistic and Internal Confidence Diverge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28382)
*Hefan Zhang,Bingquan Zhang,Ming Cheng,Saeed Hassanpour,Weicheng Ma,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了LLM的语言置信度（linguistic confidence）是否与其内部置信度（如logits和语义熵）一致。通过8个分类任务、2个生成任务和30个模型，从关联性、幅度一致性和校准三个维度评估，发现两者在多条轴上经常偏离，语言置信度总体关联弱、校准差，但更分散的置信度分布能携带更多排序信息。研究支持"有损信道"观点，建议用多轴诊断评估语言置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户经常要求LLM报告自信程度，但不清楚这种语言形式表达的置信度是否真正反映模型内部的置信度。如果语言置信度不可靠，将其用于下游可靠性管道可能存在风险，因此需要系统研究两者之间的一致性。

Method: 在8个分类任务和2个生成任务上评估30个模型（来自三个模型家族）。分类任务将语言置信度与基于logits的置信度在关联性、幅度一致性和校准三个维度比较；生成任务测试语言置信度是否追踪基于语义熵的不确定性。还通过prompt设计变化和回归分析（控制分布属性后检验模型元数据的作用）来剖析对齐模式。

Result: 实例级关联平均较弱，但在更容易的样本和更强的基座模型上有所改善。指令微调模型报告更高置信度且有时关联更强，但置信度差距更大、校准更差。Prompt设计主要改变置信度分布。态度线索会膨胀置信度而不改善对齐，分数示例在避免置信度值垮塌时可保留排序信号。回归分析显示置信度分数的分布属性解释了大部分对齐模式，模型元数据在控制后作用较小。

Conclusion: 语言置信度应被视为通过"有损信道"传达的信息——更分散的置信度分布能携带有用的排序信息，但并不会使其校准。因此，语言置信度在用于下游可靠性管道之前，必须用多轴诊断进行评估。

Abstract: Users often ask large language models (LLMs) to report how confident they are, but it is unclear whether such linguistic confidence tracks the model's internal confidence. We study this question across 8 classification tasks, 2 generation tasks and 30 models from three families. For classification, we compare linguistic confidence with logits-based confidence along three axes: association, magnitude agreement and calibration. For generation, we test whether linguistic confidence tracks semantic-entropy-based uncertainty. The axes frequently diverge. Instance-level association is weak on average, although it improves on easier items and for stronger base models. Instruction-tuned models often report higher confidence and sometimes show higher association, but they also have larger confidence gaps and worse calibration. Prompt design mostly changes the distribution of reported confidence. Attitude cues inflate confidence without improving alignment, while score exemplars can preserve rank-order signal when they avoid collapsed confidence values. Regression analyses show that distributional properties of confidence scores explain much of the observed alignment pattern, with model metadata playing a smaller role after controls. These results support a lossy-channel view of linguistic confidence. A more dispersed verbal confidence distribution can carry useful rank information, but it does not make the scores calibrated. Linguistic confidence should therefore be evaluated with multi-axis diagnostics before being used in downstream reliability pipelines.

</details>


### [47] [A Unified Framework to Elicit Structured Feedback for Interpretable Multi-Trait Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28407)
*Shihang Yang,Sanwoo Lee,Ningning Zhao,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HiFTS，一个统一的自回归框架，在预测特质级和整体评分之前生成分层思维链（CoT）反馈，通过教师LLM蒸馏rubric导向的分层CoT反馈，并采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）与复合奖励来平衡评分一致性、校准、反馈质量和结构有效性，同时引入CFMS-34中文多特质AES数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增强反馈的AES方法往往将反馈生成与评分解耦，或独立评估各特质，导致评分与反馈不一致、与评分标准（rubric）对齐不足。多特质AES需要基于评分标准进行跨相互依赖特质的推理，而非孤立的分数预测。

Method: 1) 构建HiFTS统一自回归框架，先生成分层CoT反馈，再预测特质级和整体分数；2) 从教师LLM蒸馏rubric导向的分层CoT反馈来训练学生模型联合生成反馈与评分；3) 应用GRPO，采用复合奖励同时优化评分一致性、校准、反馈质量和结构有效性；4) 推理时引入轻量全局先验作为整体引导，减少长形式推理中的漂移。

Result: 在CFMS-34和ASAP++数据集上的实验表明，HiFTS在整体和特质级评分上均表现强劲，同时能生成连贯、与rubric对齐的反馈。

Conclusion: 通过统一的反馈与评分生成框架、蒸馏与策略优化相结合的训练方法，HiFTS有效实现了多特质AES中评分准确性与反馈可解释性、rubric一致性之间的协同提升，并贡献了新的中文多特质AES基准数据集CFMS-34。

Abstract: Multi-trait Automated Essay Scoring (AES) requires rubric-grounded reasoning across interdependent traits, rather than isolated score prediction. Existing feedback-enhanced methods often decouple feedback from scoring or assess traits independently, weakening score--feedback consistency and rubric alignment. We propose HiFTS, a unified autoregressive framework that generates hierarchical CoT feedback before predicting trait-level and holistic scores. HiFTS distills rubric-grounded hierarchical CoT feedback from a teacher LLM and trains student models to jointly generate feedback and scores. HiFTS further applies Group Relative Policy Optimization with a composite reward balancing score agreement, calibration, feedback quality, and structural validity. At inference, a lightweight global prior provides holistic guidance to reduce drift during long-form reasoning. We also introduce CFMS-34, a Chinese multi-trait AES dataset with 951 essays annotated with holistic scores and 34 rubric-based traits. Experiments on CFMS-34 and ASAP++ show that HiFTS achieves strong holistic and trait-level scoring while producing coherent, rubric-aligned feedback.

</details>


### [48] [Are These Modules Worth Their Cost? A Paradigm-Level Accuracy-Cost Analysis of In-context Learning Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28432)
*Jiayan Lin,Yujia Liu,Zijin Hong,Zheng Yuan,Yilin Xiao,Hao Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Xiao Huang,Feiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在统一受控实现下，实例化了ICL text-to-SQL流水线中5个反复出现的模块的17种范式级配置，系统量化了每个范式在4种不同能力层级骨干模型上的边际准确率贡献与token成本，揭示了执行反馈细化是唯一成本低且普适有效的范式，并提出了可跨骨干迁移的成本感知分层指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL text-to-SQL研究大多围绕基础生成器堆叠复杂流水线以提升执行准确率，但通常仅报告端到端整体准确率，未对单个设计选择的边际准确率-成本贡献进行量化，导致缺乏统一、范式级别的成本-准确率量化框架，难以指导现代text-to-SQL系统的理解与配置。

Method: 作者在同一受控实现下，将ICL text-to-SQL流水线解构为5个反复出现的模块，实例化17种范式级配置；在4个覆盖不同能力层级与推理风格的骨干模型上，归因每个范式的边际贡献与成本消耗；并进行token核算与跨模块组合性分析，最终凝练出成本感知的分层指南。

Result: 执行反馈细化是唯一在所有骨干上以低成本稳定带来收益的范式；大多数其他模块仅在骨干相关的特定条件下有效。输入token需求与流水线结构关联更强，而输出token需求对骨干生成行为更敏感。跨模块分析显示堆叠在多数骨干上可提升准确率，但收益组合方式随骨干能力变化。在固定预算下，对中端骨干设计更精细流水线往往优于对前沿模型采用精简流水线。

Conclusion: 研究凝练出可操作的成本感知分层指南，无需逐范式搜索即可迁移至另外5个骨干模型，为构建与配置现代ICL text-to-SQL系统提供了范式级别的统一成本-准确率量化依据。

Abstract: Recent advances in in-context learning (ICL) text-to-SQL have substantially improved execution accuracy on public benchmarks by assembling increasingly elaborate pipelines around the base generator, yet existing studies typically report aggregate end-to-end accuracy, without quantifying the marginal accuracy-cost contribution of individual design choices. Consequently, providing a unified, paradigm-level cost-accuracy quantification remains a critical challenge for understanding and configuring modern text-to-SQL. To address this, we instantiate 17 paradigm-level configurations across five recurring modules of the ICL text-to-SQL pipeline under a single controlled implementation, and attribute each paradigm's marginal contribution and incurred cost across all four backbones spanning diverse capability levels and reasoning styles. Our analysis reveals that execution-feedback refinement is the only paradigm whose benefit holds universally at consistently low cost, while most other modules help only under backbone-dependent conditions. Token accounting shows that input demand is more closely tied to pipeline structure, whereas output demand is more sensitive to backbone generation behavior. Cross-module analysis further shows that stacking improves accuracy on most backbones, although how the gains compose varies with backbone capability. We also find that a fixed budget is often better spent engineering a more elaborate pipeline over a mid-tier backbone than upgrading to a frontier model with a lean pipeline. These findings distill into an actionable, cost-aware tiered guideline that transfers to five additional backbones without per-paradigm search.

</details>


### [49] [Fidelity Is Not Enough: Dispatch-Level Instrumentation for Agentic Datasheet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28439)
*Qing Ye,Meng-Hsuan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体文档提取中的"静默失败"问题：一个模型因结构化输出约束禁用了工具调用，却仍生成伪造文本且通过保真度检查。作者通过25+12=37条人工标注声明、记录每次工具调用来构建基准，并开发了规则式故障归因分类器和静默失败检测器（仅检查工具调用、不检查提取值）。检测器在207次干净提取上无误报，并恢复全部50个植入故障。作者还引入"因果室"作为独立oracle，对物理可测的2/37条声明进行验证，发现在测量不确定性边界处保真度通过但因果室判定翻转。工具层带来可移植性和可观测性而非准确性，仅在文档超出上下文窗口时才体现价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保真度（fidelity）指标无法捕捉智能体文档提取中的静默失败——模型在工具被禁用时仍能"成功"回答并生成伪造文本。作者发现仅凭最终输出值无法判断提取是否真实，需要记录工具调用轨迹来暴露此类故障。

Method: 1) 构建包含37条人工挑选声明的智能体基准，记录每次工具调用；2) 开发规则式故障归因分类器；3) 构建静默失败检测器（仅检查工具调用模式，不检查提取值）；4) 引入"因果室"作为独立oracle，对物理可测量的声明进行验证；5) 在受控扰动下对比保真度与因果室判定。

Result: 检测器在207次保真度通过的干净提取上无误报（界定假阳性率），并恢复全部50个故意植入的、恰好扣留其规则检查工具的故障（召回率由构造保证）。但对"调用工具仍答错"的情况检测能力未测量。因果室仅能验证37条中2条声明（其余不可物理分级并有分类学解释），在测量不确定性处保真度通过而因果室判定翻转。三个模型栈中工具层带来可移植性和可观测性而非准确性，仅在文档超出上下文窗口时有价值。

Conclusion: 保真度作为标准指标不足以评估智能体化文档提取；工具调用轨迹暴露静默失败。静默失败检测器（基于工具调用模式而非输出值）有界假阳性率、构造性召回。因果室提供部分但独立的验证，在测量不确定边界可见保真度与物理验证的分歧。工具层的真正价值是可移植性和可观测性，而非准确性，其溢价仅在上下文超限时成立。两个oracle的结果不对称，检测对"调用工具但仍答错"的情况无能为力。

Abstract: One model passed our fidelity check without ever opening the datasheet. We found it while qualifying models for an internal extraction service: a structured-output constraint had silently disabled tool use, and the model answered anyway, with fabricated source text. Only the per-tool trace exposed it. Fidelity -- whether an extracted value matches the source -- is the standard measure for agentic document extraction, and it scores that run a success. We therefore log every tool call in an agentic benchmark of 25 hand-curated claims over three components, with 12 more on a fourth, 37 in all. From that dispatch record we build two instruments: a rule-based failure-attribution classifier, and a silent-failure detector whose two rules check only which tools were called, never the extracted value. The detector raises no flag on 207 clean fidelity-passing extractions across three model families, and recovers all 50 planted faults that withhold exactly the tools its rules check. The two results are not symmetric: the first bounds the false-positive rate, the second is recall by construction, and detection power against runs that call their tools and still answer wrongly is unmeasured. A second, independent oracle, a causal chamber that tests whether the datasheet's claims hold under physical measurement, is intentionally partial: it confirms only what the apparatus can exercise, a verifiable envelope of 2 of those 37 claims, and we give a taxonomy of why the rest are not physically gradable. Under a controlled perturbation, fidelity passes throughout while the chamber verdict flips exactly at the measurement uncertainty. Across three deployed model stacks (one destabilised by its serving stack, not by any capability gap) the tool layer buys portability and observability rather than accuracy, and earns its premium only once a document outgrows the context window.

</details>


### [50] [Sliding-window beats linear attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28444)
*Alexia Jolicoeur-Martineau,Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker,Pashmina Cameron,Emy Gervais*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，尽管线性注意力被广泛视为解决LLM二次方注意力缩放问题的有前景方案，但该方向的研究缺乏与更简单基线的公平比较。作者证明，带sink token的滑动窗口注意力（SWA）在多个LLM和下游任务上表现与后训练的线性注意力模型相当甚至更好，尤其在长上下文推理任务上性能高出2到10倍，且无需后训练、速度快、内存低，是更经济可靠的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的二次方注意力导致内存和能耗随上下文长度无限增长，不可持续。线性注意力被提出作为解决方案，但其研究未与简单基线进行恰当对比，导致对线性注意力实际有效性的认知可能存在偏差。

Method: 作者将带sink的滑动窗口注意力（SWA）与后训练的线性注意力模型在多个LLM和多种下游任务上进行系统对比，包括长上下文推理任务（Needle-in-a-Haystack和BABILong）和其他任务。

Result: SWA在多个LLM和下游任务上表现与线性注意力相当或更好；在长上下文推理任务中，SWA性能比线性注意力高2到10倍。SWA无需后训练、速度极快、内存需求低。

Conclusion: 作者强烈建议改用SWA而非后训练线性模型来降低推理内存成本。线性注意力模型若要匹配SWA，可能需要从头训练或大量后训练，其优势并未得到充分验证。

Abstract: Due to the nature of quadratic attention, Large Language Models (LLMs) consume a lot of memory and energy. Every new token costs more than the previous one. For each additional token, the keys and values must be stored in memory indefinitely, which is unsustainable.
  Several alternatives have been proposed to fix the quadratic scaling problem, one of which is retrofitting LLMs to use Linear Attention. This idea has attracted a lot of attention, given its promise to solve the quadratic scaling problem with state-of-the-art performance at low cost. However, this line of research has not been properly compared to simpler baselines.
  In this work, we show that Sliding Window Attention (SWA) with sinks performs as well or better than post-trained Linear Attention models. We observe this across multiple LLMs on various downstream tasks. For long-context reasoning tasks (Needle-in-a-Haystack and BABILong), SWA achieves massively higher performance (2 to 10 times higher than linear attention). SWA requires no post-training, is extremely fast, and requires low memory; therefore, making it an extremely cheap and reliable solution.
  To reduce inference memory cost, we strongly recommend switching to SWA instead of post-training linear models. Linear attention models may have shown some promise, but they likely require to be trained from scratch or extensive post-training in order to even match SWA.

</details>


### [51] [Acquire, Repair, Preserve: A Diagnosis-Guided Post-Training Recipe for Small-Model Dialogue Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28458)
*Nan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究交互式对话游戏中的模型能力问题，使用2B开源模型参与LM Playschool Challenge，发现失败不仅源于知识不足，更多是局部决策问题（重复猜测、格式错误、违反即时反馈）。提出三步训练方案：SFT获取广泛游戏参与能力、针对单一对话游戏家族用turn-local偏好对修复机械可验证失败、保持通用能力。官方评估中clemscore从10.67提升至38.92，域内从13.41升至41.17，静态性能基本保持（44.14 vs 44.24），但域外仅7.88，迁移主要局限在目标家族内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准无法充分评估模型在交互式对话游戏中的能力——这种场景要求模型跨轮次保持状态、解读反馈、在动态约束下选择有效动作。作者发现现有模型在此类场景中的失败不仅是知识性问题，更是局部决策失败，因此需要针对性的诊断和训练方法。

Method: 提出由三步组成的训练方案：(1) 通过监督微调（SFT）获取广泛的游戏参与能力；(2) 在单一目标对话游戏家族内，利用turn-local（回合局部）的偏好对来修复机械上可验证的失败（如重复猜测、格式错误、违反即时反馈）；(3) 保持超越对话游戏之外的通用能力。

Result: 官方最终评估中，public clemscore从10.67提升到38.92，closed in-domain score从13.41提升到41.17，而聚合静态性能基本保持不变（44.14 vs 44.24基线）。域外clemscore仍较低（7.88），最大增益集中在目标家族未见变体上。

Conclusion: 广域SFT带来了模型能力提升的主要部分；当失败检测足够精确时，turn-local监督是有效的，但观察到的迁移主要集中在家族内部，跨域泛化仍然有限。这为交互式游戏场景下的训练策略提供了实证参考。

Abstract: Interactive dialogue games test a capability that static benchmarks largely leave implicit: a model must carry state across turns, interpret feedback, and choose valid actions under changing constraints. We study this setting in the LM Playschool Challenge with a 2B open-weight model, and find that many failures are not only broad knowledge failures but also local decision failures: repeated guesses, malformed actions, and violations of feedback that the model has just seen. These diagnostics motivate a training recipe organized around three steps: acquire broad game participation through supervised fine-tuning, repair mechanically verifiable failures within one targeted dialogue-game family using turn-local preference pairs, and preserve general capabilities beyond these dialogue games. In the official final evaluation, our submission improves public clemscore from 10.67 to 38.92 and closed in-domain score from 13.41 to 41.17, while approximately preserving aggregate static performance (44.14 vs. 44.24 for the baseline). Out-of-domain clemscore remains low at 7.88, with the largest gains concentrated in unseen variants of the targeted family. Our results suggest that broad SFT brings most of the model's capability improvement; turn-local supervision can be effective when failure detection is precise, with observed transfer concentrated primarily within-family.

</details>


### [52] [Stranger, Fan, or Peer? A Systematic Study on the Role of Interlocutor in Persona-Based Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28467)
*Daniela Occhipinti,Malvina Nissim,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究基于人物的对话系统中，说话人传记在训练、推理和评估三个阶段的可见性分别设置对模型行为的影响。研究发现训练阶段的可见性比推理阶段更能决定模型是否通过对话表达人物特质还是直接复制传记文本；交错者可见性配置会影响目标传记内容的泄露情况，且这种泄露使对话更容易被识别出作者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作通常将传记可见性视为单一因素，忽略了训练、推理和评估三个阶段的独立变化。由于对话涉及至少两个参与者，谁能看到谁的传记可能在不同阶段有所不同，这种差异可能导致模型行为机制的混淆，需要系统研究这一三阶段因子分解。

Method: 在带说话人传记的对话数据集上，分别独立变化目标和交错者说话人在训练及推理阶段是否看到对方传记，并使用LLM作为评审进行作者识别任务。

Result: (i)训练阶段可见性比推理阶段更能决定模型是否表达人物特质或复制传记文本；(ii)训练时提供交错者传记可见性会减少目标传记文本的复制，而仅推理时改变可见性效果不一致；(iii)在非对称披露（只有交错者看到目标传记）下，目标内容更常泄露到交错者对话轮次中，且包含此类痕迹的对话更容易被评审识别。

Conclusion: 传记泄露到生成对话是一个受交错者可见性在训练和推理阶段配置影响的伪影现象，分离三个阶段进行研究是必要的。

Abstract: Persona-based dialogue systems are usually conditioned on speaker biography, but dialogues involve at least two participants, and who has access to whose biography can vary across training, inference, and evaluation. Prior work often neglected these aspects, obscuring mechanisms that only appear when biography visibility is toggled separately across training, inference, and evaluation, a three-stage factorisation that prior work has largely treated as a single factor. We study this factorisation on a dataset of dialogues paired with speaker's biographies, varying whether the target and interlocutor speakers see each other's biographies during training and inference, and using an LLM as a judge to perform author identification. We find that (i) training-time visibility, more than inference-time visibility, determines whether models express persona traits through dialogue or fall back on copying biographical text (a known problem/phenomenon in persona-based generation); (ii) models trained with interlocutor-biography visibility copy less target-biographical text than models trained without it, while changing visibility only at inference time has a less consistent effect; and (iii) under asymmetric disclosure, where only the interlocutor sees the target biography, target content leaks into interlocutor turns more often, and dialogues containing such traces are easier for the judge to identify, especially when interlocutor turns are visible. These results suggest that biography leakage into generated turns is an artefact of how interlocutor visibility is configured across training and inference, and separating the three stages is necessary.

</details>


### [53] [ContextPilot: Teaching Agents for Proactive Context Management via Fine-grained RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28476)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Qizhi Pei,Junru Lu,Honglin Lin,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextPilot是一个面向长时程智能体任务的前瞻性上下文管理框架，通过扩充工具集（加入规划、长期记忆和软上下文卸载工具）并设计专门针对上下文管理的强化学习方法，利用上下文和熵变化识别关键编辑决策进行分支采样，从所有经过该编辑动作的分支轨迹估计动作级优势值，从而在更紧凑的工作上下文中取得更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体任务要求LLM在多轮交互中迭代检索、整合和维护分散信息，但保留全部交互历史会导致工作上下文持续膨胀。现有前瞻性上下文管理方法存在三个关键局限：工具集受限（仅支持搜索、删除和摘要，缺乏全局规划、长期记忆和自适应压缩）、探索效率低下（对异构影响的上下文管理动作一视同仁）、以及粗粒度信用分配（RL中用最终轨迹级奖励分配给所有中间上下文编辑动作）。

Method: ContextPilot系统地扩充工具集，加入规划、长期记忆和软上下文卸载工具；提出专门针对上下文管理的RL方法，利用上下文和熵变化识别关键编辑决策用于分支采样，并从所有经过对应上下文编辑动作的分支轨迹估计动作级优势值。

Result: 在长上下文问答和深度搜索任务上的实验表明，ContextPilot在更紧凑的工作上下文下取得更强性能，在多种基础模型和基准上一致优于现有基线。

Conclusion: ContextPilot通过系统化的工具集扩充和针对上下文管理定制的RL方法，有效解决了长时程智能体任务中工作上下文膨胀的问题，实现了更高效的上下文管理和更优的任务性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks require large language models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve, integrate, and maintain dispersed information across multi-turn interactions, but preserving all interaction histories leads to a continuously growing working context. Recent proactive context management methods allow models to edit their own working context with specialized tools, yet they still face three key limitations: (1) a limited toolset restricted to search, deletion, and summarization, with no support for global planning, long-term memory, and adaptive compression; (2) inefficient exploration that treats context management actions uniformly despite their heterogeneous impacts on final outcomes; and (3) coarse-grained credit assignment that assigns the final trajectory-level reward to all intermediate context editing actions during RL. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ContextPilot, a proactive context management framework for long-horizon agentic reasoning. Our approach systematically augments the toolset with planning, long-term memory, and soft context offloading tools. We further propose an RL method tailored for context management, which uses context and entropy variation to identify critical editing decisions for branch sampling and estimates action-level advantages from all branched trajectories that pass through the corresponding context editing action. Experiments on long-context QA and deep search tasks show that ContextPilot achieves stronger performance with a more compact working context, consistently outperforming existing baselines across various base models and benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ContextPilot.

</details>


### [54] [Blind Men and the Elephant: Probing the Epistemic Myopia of LLMs under Long-Tail Divergent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28478)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Junru Lu,Yan Qian,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ElephantBench是一个用于评估LLM在长尾事实多账户知识记忆能力的基准测试,包含1,094个通过可审计图谱管道生成的问题。测试发现即使最强模型也仅能在52.4%的问题上同时回忆两个账户,其余问题大多只能回忆一个账户,揭示了模型参数记忆中的"认识论近视"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事实问答假设存在单一标准答案,难以反映LLM是否保留对长尾事实的多重分歧叙述。现有评估方法忽视了模型在记忆层面的"认识论完整性",即能否同时保留同一事实的不同版本/账户。

Method: 提出基于图谱的管道:①从低曝光网络语料库检索相关文档;②识别文档中自然存在的分歧/矛盾账户;③将其转换为多账户QA记录;④每项答案经原始文档和权威公共网络来源验证,并由人工标注者审核。最终构建包含1,094个问题的ElephantBench基准。

Result: 在32个模型上测试:最强模型也仅能在52.4%的问题上同时恢复两个账户;几乎所有剩余问题上模型只回忆一个账户而遗漏另一个。增大模型规模和推理时推理可改善召回率但无法消除不完整性。语料分析表明曝光不平衡偏向主导账户,而少数方曝光度更高则与更完整的召回相关。

Conclusion: ElephantBench可作为诊断参数记忆中"认识论近视"的可复现知识探针。其基于图谱的基准构建管道为将长尾语料转化为可溯源知识探针提供了高效可扩展的方法,支持评估和提升下一代LLM的认识论严谨性。

Abstract: Factual question answering (QA) typically assumes a single canonical answer, obscuring whether large language models (LLMs) retain divergent accounts of long-tail facts. To address this gap, we introduce ElephantBench, a closed-book knowledge probe comprising 1,094 questions generated through an auditable graph-based pipeline. The pipeline retrieves related documents from a low-exposure web corpus, identifies naturally occurring disagreements, and converts them into multi-account QA records. Each answer is verified against the originating documents and authoritative public web sources and is then reviewed by human annotators. Across 32 models, even the strongest model recovers both accounts on only 52.4% of questions, while on nearly all remaining questions it recalls one account but omits the other. Scaling model size and inference-time reasoning improve recall but do not eliminate this incompleteness. Corpus analysis further shows that exposure imbalance favors the dominant account, whereas greater minority-side exposure is associated with more complete recall. These findings establish ElephantBench as a reproducible knowledge probe for diagnosing epistemic myopia in parametric memory. More broadly, our graph-based benchmark construction pipeline provides an efficient and scalable way to turn long-tail corpora into source-traceable knowledge probes, supporting efforts to evaluate and advance the epistemic rigour of next-generation LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ElephantBench.

</details>


### [55] [NL2AGBench: Benchmarking LLM Auto-Formalization for AlphaGeometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28481)
*Samuel Xiao,Judy Song,Rory Hu,Ziliang Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍NL2AGBench基准，用于评估大语言模型将自然语言几何问题翻译成AlphaGeometry形式化表示的（DSL）能力，使用执行验证而非文本相似度评估翻译质量，并测试了十个开源与闭源LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaGeometry在定理证明上表现接近IMO金牌水平，但其输入需要专门的DSL形式化表示，人工将自然语言问题转换为形式语法是重大的可用性瓶颈。LLM在这方面的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建NL2AGBench基准，采用基于AlphaGeometry执行验证的方式评估翻译质量；评估十个不同参数规模的开源与闭源LLM，分析可执行翻译准确率、语法正确性和错误特征；提出区分语法错误与逻辑错误的误差分类体系；探索少样本提示、微调和人指导提示等缓解策略。

Result: 闭源与开源模型之间存在显著性能差距：领先闭源模型的可执行翻译率超过80%，而最大的开源模型也难以一致地保持几何约束并生成有效形式化表示。

Conclusion: 提出的缓解策略（少样本提示、微调、人指导提示）在多个模型家族上取得了可测量的改进，为神经符号几何系统的形式化翻译问题提供了有价值的基准和解决方案方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in natural language understanding and mathematical reasoning. However, their ability to translate informal mathematical problems into formal representations remains underexplored. This limitation is particularly important for neuro-symbolic geometry systems such as AlphaGeometry, whose theorem-proving engine requires inputs in a specialized domain-specific language (DSL). Although AlphaGeometry achieves near-IMO gold-medalist performance, manually converting natural-language problems into its formal syntax remains a significant usability bottleneck. To address this challenge, we introduce the Natural Language to AlphaGeometry Benchmark (NL2AGBench), which evaluates LLMs in translating English geometry problems into AlphaGeometry-compatible formal representations. NL2AGBench uses execution-based verification within AlphaGeometry to assess translation quality rather than relying solely on textual similarity. We evaluate ten state-of-the-art open- and closed-source LLMs across multiple parameter scales and analyze executable translation accuracy, syntactic correctness, and error characteristics. Our experiments reveal a substantial performance gap between closed- and open-source models: leading closed-source models achieve executable translation rates above 80%, while even the largest open-source models struggle to consistently preserve geometric constraints and produce valid formalizations. We introduce an error taxonomy distinguishing syntax and logic errors and investigate mitigation strategies, including few-shot prompting, fine-tuning, and human-guided hinting, which yield measurable improvements across multiple model families.

</details>


### [56] [Ladders in Chaos: When, How, (and Perhaps Why) Does Test-Time Scaling Improve LLM Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28496)
*Di Wu,Sergey Troshin,Christof Monz,Antske Fokkens,Vlad Niculae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）在机器翻译任务中的两种测试时扩展（test-time scaling）范式：顺序式（sequential）和并行式（parallel，如i.i.d.采样加重排序）。研究发现顺序采样具有更高的性能上限，尤其在较小采样预算下能产生更多样且有效的样本；人工分析表明顺序采样显著提升翻译流畅性和自然度，但在推理预算较大时可能损害准确性；机制分析将顺序自改进的成功部分归因于模型获得更大的目标端上下文。消融实验显示顺序采样在不同采样温度下表现稳健，但对上下文构建方式敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展已成为提升LLM性能的重要范式，但顺序式和并行式两种扩展方式在机器翻译任务中的特性和机理尚不清楚，需要系统研究它们各自的优劣、适用场景以及背后的作用机制。

Method: 通过对比顺序采样和i.i.d.并行采样（Best-of-N重排序）在翻译任务上的表现；采用多维人工分析评估翻译质量（流畅性、自然度、准确性）；通过控制变量和消融实验（不同采样温度、上下文构建方式）揭示顺序扩展的提升机制。

Result: 顺序采样性能上限更高，在小采样预算下样本更多样有效；顺序采样显著改善流畅性和自然度但大预算下可能降低准确性；顺序扩展的成功部分归因于更大的目标端上下文；顺序采样对温度稳健但敏感于上下文构建。

Conclusion: 顺序式测试时扩展在机器翻译中总体优于并行式，但其提升机制主要来自目标端上下文的扩大而非单纯的采样策略本身；未来改进应聚焦于更好的上下文构建方式，以在保持流畅性优势的同时避免大预算下的准确性下降。

Abstract: Two forms of test-time scaling for Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as effective and widely adopted paradigms: sequential, in which later answer attempts depend on earlier ones, and parallel, such as i.i.d. sampling with reranking. In this study, we investigate their properties in translation. First, our study shows that sequential sampling has a higher performance ceiling, providing a more diverse and effective pool of samples, particularly under smaller sampling budgets. Second, we interrogate the nature of test-time scaling through a multidimensional manual analysis. Human analysis of the Best-of-$N$ translations demonstrates that sequential sampling substantially improves translation fluency and naturalness, but can degrade accuracy when inference budgets are large. Finally, we suggest an explanation of the mechanism through which sequential scaling improves machine translation. Our controlled analysis partially attributes the success of sequential self-improvement to the model's access to a larger target-side context. Ablation experiments on sequential sampling demonstrate its robustness across different sampling temperatures, while also revealing sensitivity to context construction, suggesting directions for future improvement.

</details>


### [57] [Phoneme- and Word-Level Metrics Using Self-Supervised Speech Representations for Forced Alignment Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28508)
*V. S. D. S. Mahesh Akavarapu,Michael Daniel,Gerhard Jäger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于自监督语音表征的无参考强制对齐评估指标（PCMI和WACS），无需人工标注时间戳即可评估对齐质量，并在85种语言的FLEURS和45种语言的DoReCo数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强制对齐评估依赖人工标注的时间戳，这限制了大规模和多语言的评估分析。需要一种可扩展、无参考的评估方法来克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出两个语料级指标：(1) 音素-聚类互信息（PCMI），衡量对齐音素标签与SSL语音表征诱导的聚类之间的一致性；(2) 词声学一致性分数（WACS），通过动态时间规整（DTW）计算重复词实现之间的表征序列相似度。采用随机和系统性扰动验证指标对扰动的敏感性。

Result: PCMI和WACS在扰动下持续下降；在85种语言的FLEURS和45种语言的DoReCo上验证，能有效区分高低质量对齐，并与基于时间戳的对齐质量指标强相关；在两种音系复杂的低资源语言上也表现良好。

Conclusion: SSL语音表征能够实现可扩展、无参考的强制对齐评估，所提出的指标已开源为Python包。

Abstract: Forced alignment evaluation typically requires manually annotated timestamps, limiting large-scale and multilingual analysis. We introduce two corpus-level metrics based on self-supervised (SSL) speech representations for reference-free forced alignment evaluation: Phoneme-Cluster Mutual Information (PCMI) and Word Acoustic Consistency Score (WACS). PCMI measures agreement between aligned phoneme labels and clusters induced from SSL-speech representations, while WACS measures consistency of repeated word realizations using dynamic time warping similarity between word representation sequences. Using both random and systematic perturbations, we show that PCMI and WACS degrade consistently under alignment perturbations. We further analyze the metrics across multiple alignment systems on 85 languages from FLEURS, validate them against manually annotated alignments from 45 languages in DoReCo, and evaluate them on two phonologically complex low-resource languages. The metrics effectively separate high- and low-quality alignments and correlate strongly with timestamp-based alignment quality measures. Our results demonstrate that SSL-speech representations enable scalable, reference-free forced alignment evaluation. The metrics are available as an open-source Python package at https://github.com/mahesh-ak/forced-aligner-metrics.

</details>


### [58] [A Formal Limitation on Learning Human Language From Textual Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28560)
*Emily Cheng,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度回答了一个核心问题：听话者能否仅凭话语的形式（如LLM的隐状态表示）恢复说话者的意图。作者推导出解码器恢复说话者意图概率的上界，该上界由话语形式对意义的不确定性决定，且这种不确定性是不可约的——任何表示方法（无论训练数据多丰富）都无法超越。实验在人工语言、汉语零形回指消解和颜色指称三个任务上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究语言理解的理论极限：一个听话者仅凭话语形式能否恢复说话者意图？这一问题在当代大语言模型盛行的背景下尤为重要，因为LLM的隐状态被认为是文本的"表示"。作者希望通过信息论给出一个理论框架，界定任何表示方法（包括LLM）在从形式恢复意义这一任务上的根本性上限。

Method: 将语言使用建模为意义、语境和话语的联合分布。推导解码器从话语表示中恢复说话者意图概率的上界。该上界由形式对意义的不确定性控制，此不确定性分解为不可约部分（语言本身固有的）和只能由额外语言外语境（而非话语本身）解决的部分。这些量是语言的内在属性，因此对任何表示方法（离散或连续的意义空间）都成立。实验验证包括人工语言、汉语零形回指消解和颜色参考三个场景。

Result: 理论推导得出：任何基于话语形式的解码器恢复说话者意图的概率存在上界，该上界由形式-意义间的固有不确定性决定。在人工语言、汉语零形回指消解（需要语境恢复省略的主语）和颜色指称三个实验中都获得了支持理论的证据。

Conclusion: 结论是：仅凭话语形式无法完全恢复说话者意图，存在信息论意义上的不可逾越的界限。语言本身在设计层面就存在意义歧义，任何表示方法（包括LLM的隐状态）都无法完全消除这种不确定性，必须依赖额外语言外语境。这为理解LLM的能力边界提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Can a listener recover what a speaker means from the form of an utterance alone? We answer this question information-theoretically, and for a listener given by any featurizer of text, including the hidden states of contemporary large language models. Modeling language use as a joint distribution over meanings, contexts, and utterances, we derive upper bounds on the probability that a decoder recovers a speaker's intended meaning from a representation of the utterance. The bounds are governed by the uncertainty that form leaves about meaning, which splits into an irreducible part and a part that only (extralinguistic) context, but never the utterance alone, can resolve. Because these quantities are intrinsic to language, no representation, however much text or supervision produced it, can surpass them; the bounds hold whether the space of meanings is discrete or continuous. Experiments on artificial languages, Mandarin zero-pronoun resolution, and color reference provide empirical evidence in support of the theory.

</details>

### [59] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCC-PGPSE方法，通过leave-one-policy-out覆盖和状态归属专业化计算每个策略的边际覆盖信用，在不改变总奖励质量的前提下重新分配非负辅助内在奖励，以抑制冗余访问、促进互补覆盖。实验在受控环境、7个公开离散状态基准和原始Room/Maze设定中均获得正面的归一化团队状态熵和状态支持提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE虽然通过多个独立参数化策略扩展状态空间覆盖，但其池化团队熵指标只能度量集体探索，无法识别哪些策略贡献了非冗余的覆盖。缺乏对个体策略边际贡献的评估，导致策略可能重复访问相同状态，探索效率低下。

Method: MCC-PGPSE结合leave-one-policy-out覆盖与状态归属专业化来估计策略专属信用，保留PGPSE的池化目标不变，仅将非负辅助内在奖励按信用分数重新分配，且不改变奖励总量。这种再分配机制旨在抑制冗余访问偏好，促进策略间的互补性覆盖。

Result: 在所有测试设置中，MCC-PGPSE相比Entropy基线在归一化团队状态熵和状态支持上均取得正的最终窗口增益。受控任务对比和固定公开测试套件聚合结果显著，5种子原始协议对比方向一致。消融实验和信用对齐控制表明增益主要来自leave-one-policy-out覆盖而非非均匀加权、信用错配或神经新颖性。

Conclusion: 结果支持将贡献条件化的辅助奖励分配作为在离散状态空间中提升并行策略互补覆盖的可解释方法，为多策略探索提供了细粒度的信用分配机制。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [60] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了后训练量化并非语义中立的过程，形式化了"验证-部署鸿沟"（量化是多对一映射，源精度认证无法保证部署行为等价），并提出基于量化行为等价类（QBECs）的量化触发后门攻击框架，在机器翻译和立场分析场景中证明源精度审计无法排除量化触发行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流中，模型在源精度（全精度）下评估，量化作为下游步骤被应用于边缘部署，但未进行等价重评估。由于量化在参数空间上是多对一映射，源精度认证并不保证部署配置下的行为等价，这一结构性鸿沟可能被恶意利用来实施后门攻击。

Method: 形式化定义量化行为等价类（QBECs）并证明其成员关系不蕴含行为等价性；构建三阶段对抗微调框架，在满足源精度检查的同时嵌入潜在恶意载荷，使模型在INT8或4-bit压缩后才激活目标对抗行为；将威胁评估从解码器-仅因果LM扩展到多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型，并开展跨量化器可迁移性分析。

Result: 带后门的翻译模型从修复FP16下的零友敌腐化到量化后最高85.02%的逆转；配对立场分类器在压缩后表现出最高ΔBias=0.33的意识形态偏移；跨量化器分析显示攻击持久性因量化方案和模型架构而异，而非仅由标称位宽决定。

Conclusion: 源精度审计本身无法排除量化触发的对抗行为，最终部署配置必须纳入行为认证中，才能实现可信的边缘AI。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [61] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究基于 Gated Delta Net (GDN) 和 Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) 语言模型中循环状态的后训练量化问题，提出 DAMP 方法。该方法基于量化误差能量和衰减持久性识别高风险通道，对高风险通道保留高精度、其余通道用 INT8 存储，在 9.9 bits/状态值的代价下接近 FP32 基线精度，同时将循环状态存储降低 69.1%、更新内核加速最高 2.01 倍、全模型 TPOT 降低 10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN/KDA 等模型用固定大小的循环状态替代大部分层的 KV cache 以减少内存增长，但循环状态以 FP32 存储，占用大量 GPU 内存且更新受内存带宽限制，显著拖慢解码延迟。现有研究表明均匀量化（INT8/FP8/INT4/NVFP4）在这些状态上精度退化严重，因此需要更好的量化方案。

Method: DAMP 方法基于两个关键发现：(1) 大部分量化误差能量集中在少数通道子集中；(2) 状态通道的相对衰减强度在提示和任务间保持稳定。DAMP 在离线校准阶段同时利用量化误差能量和基于衰减的持久性来识别高风险通道，将这些通道以更高精度存储，其余通道用 INT8 存储。

Result: 在 Qwen3.6-35B 和 Kimi-Linear-48B 上、覆盖数学推理、通用推理和代码生成的六个基准测试中，DAMP 在 9.9 bits/状态值的存储代价下保持接近 FP32 基线的平均准确率，循环状态存储降低 69.1%，循环状态更新内核加速最高 2.01 倍，全模型 TPOT 降低 10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP 证明了 GDN/KDA 模型的循环状态可以通过混合精度后训练量化实现显著的存储和延迟优化，同时保持精度；量化误差能量与衰减持久性相结合是识别关键通道的有效准则。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [62] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究块稀疏特征化器（BSF）的优缺点，发现它仍存在特征分裂和组合等经典SAE失败模式，并提出包括Tournament Top-K选择规则在内的多种架构改进，同时将块范式扩展到交叉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSF作为一种类似稀疏自编码器的新型特征化工具，以子空间（块）而非单个方向为原子单元，专为视觉中常见的低维流形特征设计。但作者发现BSF仍受传统SAE失败模式（如特征分裂和组合）困扰，需要系统性研究并改进。

Method: 作者对BSF进行深入分析，识别其优势和不足；提出多种架构改进，其中核心创新是Tournament Top-K选择规则以减少特征分裂；此外将块范式扩展到crosscoder架构。

Result: Tournament Top-K选择规则显著减少了特征分裂现象，同时块范式成功扩展到crosscoder，为低维流形特征的跨编码提供了新方案。

Conclusion: 通过架构改进，BSF在低维流形特征表示上的能力得到增强，克服了部分经典SAE失败模式，并为未来的特征化研究开辟了块范式+交叉编码器的新方向。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [63] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了持续学习（CL）和模型合并（MM）中看似独立的两个挑战——灾难性遗忘和权重解纠缠误差——实际上是同一现象"任务干扰"的两种表现。作者将其形式化为层间Frobenius内积，并推导出上界，证明优化器的谱范数控制是调节该干扰的关键因素。研究发现Muon优化器通过构造性控制谱范数，能同时改善CL和MM的性能，在多个基准上超越AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CL和MM长期以来被视为两个独立问题，分别用不同方法缓解。然而两者面临的本质困难——参数更新对某任务有利却损害另一任务——可能共享相同根源。此外，基础优化器诱导的几何特性一直被当作实现细节而忽视。作者希望统一这两个领域，并揭示优化器在其中的核心作用。

Method: 1) 形式化"任务干扰"为层间Frobenius内积⟨ΔWℓ, Jℓ(x)⟩F；2) 理论上推导出上界，隔离出谱范数‖ΔWℓ‖2作为优化器可控制的干扰因子；3) 通过逐模式（per-mode）分析验证该上界捕捉经验干扰的主导部分；4) 识别Muon优化器通过构造性机制控制谱范数，从而收紧干扰上界。

Result: 将AdamW替换为Muon后，在8任务模型合并基准（3个CLIP骨干）上准确率提升最高达+5.02点；在10个类增量协议、3个任务增量协议和11任务MTIL基准上均获得一致的正面增益。

Conclusion: 任务干扰统一了CL和MM两个领域的问题本质，优化器的谱范数控制是缓解干扰的关键杠杆。Muon作为优化器中心的方法，与现有解决方案互补，为CL和MM提供了原则性的新途径。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error. In the literature, these difficulties are merely treated separately and mitigated through a variety of solutions, while the geometry induced by the base optimizer is treated as an implementation detail. In this work, we show that the two difficulties are in fact two instances of the same phenomenon: a parameter update useful for one task shifts the model's outputs on another. We formalize this shared phenomenon as \textit{task interference} and reduce it to a common layer-wise Frobenius inner product $\langle ΔW_\ell, J_\ell(x)\rangle_F$. This quantity, in turn, is utilized to expose the role of the optimizer. We theoretically derive an upper bound that isolates the spectral norm $\|ΔW_\ell\|_2$ as an optimizer-controllable factor of task interference, and a per-mode analysis shows that this bound tracks the dominant part of the empirical interference. Specifically, we then identify the recent Muon optimizer as a mechanism that regulates this factor by construction. Our work reveals that its elegant control on spectral norm tightens the interference bound for both CL and MM, positioning Muon as a principled optimizer-centric approach complementary to existing solutions. Our theoretcal analysis is well validated by experimental results. Replacing the AdamW optimizer with Muon improves accuracy by up to +5.02 points on the eight-task model-merging benchmark across three CLIP backbones. For continual learning, Muon also delivers uniformly positive gains across ten class-incremental protocols, three task-incremental protocols, and the 11-task MTIL benchmark.

</details>


### [64] [Dandelion: A Spherical Flower for Neural Simulation of Planetary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27521)
*Till Muser,Giovanni Abati,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dandelion，一种球形版本的warp-based神经PDE求解器（源于Flower架构），完全在球谐域中实现分层池化，通过预测切平面位移并沿大圆传输特征来实现空间混合，无需卷积。作者同时发布了一个演化中的球形PDE基准测试套件，包含多个具有挑战性的原生球面PDE数据集。实验表明Dandelion在所有数据集上表现最佳或次佳，且在高分辨率下与基线模型的差距进一步扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力学过程发生在球面上，但默认的科学机器学习架构是欧几里得的。将这些架构应用于规则经纬度网格会产生问题：笛卡尔卷积在高纬度畸变，2D FFT在傅里叶神经算子中错误地假设双重周期性，ViT中的笛卡尔位置编码扭曲球面测地距离。现有球形数据集要么太小且风格化，要么太大（如ERA5）不利于模型迭代。

Method: Dandelion是Flower（一种warp-based神经PDE求解器）的球形版本。每一层预测一个切平面位移，并沿大圆弧传输特征。通过完全在球谐域中实现分层池化来获得类似U-Net的结构。架构中没有卷积，空间混合仅通过球形坐标变换（warp）实现。

Result: Dandelion在发布的每个数据集上都是最佳或次佳表现。与无warp基线相比，性能差距随分辨率增大而扩大：在256×512分辨率下，Dandelion和Flower2D在单步预测和滚动预测中占据前两名。

Conclusion: Dandelion验证了warp-based方法在球面PDE求解中的有效性，其无卷积设计避免了欧几里得架构在球面上的各种畸变问题。同时，新发布的基准测试套件为球形PDE领域提供了规模适中的评估平台，填补了现有数据集的两极化空白。

Abstract: Many dynamical processes unfold on the sphere but the default scientific machine learning architectures are Euclidean. Applying these architectures on a regular lat-lon grid causes problems: Cartesian convolutions become distorted at high latitude; 2D FFTs in Fourier neural operators incorrectly assume double periodicity; Cartesian positional encodings in ViTs distort spherical geodesic distances. Recent work moves towards natively spherical primitives, including spherical convolutions (e.g., DeepSphere or DISCO), Spherical Fourier Neural Operators (SFNOs), and geodesic attention. Here we propose Dandelion, a spherical version of Flower, a warp-based neural PDE solver. Layers of Dandelion predict a tangent-plane displacement and transport features along great circles. We obtain a U-Net-like structure by implementing hierarchical pooling entirely in the spherical-harmonic domain. There are thus no convolutions: spatial mixing is achieved only through spherical coordinate changes, or warps. To compare Dandelion with existing spherical architectures, we release an evolving benchmark suite of challenging, natively-spherical PDE datasets including a modified Galewsky jet, anomalous chained turbulence, Cahn-Hilliard decomposition, spherical Riemann shocks, Held-Suarez dry atmospheric transport and global ocean dynamics. This new benchmark fills the gap in existing spherical datasets which are either too small and stylized, or much too large (ERA5) for model iteration. Dandelion is best or second-best on every dataset, and the gap to non-warp baselines widens with resolution: at $256\times 512$, Dandelion and Flower2D occupy the top two slots in both single-step prediction and rollout.

</details>


### [65] [Self-Explainable Multi-Label Graph Neural Network for Correlated Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27574)
*Yingqi Feng,Yufei Tang,Min Shi,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的端到端自解释多标签图神经网络（SEMGNN），在统一框架中同时进行多标签节点分类和边掩码解释器学习，利用标签-标签相关性增强预测和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏具有训练时解释能力的多标签图学习方法。虽然已有事后(post-hoc)图解释器，但它们未显式建模标签依赖的证据共享机制，尤其在标签对弱相关或负相关时，无法准确反映不同标签间证据应如何共享或分离。

Method: SEMGNN在一个统一框架和训练目标中联合学习预测器和稀疏边掩码解释器。通过建模标签-标签相关性来改进多标签节点分类并增强单个标签的解释，使得节点不同标签可由不同但连贯的结构和/或相关证据支持。

Result: 在社交网络、娱乐和生命科学等合成及真实多标签网络上的实验表明，SEMGNN在取得有竞争力或更优预测性能的同时，能提供更忠实且紧凑的标签条件化解释。

Conclusion: SEMGNN验证了将自解释能力内置于多标签图学习框架的可行性与有效性，能够同时兼顾预测性能和解释质量，填补了多标签图学习缺乏训练时解释能力的研究空白。

Abstract: Multi-label graph learning intends to capture the intrinsic complexity of real-world applications, where one sample is often related to multiple groups or consists of multiple objects. To date, a handful of multi-label graph learning methods exist, but none of them integrate training-time interpretation capability. While post-hoc graph explainers have been developed, they do not explicitly model label-dependent evidence sharing in multi-label graph learners, especially when label pairs are weakly or negatively associated. As a result, post-hoc approaches may miss how evidence should be shared or separated across different labels. This paper advances a new end-to-end self-explainable multi-label graph neural network (SEMGNN), which aims to simultaneously classify multi-labeled nodes and identify edges significantly contributing to each target node w.r.t. predicted labels. Different from post-hoc methods, SEMGNN jointly learns a predictor and a sparse edge-mask explainer within a unified framework and training objective. Label-label correlations are used to improve multi-label node classification and enhance individual label explanations, so that different labels of a node can be supported by distinct yet coherent structural and/or correlated evidence. Experiments and comparisons on synthetic and real-world multi-label networks, in social networking, entertainment, and life sciences, show that SEMGNN achieves competitive or improved predictive performance while providing more faithful and compact label-conditioned explanations.

</details>


### [66] [Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27634)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARSANN框架，通过估计局部流形曲率来动态调整每个邻域的空间范围（半径），而非传统k-NN固定的邻域数量。在70多个OpenML数据集上验证，该方法在45个数据集的40个上超越标准k-NN，并具有统计学显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-NN在整个特征空间中强制使用相同的邻域基数（k值），但数据在底层流形上的局部几何结构可能差异很大，固定邻域大小的假设不适合几何复杂度变化显著的数据。现有方法只调整邻居数量或局部度量，而不调整邻域的空间范围。

Method: CARSANN首先使用TwoNN估计内在维度，并通过主成分分析构建内在表示；然后基于形状算子公式估计局部平均曲率，用曲率控制邻域尺度：高曲率区域半径收缩更强，近似平坦区域保留更宽的邻域支持。

Result: 在70多个OpenML真实数据集上，CARSANN一致优于标准k-NN并与自适应近邻方法相当。在控制相同基础邻域大小的对比中，45个数据集的40个上取得更高平衡准确率，均值从0.6506提升至0.7528；优势在固定k=5的k-NN对比中依然存在，Friedman和Nemenyi检验确认统计显著性。

Conclusion: 局部流形曲率可作为自适应邻域支持的有效几何控制变量，提供了一种不同于基于基数自适应近邻方法的互补范式。

Abstract: Nearest neighbor classification relies fundamentally on how locality is defined, yet conventional $k$-NN imposes the same neighborhood cardinality throughout the feature space. This assumption can be inadequate for data whose local geometry varies substantially across the underlying manifold. We introduce Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification (CARSANN), a geometry-driven framework that adapts the spatial support of each neighborhood according to local geometric complexity. CARSANN first estimates intrinsic dimensionality using TwoNN and constructs an intrinsic representation through principal component analysis. Local mean curvature is then estimated using a shape-operator-based formulation and controls neighborhood scale: highly curved regions receive stronger radius shrinkage, whereas approximately flat regions retain broader spatial support. Unlike methods that modify only the number of neighbors or the local metric, CARSANN explicitly adapts the spatial extent of local evidence. Experiments on more than 70 real-world OpenML datasets show that CARSANN consistently improves upon standard $k$-NN and is competitive with adaptive nearest-neighbor methods. In a controlled comparison using the same base neighborhood size, CARSANN achieves higher balanced accuracy on 40 of 45 datasets, increasing mean balanced accuracy from 0.6506 to 0.7528. The advantage also persists against $k$-NN with fixed $k=5$. Friedman and Nemenyi tests confirm that the improvements are statistically significant. These results indicate that local manifold curvature can serve as an effective geometric control variable for adapting neighborhood support, providing a complementary paradigm to cardinality-based nearest-neighbor adaptation.

</details>


### [67] [More Data Cannot Break a Symmetry: Identifiability by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27651)
*Jing Xu,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种设计阶段的诊断方法，用于检测无监督表征对齐中的退化问题。该问题源于刺激几何的自同构群，导致数据收集前就存在对齐歧义。作者将已知的不变性转化为设计诊断和干预手段，在颜色域中证明该失败是结构性的，并展示仅凭诊断指标选择刺激（不咨询任何学习表征）即可将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表征对齐依赖几何对应关系，但刺激几何的自同构群从根本上限制了任何对齐方法能识别的内容。现有诊断（最廉价的非恒等重标定）因密集采样产生的近重复样本而失效，会错误排序两种已发表的实验设计。作者希望提供一个设计阶段的诊断工具，在数据收集之前就能识别并规避这种退化风险。

Method: 作者将Demetci等人(2024)发现的已知不变性转化为设计时诊断。具体步骤包括：(1) 在颜色域中利用候选几何的闭式解展示结构性的退化失败——增加64倍重启预算仍无法移动对称设计，而非对称设计的相同N每次都能恢复；(2) 通过3000个子集展示判别表征模型与恢复对应关系这两个目标本质不相关(r=-0.02)；(3) 仅依据诊断指标（不咨询任何学习表示）选择9种颜色，使全部93个模型表征远离退化点；(4) 将方法推广到等距分布的朝向、音调、运动方向等规则设计与等距群相遇的场景。

Result: 仅用该诊断选择9种颜色，未咨询任何学习表征，就能使所有93个模型表征远离退化点，并将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%，且模型、层、N和求解器均保持不变。该方法在数据收集前仅需一次函数调用即可完成检查成本。

Conclusion: 该工作将无监督表征对齐的退化问题从难以检测的潜在风险转化为可在设计阶段主动规避的可操作诊断。任何规则设计与其候选几何等距群相遇之处都存在同样风险（如均匀分布的朝向、音调或运动方向），该诊断为实验设计提供了低成本高收益的预防性干预手段。

Abstract: Unsupervised representational alignment recovers a stimulus-by-stimulus correspondence from geometry alone, but the automorphism group of the stimulus geometry bounds what any such alignment can identify, before data exist. The obvious diagnostic for this degeneracy, the cheapest non-identity relabelling, ranks two published designs in the wrong order, because dense sampling creates near-duplicates whose transposition is nearly free. We turn this known invariance (Demetci et al., 2024) into a design-time diagnostic and intervention. In colour, where candidate geometries have closed form, we show that the failure is structural: sixty-four times the restart budget leaves a symmetric design unmoved while an asymmetric set at the same N recovers every time. Discriminating representational models and recovering a correspondence are essentially uncorrelated objectives (r = -0.02 over 3,000 subsets). Choosing nine colours by this diagnostic alone, without consulting any learned representation, moves all 93 model representations away from the degenerate point and cuts catastrophic alignment failures from 75% to 2% with the models, the layers, N and the solver all held fixed. The same risk arises wherever a regular design meets its candidate geometry's isometry group, including evenly spaced orientations, tones, or motion directions, and the check costs one function call before data collection.

</details>


### [68] [Fast Weight Attention for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27763)
*Yifan Zhang,Steve Ta,Jasper Zhang,Jichen Feng,Shuzhen Li,Yongxin Zhang,Yifeng Liu,Huizhuo Yuan,Mengdi Wang,Quanquan Gu,Andrew Chi-Chih Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环快速权重记忆与选择性状态空间模型中的状态转移规则（在线学习规则），在自回归语义下将前缀预测目标分解为读取后写入的时序对齐问题。推导出平方误差回归与负内积两类归一化一阶更新，得到Falcon-1/2/3及其A变体（内积版本）共六种变体，提供循环、掩码并行与块并行三种计算形式及数值稳定的正衰减重归一化。实验表明代表性变体在语言建模中保持竞争力，并在变长数字加法上改善长度外推，为循环序列模型提供了时序对齐、可塑性、遗忘与有界排练的统-框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环快速权重记忆和选择性状态空间模型将不断扩展的上下文压缩为固定大小的循环状态，其状态转移本质是一种在线学习规则。现有研究对这一规则在真实自回归读取后写语义下的行为缺乏系统分析，特别是快速记忆在时间步t暴露的局部示例是前缀对齐对而非常见的同一步关联对，这导致内部优化目标与实际目标不一致，需要统一框架来阐明。

Method: 论文推导了平方误差回归与负内积两类目标的归一化一阶更新公式。回归族包含Falcon-1（标量NLMS更新）、Falcon-2（逐列扩展）和Falcon-3（滑动窗口小批量更新）；内积族对应Falcon-1A/2A/3A变体。提供了循环、掩码并行和块并行三种计算形式，并引入数值稳定的正衰减重归一化技术。

Result: 代表性变体在语言建模任务上与现有方法保持竞争力，在变长数字加法任务上显著改善了长度外推能力。该框架成功分离并解耦了时序对齐、可塑性、遗忘和有界排练四个核心维度。

Conclusion: 本文通过形式化前缀预测目标下的读取后写自回归语义，将快速权重记忆的状态转移统一为可推导的在线学习规则，提出Falcon系列更新方法。该工作为理解循环序列模型中的记忆机制提供了清晰的模块化框架，并为未来在时序对齐、可塑性与遗忘之间的平衡设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recurrent fast-weight memories and selective state-space models compress an expanding context into a fixed-size recurrent state, making the state transition an online learning rule. We study this rule under read-after-write autoregressive semantics. For the prefix-prediction objective considered here, the local fast-memory example revealed at step $t$ is the prefix-aligned pair $(\mathbf{x}_t,\mathbf{y}_t)=(φ(\mathbf{k}_{t-1}),\mathbf{v}_t)$. The common same-step association $(φ(\mathbf{k}_t),\mathbf{v}_t)$ remains causal, but optimizes a different internal objective. We derive normalized first-order updates for squared-error regression and negative inner-product objectives. The regression family comprises Falcon-1 (a scalar NLMS update), Falcon-2 (its per-column extension), and Falcon-3 (a sliding-window mini-batch update); Falcon-1A/Falcon-2A/Falcon-3A are the corresponding inner-product variants. We provide recurrent, masked-parallel, and chunk-parallel forms, together with numerically stable positive-decay renormalization. Representative variants remain competitive in language modeling and improve length extrapolation on variable-digit addition. This framework separates temporal alignment, plasticity, forgetting, and bounded rehearsal in recurrent sequence models.

</details>


### [69] [Unsupervised Continual Learning with Growing Self-Organizing Maps and Synthetic Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27662)
*Pujan Thapa,Alexander Ororbia,Travis Desell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生长自组织映射（GSOM）的生成式持续学习框架，通过分布统计记忆和编码器-解码器模型实现类增量学习，无需存储原始数据即可进行无示例重放，且完全无监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中传统方法依赖存储原始样本或需要任务边界/类别标签，存在存储开销大和依赖监督信息的问题。作者希望提出一种无需存储原始数据、无需任务边界和标签的无监督持续学习方案。

Method: 利用GSOM进行拓扑驱动的聚类与结构生长，每个GSOM单元维护自身的均值、方差和协方差估计构成分布统计记忆；通过祖先采样生成合成样本用于重放；在编码器-解码器配置中将合成样本解码回输入空间进行后续训练，实现完全无监督的类增量学习。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法性能与有监督的基于记忆的最先进方法相当，并持续优于无需记忆的方法；在具有挑战性的单类增量场景中匹配或超越现有基线；提供了单类增量TinyImageNet和MiniImageNet的基线结果。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了无监督、自适应、拓扑驱动的神经统计重放作为可扩展且灵活的持续学习方法的有效性，为持续学习提供了一种无需监督信息、无需存储原始数据的替代方案。

Abstract: This work presents a generative continual learning framework based on growing self-organizing maps (GSOMs) that are augmented with learned distributional statistics as well as encoder-decoder models for class-incremental learning. The proposed approach enables exemplar-free replay using distributional statistical memory, which eliminates the need to store raw data. Each GSOM unit maintains its own mean, variance, and covariance estimates, which are subsequently used to generate synthetic samples for replay; in encoder-decoder configurations, these samples are then decoded back into the input space (via ancestral sampling) for subsequent training. Our method is fully unsupervised, as it does not rely on explicit task boundaries or class labels during training. Results across multiple benchmarks show that the proposed approach achieves performance competitive even with supervised state-of-the-art memory-based methods while consistently outperforming memory-free approaches. In several settings, our framework matches or exceeds existing baselines, particularly in challenging single-class incremental scenarios. We also provide baseline results for single-class incremental TinyImageNet and MiniImageNet, offering a useful reference for future work. This work highlights the effectiveness of an unsupervised, adaptive, topology-driven neural form of statistical replay as a scalable, flexible approach to continual learning.

</details>


### [70] [Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting for Forecast Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28116)
*Prasen R. Nuthanakaluva,Nava K. Gaddam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义吉布斯集成加权（GGEW）框架，将预测模型视为专家，通过吉布斯风格指数变换归一化预测损失来分配集成权重。该框架包含数值稳定、多样性感知修正和在线超参数自适应，衍生出多种变体方法，并在M4竞赛及多个滚动起点部署数据集上验证了其竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测组合是提升预测性能的可靠方法，但简单的聚合规则（如均值、中位数、裁剪均值、逆损失加权和指数加权）在不同数据集、预测视野和部署场景下的相对表现存在差异，缺乏一个统一且自适应的框架来动态调整加权策略。

Method: GGEW框架通过吉布斯风格指数变换处理归一化预测损失来分配权重，包含数值稳定化、多样性感知分数修正（Directional Gibbs-NCL和Symmetric Gibbs-NCL变体）以及在线超参数自适应（采用UCB风格bandit机制online Local-UCB来适应学习率、多样性强度及变体选择）。

Result: 在M4官方竞赛提交和Monash Traffic Hourly、Electricity Hourly、Solar Weekly等外部滚动起点部署实验上，GGEW表现出竞争性的预测性能，但其相对表现因数据集、预测视野、部署协议及预测分歧群体而异。

Conclusion: 吉布斯风格自适应加权是多种基准设定下有价值且有竞争力的预测组合工具，但本文不主张普遍优越性，而是提供一个框架和实证研究，以激励进一步探索自适应吉布斯风格预测组合在何种条件下最为有效。

Abstract: Forecast combination is a reliable way to improve predictive performance when several forecasting models are available. Simple aggregation rules such as the mean, median, trimmed mean, inverse-loss weighting, and exponential weighting are often strong baselines, but their relative performance can vary across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment settings, and levels of disagreement among base forecasters. We develop Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting (GGEW), a probabilistic framework that treats forecasting models as experts and assigns ensemble weights using a Gibbs-style exponential transformation of normalized predictive loss. The framework extends this basic weighting rule through numerical stabilization, diversity-aware score corrections, and online hyperparameter adaptation. GGEW produces a family of related methods, including Stable Gibbs weighting, Directional Gibbs-NCL, and Symmetric Gibbs-NCL. These variants share one core algorithm and differ only in the score used inside the exponential weighting rule. For sequential deployment, we adopt a UCB-style bandit mechanism, called online Local-UCB, to adapt the learning rate, diversity strength, and Gibbs variant without evaluating the full hyperparameter grid at every prediction step. We evaluate GGEW on official M4 competition forecast submissions and external rolling-origin deployment experiments using Monash Traffic Hourly, Electricity Hourly, and Solar Weekly datasets. Results suggest that Gibbs-style adaptive weighting is a useful and competitive tool across several benchmark settings, although its relative performance varies across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment protocols, and forecast disagreement groups. The contribution is not a universal dominance claim, but a framework and empirical study motivating further investigation of when adaptive Gibbs-style forecast combination is useful.

</details>


### [71] [SegBench-GC: Testing Segmentation Invariance in Multi-Step Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27678)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SegBench-GC，一个受控的基准测试，用于检验离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）对轨迹分段（administrative segmentation）的鲁棒性。通过仅改变人工备份边界及其是否保留continued value（CVT），发现朴素处理分段会显著降低性能（PointMaze中成功率从50.5%降到19.1%），而使用CVT（续值引导）可保持与未分割一致的性能。跨学习器证据表明管理性分段会实质性地影响多步离线GCRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线GCRL通常利用轨迹结构进行未来目标采样和多步目标，但记录轨迹可能因管理原因被分割，而这些分割边界并不对应真实终止。这种人为分割可能破坏轨迹结构，导致算法失效，但目前缺乏受控实验来量化这种影响。

Method: 1) 构建SegBench-GC基准：固定转换、源轨迹、目标采样、优化设置和评估，仅改变人工备份边界及是否保留continuation value。2) 提出CVT（Continuation-Valid Targets）：在人工切割处停止奖励累积，但目标从存储的后继状态进行bootstrap。3) 在PointMaze（35,000个人工切割）和Puzzle-4x5上对比未分割、CVT和朴素吸收处理三种设置。4) 通过目标级诊断和学到的critic诊断验证分析性目标差异。

Result: 在PointMaze上，50集/任务的成功率：未分割50.5%，CVT 39.1%，朴素吸收处理仅19.1%；不同分割实现在朴素处理下成功率从4.8%到31.9%波动。在Puzzle-4x5上，独立n-step基线显示：未分割47.2%，CVT 58.5%，朴素仅0.27%。目标级诊断和critic诊断证实朴素处理导致critic出现大幅乐观偏差，而CVT保持与未分割critic一致。

Conclusion: CVT使用标准的continuation bootstrapping而非新的Bellman规则，本文贡献在于受控基准、故障隔离和跨学习器证据，表明管理性分割会实质性地改变多步离线GCRL的性能。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) often uses trajectory structure for future-goal sampling and multi-step targets, yet logged trajectories may be partitioned for administrative reasons that do not correspond to termination. We introduce SegBench-GC, a controlled stress test of segmentation invariance that holds transitions, source trajectories, goal sampling, optimization settings, and evaluation fixed while varying only artificial backup boundaries and whether those boundaries retain continuation value. Continuation-valid targets (CVT) provide the segmentation-consistent control: reward accumulation stops at an artificial cut, but the target bootstraps from its stored successor. In a matched-count PointMaze study with 35,000 artificial cuts, three segmentation realizations, and three optimization seeds, final 50-episode-per-task success is 50.5% uncut, 39.1% with CVT, and 19.1% when the same cuts are treated as absorbing; across segmentation realizations, naive mean success ranges from 4.8% to 31.9%. An independent published n-step baseline (n=25) from the Decoupled Q-Chunking codebase shows the same failure on Puzzle-4x5: 47.2% uncut, 58.5% CVT, and 0.27% naive across three optimization seeds. A target-level diagnostic verifies the analytic target difference to numerical precision, and learned-critic diagnostics show a large optimistic shift under naive handling while CVT remains approximately aligned with the uncut critic. CVT applies standard continuation bootstrapping rather than a new Bellman rule; the contribution is the controlled benchmark, failure isolation, and cross-learner evidence that administrative segmentation can materially change multi-step offline GCRL.

</details>


### [72] [Performative Privacy: When Differential Privacy Maximizes Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28198)
*Uddalak Mukherjee,Edwige Cyffers,Yann Chevaleyre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将"表现性学习"（performative learning）与差分隐私相结合，提出"表现性隐私"（performative privacy）概念。在数据泄露会导致用户流失的反馈循环中，通过理论分析和数值实验证明，有限隐私预算在长期效用上可超越非私有估计，为差分隐私不仅作为保护机制、也作为长期效用最优策略提供了首个证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护学习常被动机化为"保护数据可维持信任与参与度，从而提升长期效用"，但这一主张此前从未被形式化。作者希望填补这一理论空白，从严格的框架层面验证隐私保护在长期博弈中的价值。

Method: 作者将表现性学习框架与差分隐私机制结合，建立"表现性隐私"模型：代理（用户）反复贡献数据用于均值估计，若数据泄露则可能离开系统。隐私通过差分隐私机制实现，形成"估计噪声"与"未来参与率"之间的权衡。通过理论分析系统动力学，并结合数值实验进行评估。

Result: 研究表明，当泄露与参与之间的反馈回路足够强时，有限隐私预算在长期能够优于非私有估计。这是首个证明差分隐私在长期效用视角下具有最优性的证据。

Conclusion: 差分隐私不仅是数据保护机制，在存在用户流失反馈的环境中，它同时是最大化长期效用的策略选择。该工作为隐私保护的长期价值提供了严格的理论基础，并开辟了"表现性隐私"这一新的研究方向。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving learning is often motivated by the idea that protecting users' data can preserve trust and thus participation, improving utility in the long term. However, this claim has not been formalized so far. In parallel, performative learning provides a framework for studying learning systems whose deployment affects the data they later observe. In this work, we bring these two perspectives together and introduce \emph{performative privacy}, where data leakage reduces future participation. We study a simple model where agents repeatedly contribute data for mean estimation but may leave the system when their data is leaked. Privacy is implemented through differentially private mechanisms, creating a trade-off between estimation noise and future participation. We show, through a theoretical study of the dynamics and numerical experiments, that a finite privacy budget can outperform non-private estimation in the long term when the feedback loop between leakage and participation is sufficiently strong. This provides first evidence that differential privacy can be optimal not only as a protection mechanism, but also from the perspective of long-term utility.

</details>


### [73] [SafeStep: An Interactive Demonstration of Semantic Communication for Pedestrian Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27688)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Jeremiah Yang,Sirin Chakraborty,Samuel Chamoun,Travis Ross,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SafeStep，一个基于浏览器的交互式实时语义通信平台，用于行人安全监控。平台从四个实时交通摄像头提取行人信息，通过语义通信收发器在AWGN信道传输，并渲染用户特定的位置、轨迹和风险标签。SafeStep集成了Meta-VIB语义通信设计与五个基线收发器进行对比，支持独立选择收发器、SNR、码长和信息年龄(AoI)等参数。实验表明Meta-VIB在平均任务损失上最高降低92.1%，平台在高性能GPU服务器上可支持20个用户维持5帧/秒，100个用户时无请求失败但帧率降至约1帧/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义通信系统缺乏可交互的真实场景演示平台，且信息年龄(AoI)对下游应用性能的影响在实时监控中难以被直接观察和量化。作者旨在构建首个实时的、可视化的语义通信平台，使AoI诱导的下游性能退化在实时监控应用中可直接观察。

Method: 开发SafeStep浏览器端交互平台，包含四个实时交通摄像头的数据采集、语义通信收发器（以Meta-VIB为核心，仅416万参数，无需在线重训练即可泛化），以及五个基线收发器对比。平台支持用户独立选择收发器、SNR、码长和AoI配置，并在AWGN信道下实时演示行人安全监控效果。系统架构设计支持多用户并发访问。

Result: Meta-VIB在平均任务损失上较基线最高降低92.1%。在高性能GPU服务器上，集成并发访问工作负载在20个用户时可维持目标5帧/秒；100个用户各请求不同配置时，无请求失败，平均应用响应时间低于1秒，但每浏览器平均帧率降至约1帧/秒。

Conclusion: SafeStep是首个使AoI诱导的下游性能退化在实时监控应用中可直接观察的实时语义通信平台。该平台验证了Meta-VIB在多种通信条件下无需在线重训练的鲁棒性和高效性，并为语义通信系统的实际部署和评估提供了可交互的参考平台。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop SafeStep, an interactive browser-based semantic communication platform for live pedestrian safety monitoring. SafeStep extracts pedestrian information from four live traffic-camera feeds, transmits it through a semantic communication transceiver over an Additive White Gaussian Noise (AWGN) channel, and renders user-specific positions, trajectories, and risk labels. The platform allows to independently select the transceiver, Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR), codelength, and Age of Information (AoI), and demonstrates the transceiver performance of the selected configuration through live pedestrian safety monitoring to each browser. SafeStep compares a recently proposed semantic communication design called Meta-VIB with five baseline transceivers. Meta-VIB uses a compact neural model with only $4.16$ million parameters to generalize across varying SNR, codelength, and AoI values without online retraining. Experimental results show that Meta-VIB achieves mean task-loss reductions of up to $92.1\%$. On one high-end GPU server, the integrated concurrent-access workload maintains the target $5$ frames/s through $20$ users. At $100$ users, each requesting a distinct configuration, SafeStep records no request failures and a mean application response time below $1$ s, but its mean per-browser frame rate falls to approximately $1$ frame/s. To our knowledge, SafeStep is the first real-time semantic communication platform to make AoI-induced downstream degradation directly observable in live monitoring applications.

</details>


### [74] [RiskBlend: A Multi-Signal Framework for Test Input Prioritization in Machine Learning Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27704)
*Madhusudan Srinivasan,Namith Nishal Raphae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiskBlend，一个分类器无关的测试输入优先级排序框架，通过结合历史失败模式、预测偏移、决策边界偏移和邻域变化四种互补风险信号，并利用验证学习的APFD-squared加权方式融合这些信号，在机器学习模型重训练后有效发现回归故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习分类器重训练后，之前正确分类的输入可能被新版本错误分类，产生回归故障。由于验证预测需要人工标注或昂贵模拟，检测成本高昂。现有方法主要依赖单一模型的置信度分数，未能利用模型版本之间预测、决策边界和局部邻域的变化信息。

Method: RiskBlend框架融合四种互补风险信号：（1）历史失败模式；（2）预测偏移；（3）决策边界偏移；（4）邻域变化。这些信号通过验证集学习得到的APFD-squared权重进行加权组合，实现分类器无关的优先级排序。

Result: 在4个数据集、5种分类器、4种回归更新场景和15个随机种子（共1,200个实验配置）下，RiskBlend在全部80种数据集-分类器-场景组合中取得最高平均APFD，比最强基线提升最高达0.32 APFD。基于置信度的方法仅在稀疏类别特征的线性分类器上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 跨版本行为信号为机器学习系统的回归故障优先级排序提供了重要的互补信息，RiskBlend框架证明了综合多种版本间变化信号的有效性。

Abstract: When machine learning classifiers are retrained, inputs correctly classified by the previous model version may be misclassified by the updated version, creating regression faults that are costly to detect because verifying predictions against ground truth may require human annotation, expert review, or expensive simulation rather than inexpensive model inference. Test input prioritization addresses this problem by ranking inputs so that a limited verification budget reveals as many regression faults as possible. Existing approaches rely predominantly on single-model confidence scores and do not exploit how predictions, decision boundaries, and local neighborhoods change between model versions. We propose RiskBlend, a classifier-agnostic prioritization framework that combines four complementary risk signals: historical failure patterns, prediction shift, decision-boundary shift, and neighborhood change. These signals are combined using validation-learned APFD-squared weighting. Across four datasets, five classifiers, four regression-update scenarios, and 15 random seeds, totaling 1,200 experimental configurations, RiskBlend achieves the highest average APFD in all 80 dataset-classifier-scenario combinations, with improvements of up to 0.32 APFD over the strongest baseline. Confidence-based methods remain competitive primarily for linear classifiers on sparse categorical features, which we attribute to feature-space geometry. The results show that cross-version behavioral signals provide important complementary information for prioritizing regression faults in machine learning systems.

</details>


### [75] [DART-FL: Burst-Aware Multitask Federated Learning under Dynamic Inference Demand at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27713)
*Yiming Xie,Pinrui Yu,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DART-FL框架，用于在资源受限的边缘设备上同时进行模型训练和在线推理。该框架根据推理负载动态分配推理与训练之间的资源比例，并通过基于队列的调度器将剩余训练资源按任务需求分配，使高需求任务能更早获得训练关注，在保持整体多任务性能的同时提升高频任务的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统中训练与推理共存于资源受限设备，存在两个耦合挑战：一是必须为推理预留足够算力以满足SLO要求，二是剩余训练容量需按任务需求自适应分配，使高频请求任务能更早得到训练提升。

Method: DART-FL框架在每个调度间隔内：1) 利用推理积压队列和已配置的服务能力确定推理所需的最小资源分配；2) 将剩余训练资源通过队列感知的DPP启发式调度器分配到各任务；3) 将任务分配映射为动态损失权重，使高推理需求任务在早期通信轮次获得更大训练权重；4) 客户端训练共享骨干网络和任务特定头，通过FedAvg聚合完整多任务模型。

Result: 在Stanford Cars和Oxford Flowers 102数据集上，使用合成及真实阿里巴巴trace工作负载进行评估。DART-FL能动态调整推理-训练资源分配以适应时变推理需求，将高需求任务的学习进度向突发期转移，在这些任务被频繁请求时提升模型准确率，同时保持可比的长期多任务性能。

Conclusion: DART-FL有效解决了边缘智能中推理SLO保障与任务自适应训练的联合优化问题，通过资源感知的调度策略实现了需求驱动的多任务联邦学习，在高需求任务上获得早期性能提升且不牺牲长期整体表现。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems increasingly require model training and online inference to coexist on resource-constrained devices, while inference demand can vary substantially across tasks over time. This creates two coupled challenges: sufficient computation must be reserved for inference to maintain service-level objectives (SLOs), while the remaining training capacity should adapt to task-specific demand so that frequently requested tasks can improve earlier during training.
  We propose an SLO-aware, demand-driven multitask federated learning framework (DART-FL) that jointly adapts the inference-training resource split and task-level training emphasis. At each scheduling interval, DART-FL uses the inference backlog and profiled service capacity to determine the minimum resource allocation required for inference. The remaining training capacity is then distributed across tasks using a queue-aware DPP-inspired scheduler, and the resulting task allocations are mapped to dynamic loss weights. This allows tasks experiencing higher inference demand to receive greater training emphasis in earlier communication rounds. Clients train a shared backbone with task-specific heads, and the complete multitask model is aggregated through FedAvg.
  We evaluate DART-FL using Stanford Cars and Oxford Flowers 102 under both synthetic and real Alibaba trace-derived workloads. Results show that DART-FL dynamically adapts the inference-training resource split to time-varying inference demand and shifts the learning progress of high-demand tasks toward their burst periods, improving model accuracy when those tasks are frequently requested while maintaining comparable long-term multitask performance.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Non-IID: Learner--Client Distribution Mismatch in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27715)
*Yiming Xie,Lili Su,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态、影响力感知的客户端选择框架，用于解决联邦学习中客户端数据分布与学习者目标分布不一致的问题。通过学习者持有的小型代理数据集估计每个客户端的潜在效用，并利用留一法评估优先选择最有信息量的数据源，从而实现更快的收敛和更高的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习实践通常隐含假设聚合客户端数据分布能够代表学习者的目标分布，但这一假设在现实中往往不成立。传统的客户端选择策略忽视了这种错配，而多源迁移学习要么需要直接访问本地数据，要么仅使用一次性的模型/特征聚合，均无法有效解决学习者和客户端群体之间的分布错配问题。

Method: 提出一个动态的、影响力感知的客户端选择框架。该框架假设学习者持有一个小型代理数据集，通过代理影响力信号来估计每个客户端对学习者优化目标的潜在效用，并采用留一法评估来优先选择最有信息量的知识来源，同时控制统计噪声和数据异质性带来的负面影响。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上异构数据划分的实验表明，该方法在收敛速度和最终准确率上均一致优于静态和动态基线方法。

Conclusion: 客户端贡献在不同训练轮次间存在显著变化，传统技术不足以在多源迁移多样性下识别有益数据源。所提出的动态影响力感知客户端选择框架能够有效应对学习者与客户端群体的分布错配问题，在异构联邦学习场景中取得更优性能。

Abstract: Federated learning systems are increasingly deployed to facilitate collaborative model training across a heterogeneous client population. Existing practice mostly implicitly assumes that the aggregated client data distribution is representative of the learner's target distribution or that learning from all available clients is uniformly beneficial for the learner distribution. However, such an assumption often does not hold in reality.
