Abstract: Randomized neural networks enable fast and analytically tractable training by fixing the input to hidden layer parameters at random and learning the output weights in closed form; however, their performance critically depends on a single uninformed draw of hidden units. This one shot and task uninformed feature construction often leads to redundant representations and suboptimal utilization of model capacity. To address this limitation, we propose a simple and broadly applicable residual guided procedure that greedily constructs the hidden layer using a closed form residual decrease criterion. At each stage, we (i) generate a pool of random candidate units, (ii) score each candidate by the exact reduction it induces in the ridge regularized objective, (iii) select the top k units, and (iv) refit the readout in closed form using the standard design with direct input links. This procedure yields a progressive training process with a guaranteed monotonic decrease of the training objective. The method is model agnostic: only the candidate generation is architecture specific, while the scoring selection refitting loop is shared across models. Extensive experiments on 71 benchmark datasets from the UCI repository, covering both binary and multiclass classification tasks, demonstrate that the proposed residual-guided models consistently outperform their baseline counterparts in terms of accuracy, stability, and overall ranking performance.

</details>


### [111] [Learning to Transfer Across Modes: Towards Unified Urban Mobility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28273)
*Yixuan Zhao,Man Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TransMod框架，通过构建共享的zone级空间表示和对齐异构移动模式，实现跨城市移动模式的知识迁移，用于多模式城市出行需求预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模式城市交通系统中不同出行模式间存在复杂依赖性和关联的需求动态，但现有预测方法多为单一模式设计，且隐式假设源与目标系统具有兼容的空间结构，难以应用于异构的多模式场景。此外，新兴模式缺乏历史数据，进一步增加了联合预测的难度。

Method: TransMod框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 构建共享的zone级空间表示，将不同空间粒度的移动系统对齐到公共空间中，减少结构错配和分布偏移；(2) 基于统一表示，从数据丰富的源模式学习可迁移的时空模式，并将其适配到数据稀缺的目标模式，减少对目标域历史数据的依赖。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，TransMod在目标数据有限的条件下持续优于现有方法，并提供了稳健的预测性能。

Conclusion: TransMod通过跨异构移动模式的知识迁移有效解决了多模式出行需求预测中的空间异质性和数据稀缺问题，为多模式城市交通预测提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban transportation systems consist of multiple mobility modes that coexist within the same city and exhibit complex interdependencies, leading to correlated demand dynamics across modes. However, forecasting demand jointly across different modes remains challenging due to substantial heterogeneity in space and the limited availability of historical data for emerging modes. Existing forecasting methods are largely developed for individual mobility modes and implicitly assume compatible spatial structures between source and target systems, which severely restricts their applicability in multi-modal settings. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{TransMod}, a unified framework for urban mobility demand forecasting that enables effective knowledge transfer across heterogeneous mobility modes. TransMod constructs a shared zone-level spatial representation that aligns mobility systems with different spatial granularities into a common space, thereby reducing structural mismatch and distributional shift. Built on this unified representation, TransMod further learns transferable spatio-temporal patterns from data-rich source modes and adapts them to data-scarce target modes, alleviating the dependence on extensive target-domain histories. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that TransMod consistently outperforms existing approaches and provides robust forecasting performance under limited target data.

</details>


### [112] [An algebraic proof of Colombo's difference-power determinant conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28274)
*Kun Li,Li Tie,Peng Wang,Zihan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文完成了Colombo在1928年提出的猜想：对于偶数n≥2，具有两两不同坐标的实数向量λ，定义了差分幂矩阵A_d(λ)。前人的工作已解决了偶数指数的情形，本文证明了所有超临界奇数指数d≥n+1情况下矩阵的非奇异性，从而完整证明了Colombo猜想。由此得出rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}对所有d∈N成立。证明方法是将假设的核向量转化为实二元型，该实二元型拥有比其实Waring长度所允许的更多的射影实线性因子（计重数），从而产生矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1928年Colombo证明了当d=n-1时det A_{n-1}(λ)≠0（从而为正），并且对0≤d<n-1有rank A_d(λ)=d+1。他进一步猜想对所有d≥n-1都有det A_d(λ)≠0。偶数指数情形已由距离幂矩阵的已有结果解决，剩下的开放情形是超临界奇数指数d≥n+1，这正是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 证明的核心思想是反证法：假设存在非零核向量，将其转化为一个实二元型（binary form）。利用Colombo已证明的结论和矩阵的结构性质，可以证明这个二元型拥有比其实Waring长度（即表示为实线性形式幂之和所需的最少项数）所允许的更多的射影实线性因子（计重数），这与Waring理论中的基本不等式矛盾，从而证明矩阵的非奇异性。

Result: 证明了所有超临界奇数指数d≥n+1下det A_d(λ)≠0，由此完整证明了Colombo猜想。作为推论，得到了对所有d∈N成立的秩公式：rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}。

Conclusion: 本文完成了1928年Colombo猜想历经近一个世纪的证明。该结果不仅解决了这一历史遗留问题，还展现了差分幂矩阵与实代数几何中Waring理论之间的深刻联系，为矩阵理论、组合数学与代数几何的交叉研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$ be even, let $λ=(λ_1,\ldots,λ_n)\in\mathbb{R}^n$ have pairwise distinct coordinates, and define the difference-power matrix \[ A_d(λ) := \bigl[(λ_r-λ_s)^d\bigr]_{r,s=1}^n, \qquad d\in\mathbb{N}. \] In 1928, Colombo proved that $\det A_{n-1}(λ)\ne0$---and hence $\det A_{n-1}(λ)>0$---and that $\operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=d+1$ for $0\le d<n-1$. He conjectured that \[ \det A_d(λ)\ne0 \qquad\text{for every } d\ge n-1. \] For even $d$, the conjectured nonsingularity follows from previously published results on distance-power matrices. The remaining open cases were therefore the supercritical odd exponents $d\ge n+1$. We prove nonsingularity for all these odd exponents, thereby completing Colombo's conjecture. Consequently, \[ \operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=\min\{n,d+1\} \qquad(d\in\mathbb{N}). \] Our proof converts a hypothetical kernel vector into a real binary form having more projective real linear factors, counted with multiplicity, than its real Waring length permits.

</details>


### [113] [Parser States Already Know: Structure-Conditioned KV Persistence for Structured Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28276)
*Linze Wu,Xinrui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PASK是一种面向结构化生成的解析器感知KV持久化方法，利用解析器导出的结构信号（如必填字段、参数、结构边界）来指导各层组的KV压缩策略，在0.33的KV预算下比最强压缩基线平均提升17.39个百分点，同时实现最高2.2倍吞吐量提升和3.3倍TPOT降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化生成对LLM代理至关重要（JSON、SQL、函数调用），单个字段错误即可导致下游操作失败。约束解码已能追踪解析器转换以强制形式有效性，这些转换暴露了生成token如何参与模式关键决策（必填字段、参数、结构边界）。但现有KV压缩基本未利用这一任务相关的结构信号，存在模型侧KV敏感性与任务级结构化风险之间的不匹配。

Method: PASK通过三个关键设计实现：(1) 将解析器派生的结构转化为按层组区分的KV持久化决策；(2) 利用任务错误敏感性设置最低保护阈值（minimum protection floors），并用注意力输出失真（attention-output distortion）分配残余KV容量，以解决模型侧敏感性与任务级风险的不匹配；(3) 离线校准阶段将信号编译为持久化策略，在线仅需轻量的结构条件查询。

Result: 在目标总KV预算为0.33时，PASK在Qwen3-4B的八个BFCL非live和live子类别上平均比最强压缩基线高17.39个百分点。端到端服务中，PASK实现最高2.2倍吞吐量提升和3.3倍TPOT降低，同时峰值GPU内存仅为Full KV的0.53倍。

Conclusion: PASK证明了将解析器结构感知引入KV持久化决策能显著改善结构化生成任务的压缩质量与推理效率，为LLM代理场景下的KV压缩提供了新的结构性视角。

Abstract: Structured generation underpins large language model (LLM) agents that produce JSON, SQL, and function calls, where a single wrong field can cause the downstream action to fail. Constrained decoding already tracks parser transitions to enforce formal validity, and these transitions expose how generated tokens participate in schema-critical decisions such as required fields, arguments, and structural boundaries under the active grammar. Existing KV compression largely leaves this task-relevant structural signal unused. We introduce PASK (Parser-Aware Structural KV Persistence), which turns parser-derived structure into layer-group-specific KV persistence decisions. PASK addresses the mismatch between model-side KV sensitivity and task-level structured risk by using task-error sensitivity to set minimum protection floors and attention-output distortion to allocate residual KV capacity. An offline calibration stage compiles these signals into a persistence policy, leaving only lightweight structure-conditioned lookup online. At a targe total KV budget of 0.33, PASK outperforms the strongest compressed baseline by 17.39 percentage points on average across eight BFCL non-live and Live subcategories on Qwen3-4B. In end-to-end serving, PASK achieves up to 2.2x higher throughput and 3.3x lower TPOT, while using 0.53x the peak GPU memory of Full KV.

</details>


### [114] [VISTA: Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28306)
*Zewen Ding,Zezhong Wu,Zhou Tao,Shida Wang,Shizhuo Hou,YongXiang Hua,Haoyu Cao,Linli Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VISTA是一种改进的on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)方法，通过将teacher向student分布自适应调整来避免固定teacher分布对学生推理的误导，在AIME24/25和HMMT25基准上各规模模型均优于标准OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPSD将teacher分布视为固定目标，仅更新student，但privileged conditioning（teacher看到参考解）并不保证teacher始终提供最适合problem-only推理的目标。这种单向往监督可能在teacher分布与有效student推理不对齐时误导student。

Method: VISTA保留标准OPSD的student更新，同时利用outcome-verified rollouts将teacher向student分布自适应调整。在每个verified rollout中，仅对teacher-student KL散度最大的top-k位置进行teacher适应。该方法复用标准OPSD的rollout和损失函数，不引入额外采样或单独的reward目标。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25和HMMT25基准上，使用Qwen3模型（1.7B、4B、8B），VISTA在每个规模都取得最高Avg@12，相较OPSD分别提升0.6、0.7和2.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果证明outcome-verified rollouts提供的student监督具有价值，student-to-teacher自适应是OPSD的一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by training a problem-only student on its own rollouts using dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher that also sees a reference solution. However, standard OPSD treats the teacher distribution as a fixed target along the student's rollout and updates only the student %, although -- even though privileged conditioning does not guarantee that the teacher always provides the most appropriate target for problem-only reasoning. This one-way supervision can therefore misdirect the student when the teacher distribution is misaligned with valid student reasoning. We therefore introduce Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation (VISTA), which preserves the standard OPSD student update while using outcome-verified rollouts to adapt the teacher toward the student distribution. Within each verified rollout, VISTA further restricts this adaptation to the top-$k$ positions with the largest teacher--student KL divergence. Notably, VISTA reuses the rollout and loss function from standard OPSD, introducing no additional sampling or separate reward objective. Across AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25 with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, VISTA achieves the highest Avg@12 at every scale, improving over OPSD by $0.6$, $0.7$, and $2.1$ points, respectively. These results demonstrate the value of student supervision from outcome-verified rollouts and highlight student-to-teacher adaptation as a promising direction for OPSD.

</details>


### [115] [Deriving Scaling Laws for OpenEuroLLM Models: Learning Rate, Batch Size and Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28308)
*Niccolò Ajroldi,Diana Alexandra Onutu,Haider Al-Tahan,Jörg Franke,Sampo Pyysalo,Jenia Jitsev,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练大语言模型（LLM）中学习率和批大小随模型规模和数据规模的缩放行为，开发了捕捉这些关系的模型，并探讨了Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度下的退火收益、超参数转移性以及损失对模型容量和数据集大小的依赖关系，为OpenEuroLLM项目建立了基准和缩放流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型预训练中，需要理解学习率和批大小等超参数如何随模型容量与数据规模演化，以及它们之间的相互作用，以指导未来模型（如OpenEuroLLM）的高效训练和资源分配。

Method: 在英语为主的语料上预训练密集LLM，系统研究学习率和批大小的联合最优缩放及其边际演化；采用Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度，评估学习率退火在广泛的超参数、模型结构和数据预算下的收益；检验最优超参数在稳定与退火阶段间的转移性；评估新提出的显式建模模型容量与数据量交互的缩放形式。

Result: 发现所评估的缩放形式在捕捉欠训练和过训练场景方面特别有效；建立了第一个预先训练的缩放基线和程序，并开源了所有预训练运行的数据。

Conclusion: 该研究为大规模LLM预训练的超参数缩放提供了系统性参考，验证了交互式缩放形式在宽泛条件下的有效性，并为未来OpenEuroLLM模型开发和社区复现奠定了坚实的数据与方法基础。

Abstract: We study the scaling behavior of learning rate and batch size in pretraining dense large language models on English-prevalent corpora. Beyond scaling \textit{jointly optimal} learning rates and batch sizes, we investigate their \textit{marginal} evolution with model capacity and data scale and develop a model that captures these relationships. As we employ a Warmup-Stable-Decay learning rate schedule, we further investigate the gains from learning rate annealing over a broad range of hyperparameters settings, models and data budgets, and whether the optimal learning rate and batch size \textit{transfer} between the stable and decay phases. Finally, we characterize the dependence of loss on model capacity and dataset size, evaluating recently proposed scaling forms that explicitly model their interaction. We find these approaches particularly effective at capturing both undertraining and overtraining regimes across our experiments. This study establishes a first baseline and scaling procedure for the development of future OpenEuroLLM models. We open-source the complete collection of pretraining runs used in this study.

</details>


### [116] [SymboLLM-FE: LLM-Accelerated Symbolic Regression for Automated Feature Engineering on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28408)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Zi-Yi Jia,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymboLLM-FE，结合符号回归与大型语言模型进行自动特征工程，通过符号回归提取与目标强相关的数学表达式，再用LLM精炼以确保可解释性，在六个真实数据集和四个Kaggle竞赛中超越现有AutoFE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动特征工程依赖盲目数学变换导致特征可解释性差，而基于LLM的AutoFE需要昂贵的多轮迭代才能生成高效特征，且存在偏置和幻觉风险。论文旨在同时解决可解释性差和迭代次数多两大挑战。

Method: 提出SymboLLM-FE框架：首先通过符号回归提取与目标高度相关的数学表达式来提升模型性能，然后利用具有丰富先验知识的LLM对表达式进行精炼以确保可解释性。采用统计先验引导的LLM精炼机制和单位数LLM调用次数来减少迭代成本。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集和四个Kaggle竞赛中的实证结果表明，SymboLLM-FE优于现有AutoFE方法。

Conclusion: SymboLLM-FE成功解决了自动特征工程中的可解释性差和迭代次数多两大难题，通过符号回归与LLM的互补结合实现了高性能与高可解释性的统一。

Abstract: Tabular data, as a core data format in machine learning, often lacks the discriminative power needed for high-performance modeling due to insufficient feature informativeness. Automated Feature Engineering (AutoFE) overcomes this by automating feature generation and selection, ensuring both model performance and operational efficiency. However, traditional AutoFE often yield features with poor interpretability because they rely on blind mathematical transformations, while large language models (LLM)-based AutoFE faces challenges in requiring costly multi-round iterations to generate high-utility features to effectively enhance model performance, compounded by inherent risks of bias and hallucination. In this paper, we combine symbolic regression with LLMs for feature engineering (SymboLLM-FE) to solve these challenges. We extract mathematically expressive formulas strongly correlated with the target via symbolic regression, which can enhance model performance, then refine them by LLMs with rich prior knowledge to ensure interpretability. Empirical results on six real-world datasets and four Kaggle competitions demonstrate that SymboLLM-FE outperforms existing AutoFE. SymboLLM-FE also addresses the dual challenges of poor interpretability and numerous iterations by employing a statistical prior-grounded LLM refinement mechanism and single-digit LLM calls.

</details>


### [117] [Euclidean Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28425)
*Nathanael Bosch,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Euclidean Fourier神经算子(EFNO)，通过将谱核参数化为物理波矢量的连续函数，解决标准FNO在不同周期域之间无法迁移的问题，使其能跨不同形状和大小的周期域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准FNO虽然与网格分辨率无关，但其离散谱权重按整数傅里叶模数索引，对应物理波矢量。当应用到不同周期域时，相同权重对应不同波矢量，导致FNO在跨域任务中失效，无法保持算子的一致性。

Method: 将FNO中的离散谱核替换为物理波矢量的连续函数参数化，使核权重与具体域无关，从而实现对不同形状和大小的周期域的一致算子学习。

Result: 在简单热方程和材料科学中跨晶体结构学习交换关联势的任务上，EFNO能够泛化到未见过的网格尺寸和不同的周期域。

Conclusion: EFNO作为域无关的算子学习框架，为需要跨不同周期域迁移的任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide an efficient framework for learning mappings between function spaces as they are, by construction, independent of the grid resolution at which they are trained and evaluated. However, FNOs are not independent of the periodic domain they are applied to: their discrete spectral weights are indexed by integer Fourier mode numbers, which correspond to physical wavevectors. When applied to a different domain, the same trained weights act at different wavevectors, and the FNO silently represents a different operator. This makes FNOs unsuitable for tasks where transfer across domains is crucial. We propose Euclidean Fourier neural operators~(EFNOs) as a domain-independent alternative to FNOs. By parameterizing the spectral kernel as a continuous function of the physical wavevector, the EFNO can learn operators that act consistently across periodic domains of varying shape and size. We evaluate the EFNO on a simple heat equation and on a practically relevant materials science task of learning exchange-correlation potentials across different crystal structures, and demonstrate that the EFNO is able to generalize to unseen grid sizes and domains.

</details>


### [118] [Curvature-Conditioned Multiscale Momentum with Sphere Constraints for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28442)
*Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Mingze Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于曲率条件的多尺度动量方法（带球面约束），仅沿平坦方向应用，以加速LLM预训练，实验表明在不同架构和模型规模上显著加速Muon优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中梯度噪声大、损失景观病态严重，现有自适应优化器（如AdamW、Muon）依赖梯度归一化对病态曲率的缓解有限，沿平坦方向（小特征值本征方向）的进展缓慢，而这一方向主导最终损失下降。

Method: 提出曲率条件化的多尺度动量方法，仅在平坦方向应用：将慢衰减分量（用于降噪）与快衰减分量（用于快速曲率适应）配对，并通过球面约束技术防止参数膨胀和有效学习率过快衰减。

Result: 在多种架构（稠密、MoE）和模型规模（0.12B-2.3B参数）的实验中，所提方法显著加速了Muon优化器；理论上验证了加速效果并提供了平坦方向多尺度动量的设计原则洞见。

Conclusion: 多尺度动量结合球面约束是一种有效的LLM预训练加速策略，能增强平坦方向的训练动力学，且在理论和实验中均得到验证。

Abstract: Pretraining accounts for a large fraction of the total computational cost in LLM training. However, noise-dominant gradients and the highly ill-conditioned loss landscape bring severe challenges. Although modern adaptive optimizers such as AdamW and Muon have achieved great success in large-scale pretraining, their reliance on gradient normalization offers limited mitigation of the ill-conditioned curvature. The progress along flat directions (eigen-directions of small eigenvalues), which dominates the final loss reduction, remains relatively slow. To enhance training dynamics along flat directions, we propose a curvature-conditioned multiscale momentum method with sphere constraints, delivering steady acceleration in LLM pretraining. This multiscale momentum, applied only along flat directions, pairs a slow-decay component for noise reduction with a fast-decay component for rapid curvature adaptation, harnessing their complementary strengths. Crucially, we employ a sphere constraint technique to prevent parameter inflation and excessively rapid effective learning rate decay that would otherwise arise from a naive combination. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method significantly accelerates Muon across diverse architectures (dense, MoE) and model sizes (0.12B--2.3B parameters). Theoretically, we verify the acceleration effect and provide insight into the design principles underlying the flat-direction multiscale momentum.

</details>


### [119] [How Proper Scoring Rules Shape LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28482)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim,Ville A. Satopää,Philip E. Tetlock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了不同奖励函数（五种合适的评分规则）作为训练目标对LLM预测器性能和行为的影响。尽管这些规则理论上都激励真实概率报告，但训练出的模型在校准度、概率使用以及偏差/信息/噪声的估计轮廓上存在差异，不过总体准确度和判别力差异较小。Brier训练的模型获得最低Brier分数和最高AUC-ROC，而log训练的模型获得最高log分数和最低校准误差。相近的聚合性能可通过不同的偏差、信息和噪声组合实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究合适的评分规则作为训练目标时是否可互换使用，以及奖励函数选择如何影响LLM预测器的性能和行为结构。

Method: 比较五种合适的评分规则作为二元事件预测的训练目标，对已解决的真实世界事件进行评估，分析模型的校准、概率使用、偏差、信息和噪声等维度。

Result: 不同评分规则训练的模型表现出不同的校准和概率使用特征；Brier训练模型在Brier分数和AUC-ROC上最优，log训练模型在log分数和校准误差上最优；聚合性能相近的模型通过不同的偏差-信息-噪声组合达到相同性能。

Conclusion: 合适的评分规则在作为训练目标时并不一定可互换；奖励选择不仅影响LLM的预测水平，还影响其预测错误的结构。但各条件仅使用单一种子，部分差异可能源于训练随机性。

Abstract: This paper evaluates how reward function choice shapes the performance and behavior of LLM forecasters. We compare five proper scoring rules as training objectives for binary forecasts of resolved real-world events. Although the rules share the same theoretical incentive for truthful probability reporting, the resulting models differ in calibration, probability use, and estimated profiles of bias, information, and noise, with smaller differences in aggregate accuracy and discrimination. The Brier-trained model has the lowest observed Brier score and highest AUC-ROC, while the log-trained model has the highest observed log score and lowest calibration error. Models with similar aggregate performance also reach that performance through different combinations of bias, information, and noise. Proper scoring rules therefore need not behave interchangeably as training objectives. Reward choice may shape not only how well an LLM forecasts, but how its forecasting errors are structured. Each condition uses a single seed, so some differences may reflect training stochasticity.

</details>


### [120] [REPLICANT: Learning Policies for Evading and Hardening Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28499)
*Shae McFadden,Ilias Tsingenopoulos,Mario D'Onghia,Alexander Herzog,Myles Foley,Chris Hicks,Lorenzo Cavallaro,Fabio Pierazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Replicant，一个基于深度强化学习的框架，用于在严格的标签-only黑盒威胁模型下进行恶意软件逃逸攻击。Replicant学习可复用的策略来修改恶意软件样本并决定何时查询目标，可跨样本、检测器和特征空间迁移。在七个Android恶意软件检测器和三个特征空间上，平均攻击成功率达78.8%，相对提升20.9%-39.2%；用于对抗训练时也能产生泛化性更强的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的恶意软件检测评估在面对强攻击者时缺乏真实性，因为最先进的攻击方法往往假设攻击者能访问特权信息（如训练数据、特征空间或置信度分数），这与现实中的对抗者能力不符。因此需要一个更贴近现实的威胁模型来评估和提升检测器的鲁棒性。

Method: Replicant采用深度强化学习框架，在严格的标签-only黑盒威胁模型下工作。它学习两种关键策略：(1)如何修改恶意软件样本以逃逸检测；(2)何时向目标查询。该策略是可复用的，能够在不同样本、检测器和特征空间之间迁移。

Result: 跨七个Android恶意软件检测器和三个特征空间，Replicant是最强且查询效率最高的方法，平均攻击成功率达78.8%，相较最先进方法相对提升20.9%-39.2%。此外，当用于对抗训练时，Replicant比最先进方法产生更具泛化鲁棒性的检测器。

Conclusion: 学习逃逸任务不仅能获得更强的攻击性能，更重要的是为加固恶意软件检测器提供了更好的信号。Replicant证明了在现实威胁模型下学习攻击策略可以有效提升检测器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: To determine the real-world effectiveness of machine learning based malware detection, it is vital to evaluate its robustness against highly capable adversaries. However, state-of-the-art attacks do not effectively model realistic adversaries, as they often assume access to privileged information such as the training data, feature space, or confidence scores of the target. In this work, we present Replicant, a deep reinforcement learning framework that learns the realistic task of evasion under a strict label-only black-box threat model. Replicant learns a reusable policy on how to modify a malware sample and when to query the target, which transfers across samples, detectors, and feature spaces. Across seven Android malware detectors and three feature spaces, Replicant is the strongest and most query-efficient approach achieving a mean attack success rate of 78.8%, a relative improvement of 20.9%-39.2% over the state-of-the-art. Furthermore, when used for adversarial training, Replicant also outperforms the state-of-the art by producing detectors with more generalizable robustness. With Replicant we demonstrate that learning the task of evasion not only results in stronger attack performance but, crucially, provides a better signal for hardening malware detectors.

</details>


### [121] [An Enclosed Mode Is a Gauge Choice: Topology Relative to Reach in Certified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28541)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究代码世界模型在采样门（sampling gate）下的认证限制：模型可在可达查询集上被精确确定，但不可达区域是"规范自由度"（gauge）。通过在环形仪器上证明极端情况——错误拓扑的填充盘伪影无法被任何采样门证伪且运行无害——并测量一个通道宽度γ旋钮如何使同一伪影穿越三个状态：不可证伪且无害、可证伪且代价高昂、即刻被证伪。三个原理组织经验结果：危险是相对于可达性的拓扑；修复受参数和传感器约束；缓解必须匹配错误的维度和方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样门接受的世界模型可能对门所见一切完全正确，但在门不可见区域任意错误。需要刻画认证模型能知道什么、错误会付出什么代价，特别是在遗漏为环形冻结模式（包含不可达内部）时。

Method: 引入门商（gate quotient）使问题精确化：接受-确定性在可达查询集上精确确定模型，超出可达即规范自由度。在最小环形仪器上证明极端案例（错误拓扑填充盘伪影不可证伪且运行位级无害），并通过三种模型家族的LLM合成测量通道宽度γ如何驱动伪影穿越三个状态。提出三个原理：危险是相对于可达的拓扑、修复受参数和传感器边界约束、缓解须匹配错误维度与方向。

Result: 通道γ存在膝点（~0.1），盲模型利用代价从1.09降至~0，而隐藏通道（相同第一贝蒂数）保持完整强度1.12。无家族能仅凭外部证据恢复区域；从内部看模型提出正确拓扑但无法确定参数，且提出的拓扑追踪引导持久同调摘要的错误β₁而非真理。点栅栏对一维边界失效，维度匹配的持久栅栏将利用崩溃至两课瞬态（0.999→0.058），双自由度证书使发明模式失败对称崩溃（1.769→0.029）。n维中壳使误识别接近必然而危险完全可被利用：两轴独立。

Conclusion: 认证代码世界模型的能力受采样门的可见性根本性限制：可达性决定认知确定性，不可达区域是规范自由度。危险由拓扑相对可达性决定（而非绝对拓扑）；修复受参数和传感器分辨率限制，模型甚至可能追踪错误同调摘要；有效的缓解必须维度匹配和方向匹配错误结构。在n维中错误识别概率与危险可利用性完全独立，说明两个轴需要分别对待。

Abstract: A code world model accepted by a sampling gate can be exactly right on everything the gate can see and arbitrarily wrong beyond it. We characterize what a certified model can know, and what its errors can cost, when the omission is an annular freeze mode enclosing an unreachable interior. The gate quotient makes the question precise: acceptance-with-certainty determines the model exactly on the reachable query set; beyond reach is gauge. On a minimal ring instrument we prove the extreme case (a wrong-topology filled-disc artifact unfalsifiable by any sampling gate and bitwise harmless at play) and measure, with LLM synthesis across three model families, how one knob (a channel of width gamma) walks the same artifact through three regimes: unfalsifiable-and-harmless, falsifiable-and-costly, and instantly falsified. Three principles organize the empirics. First, danger is topology relative to reach: a channel the planner can use collapses the blind model's exploitation (play cost 1.09 to ~0 over a knee at gamma ~ 0.1), while a hidden channel with the same first Betti number keeps it at full strength (1.12). Second, repair is parameter-bound and sensor-bound: no family recovers the region from outside evidence; from inside, models pose the right topology but cannot pin its parameters, and the posed topology tracks the guiding persistent-homology summary's wrong beta_1 (a sensor with a measured geometric resolution limit), not the truth. Third, mitigation must match the error's dimension and direction: point fences fail against the one-dimensional boundary, a dimension-matched persisted fence collapses exploitation to a two-lesson transient (0.999 to 0.058), and the dual freedom certificate collapses the invented-mode failure symmetrically (1.769 to 0.029). In n dimensions the shell makes misidentification near-certain while the danger stays fully exploitable: the two axes are independent.

</details>


### [122] [DARTS: Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28547)
*Aaryan Ajay Sharma,Sai Nishanth Padala,Seganrasan Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DARTS方法，用于解决解码器LLM模型合并中的表示偏差问题。与编码器模型不同，解码器面临两个独特挑战：因果注意力掩码导致偏差跨token位置累积（需要位置相关校正），以及不同token位置重要性不同（高熵位置对生成质量影响更大）。DARTS采用熵加权L1损失和逐位置加性偏差，在不增加过多参数（仅0.1%）的情况下显著提升合并模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可以将多个任务特定微调的LLM组合成多任务模型而无需额外训练，但合并模型存在表示偏差（隐藏状态与各源模型系统性漂移）。先前工作仅针对编码器视觉模型研究，而解码器模型因其自回归特性存在独特挑战，其表示偏差问题尚未被研究。

Method: DARTS包含两个核心组件：1）熵加权L1损失，对决策关键的高熵位置赋予更大校正权重；2）逐位置加性偏差（per-position additive bias），在不导致过参数化的前提下捕捉位置相关误差。该方法针对解码器因果注意力掩码导致的偏差累积问题进行位置相关校正。

Result: 在三个领域（代码生成HumanEval、数学推理GSM8K、指令跟随AlpacaEval）基于Llama-2-7B模型进行实验，DARTS相比标准surgery方法取得显著改进，且仅增加约0.1%的总参数量。

Conclusion: DARTS有效解决了解码器模型合并中的表示偏差问题，证明了考虑解码器自回归特性和位置重要性差异的校正策略能显著提升合并模型的多任务性能，同时保持极低的参数开销。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple task-specific fine-tuned LLMs into a single multi-task model without additional training. However, merged models are known to suffer from representation bias: systematic drift between the merged model's hidden states and those of each individual source model. Prior work (Yang et al., 2024a) study and mitigate this bias for encoder-based vision models using a lightweight correction module trained with L1 loss. However, such bias is not studied for decoder models due to their autoregressive nature. We analyze the problem of representation bias in decoder models, and show two challenges absent in encoders: (1) the causal attention mask causes bias to accumulate across token positions, requiring position-dependent correction; and (2) not all token positions are equally important, i.e., high-entropy (decision-critical) positions matter far more than low-entropy ones. To address these challenges, we propose Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery (DARTS). DARTS employs a novel entropy-weighted L1 loss to upweight correction at high-entropy positions where errors most affect generation quality, and a per-position additive bias that captures position-dependent error without overparameterization. We perform extensive evaluation on three domains: code generation (HumanEval), mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), and instruction following (AlpacaEval) on Llama-2-7B models, and show DARTS achieves significant improvement over the standard surgery approach while adding negligible parameters ($0.1\%$ of total parameters).

</details>


### [123] [Advancing Interaction-Sensitive Feature Selection: Novel Relief-Based Algorithms, Expanded Comparisons, and Recommendations for Biomedical Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28552)
*Kia Kazemi-Nia,Harsh Bandhey,Philip J. Freda,Ryan J. Urbanowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究重构并优化了scikit-rebate Python包，扩展了多种Relief-based算法（RBA）变体，并在多样化的基因组模拟数据上进行了严格的基准测试，发现新提出的MultiSWRFDB*等算法在检测2-way交互方面表现最佳，同时重构使得运行时间降低了10-35倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物医学数据建模前需要可靠的特性选择，但现有过滤式方法难以检测特征交互，而包装式和嵌入式方法计算成本过高。Relief-based算法（RBAs）兼顾了交互敏感性和计算效率，但需要更优的实现和更全面的基准评估。

Method: (1) 重构、优化并扩展scikit-rebate Python包，引入SWRF*、mu-Relief以及5种采用不同邻居选择和特征评分策略的新型RBA变体；(2) 在样本量、特征数、遗传力和关联类型各异的模拟基因组数据集上进行严格的RBA基准比较。

Result: 除mu-Relief外，所有RBA都能检测噪声数据中的2-way交互；采用'far'评分的RBA在检测2-way交互方面最佳（MultiSWRFDB*最优），但对主效应敏感度较低。SWRF、MultiSWRF、MultiSURF和MultiSWRFDB在主效应和2-way交互数据集上表现最佳，MultiSWRFDB在3-way交互方面表现最优。重构使运行时间降低10-35倍。

Conclusion: 新引入的RBA变体性能优异，能够稳健地保留主效应和2-way表观遗传交互，为下游建模保留了预测信号，是处理高维基因组数据的有效特性选择工具。

Abstract: As a precursor to high-dimensional biomedical data modeling, reliable feature selection can reduce computational expense, improve modeling performance, and yield simpler, more interpretable models. However, most filter-based feature selection methods struggle to detect feature interactions, while wrapper or embedded feature selection methods are computationally expensive. Relief-based algorithms (RBAs) are filter methods that are sensitive to feature interactions while mitigating these other limitations. This study (1) refactors, optimizes, and expands the scikit-rebate Python package with existing and newly proposed RBA variants and (2) conducts rigorous RBA benchmark comparisons across diverse genomic simulations. We expand scikit-rebate to include SWRF*, mu-Relief, and 5 novel RBA variants implementing alternative strategies for neighbor selection and feature scoring. All RBAs were evaluated to compare predictive feature ranking and runtime across simulated genomic datasets varying in sample size, number of features, heritability, and underlying association type (e.g. main effects and interactions). All RBAs, except mu-Relief, were proficient in detecting 2-way interactions in noisy data. RBAs utilizing 'far' scoring were best at detecting 2-way interactions - with MultiSWRFDB* top-performing - but were far less sensitive to main effects. SWRF, MultiSWRF, MultiSURF, and MultiSWRFDB yielded top performance across main effect and 2-way interaction datasets with MultiSWRFDB performing best when also considering 3-way interactions. Refactoring of scikit-rebate resulted in 10 to 35-fold reductions in RBA runtimes. The newly introduced RBAs were among the strongest performing, and by robustly retaining both main effects and 2-way epistatic interactions, these algorithms preserve predictive signals for downstream modeling.

</details>


### [124] [Blog: Survey of Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28557)
*Ruoran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2025-2026年神经网络优化器的研究进展，提出一个四轴分类框架（时间估计、更新几何、训练周期管理、表示与系统），涵盖Muon、Shampoo、SOAP等矩阵感知方法，并得出核心结论：矩阵感知方法是真正的进步，但不存在能全面替代AdamW的优化器，性能排名取决于多种上下文因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的优化器研究描述（如"新的Adam变体"）已不足以概括当前领域的发展。优化器设计空间已从坐标扩展到矩阵和层，从固定训练时域扩展到随时间变化的策略，从数学更新规则扩展到需要适配分片和低精度计算的表示形式，因此需要一个系统化的组织框架。

Method: 提出沿四个独立轴（temporal estimation、update geometry、horizon management、representation and systems）组织近期优化器与训练优化方法，并系统连接了Muon的谱归一化、Shampoo和SOAP的历史矩阵统计、自适应与混合矩阵方法、内存高效优化器、无调度训练、小批量修正以及量化优化器状态等研究方向。

Result: 实证结论刻意保持非胜利主义态度：矩阵感知方法确实是实质性进步，但没有与上下文无关的AdamW替代方案。优化器的排名会随模型规模、数据参数比、批大小、训练调度、参数划分、调优预算以及目标指标（tokens、FLOPs、墙钟时间或内存）的不同而改变。

Conclusion: 实际意义是应采纳组合式的优化器设计视角，并建立更严格的协议来评估优化器声明，不能脱离具体使用场景来评判一个优化器的优劣。

Abstract: Neural-network optimization in 2025-2026 is no longer well described as a succession of new Adam variants. The design space has expanded from coordinates to matrices and layers, from fixed training horizons to policies over time, and from mathematical update rules to state representations that must survive sharding and low-precision computation. This survey organizes recent optimizers and training optimization methods along four largely independent axes: temporal estimation, update geometry, horizon management, and representation and systems. It connects the spectral normalization of Muon, the historical matrix statistics of Shampoo and SOAP, adaptive and hybrid matrix methods, memory-efficient optimizers, schedule-free training, small-batch corrections, and quantized optimizer states. The central empirical conclusion is deliberately non-triumphal: matrix-aware methods represent a genuine advance, but there is no context-independent replacement for AdamW. Rankings change with model scale, data-to-parameter ratio, batch size, schedule, parameter partition, tuning budget, and whether the target metric is tokens, FLOPs, wall-clock time, or memory. The practical consequence is a compositional view of optimizer design and a stricter protocol for evaluating optimizer claims.

</details>


### [125] [QGPINNs: A Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Nonlocal Differential Equations on Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28589)
*Vaibhav Mehandiratta,Saket Ramchandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QGPINNs，一个基于PyTorch的物理信息神经网络框架，用于求解量子图上的非局部微分方程。该框架在每个图边上用神经网络近似解，通过统一的基于图的损失函数强制执行方程、初始条件、边界条件和顶点传输条件。框架支持多阶分数阶椭圆问题和时间分数阶演化方程，并集成软/硬约束、动态损失平衡、傅里叶特征嵌入和学习型奇异性捕捉特征等策略，还可扩展到反问题（识别分数阶算子阶数和物理参数）。在基准图结构及真实网络（IEEE 14节点系统和农业排水网络）上验证了准确性、计算效率和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子图上的非局部微分方程（如分数阶模型）在物理、工程和网络科学中具有广泛应用，但传统数值方法在处理量子图复杂拓扑结构和分数阶算子的非局部性时面临精度和计算效率的挑战，缺乏通用的计算框架。

Method: QGPINNs框架：1) 每个图边由独立神经网络近似解；2) 统一的图级损失函数耦合各边，融入连续性条件、Kirchhoff-Neumann顶点条件和Dirichlet边界条件；3) 针对多阶分数阶椭圆问题和时间分数阶演化方程设计；4) 集成软/硬约束强制、动态损失平衡、傅里叶特征嵌入和可学习的奇异性捕捉特征；5) 扩展至反问题，识别分数阶阶数和物理参数。

Result: 在基准图结构和真实网络（IEEE 14节点系统、开放渠道农业排水网络）上进行数值实验，验证了框架的准确性、计算效率和物理一致性，并展示了在反问题中从含噪观测数据识别分数阶阶数和物理参数的能力。

Conclusion: QGPINNs提供了一个通用、可扩展的深度学习框架，为量子图上的非局部微分方程正问题和反问题提供了有效的数值求解方案，在复杂网络结构上表现出良好的精度和物理一致性。

Abstract: We propose QGPINNs, a physics-informed neural network framework developed in PyTorch for the numerical solution of nonlocal differential equations on quantum graphs. The framework is designed as a general computational implementation in which the solution on each edge of the graph is approximated by a neural network, while a unified graph-based loss function enforces the governing equations together with initial, boundary, and vertex transmission conditions. In particular, the formulation incorporates standard continuity and Kirchhoff-Neumann vertex conditions and Dirichlet boundary conditions into the learning process to couple the local edge-wise neural approximations into a global solution on the graph. The framework is developed for two representative classes of nonlinear models: multi-order fractional elliptic problems and time-fractional evolution equations on quantum graphs. To improve accuracy and training stability, QGPINNs integrates several graph-adapted learning strategies, including soft and hard constraint enforcement, dynamic loss balancing, Fourier feature embeddings, and a learnable singularity-capturing feature for weakly singular solutions arising in the considered problems. The framework also extends naturally to inverse problems, including the identification of the orders of fractional operators and physical parameters from noisy observational data. We validate the accuracy, computational efficiency, and physical consistency of the proposed framework through numerical experiments on benchmark graph structures and real-world networks, including the IEEE 14-bus system and an open-channel agricultural drainage network.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Optimal Transport for Network Comparison: A Review with Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27500)
*James Hyun,François G. Meyer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用最优传输理论比较无向无权图的研究，重点介绍了Wasserstein、Gromov-Wasserstein和Bures-Wasserstein三种距离度量，并通过合成数据和真实时序网络数据验证了其在聚类和异常检测中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图度量指标只能给出图之间的差异数值，而最优传输方法不仅能计算网络差异，还能提供传输计划，揭示一张图如何变形为另一张图的过程。这是一个新兴且具有解释力的研究方向。

Method: 1) 综述了三种最优传输距离：Wasserstein、Gromov-Wasserstein和Bures-Wasserstein；2) 利用节点特征概率分布推导一维Wasserstein距离的闭式解；3) 分析Wasserstein和Gromov-Wasserstein的传输计划以识别影响距离的关键节点；4) 利用拉普拉斯谱推导Bures-Wasserstein距离的界，避免完整的谱分解。

Result: 在合成网络数据集上进行聚类评估，在真实时序网络上进行异常检测评估，验证了三种距离的实际性能。

Conclusion: 最优传输为网络比较提供了不仅包含距离值还包含可解释传输计划的框架，Wasserstein和Gromov-Wasserstein的传输计划能识别关键节点，而Bures-Wasserstein通过拉普拉斯谱界提高了计算效率。

Abstract: Network comparison using optimal transport is a growing area of research in network science. Unlike standard graph metrics, optimal transport computes both network dissimilarity and a transport plan that explains how one graph morphs into another. In this paper, we review how optimal transport compares undirected, unweighted graphs using three primary distances: the Wasserstein, Gromov-Wasserstein, and Bures-Wasserstein distances. We examine the closed form of the Wasserstein distance in one dimension via node feature probability distributions, and show how the transport plans of the Wasserstein and Gromov-Wasserstein distances capture which specific nodes influence the distance after graph perturbation. For the Bures-Wasserstein distance, we derive bounds using Laplacian spectra to bypass full spectral decompositions. Finally, we evaluate these distances using a synthetic network dataset for clustering and a real-world time series network for anomaly detection.

</details>


### [127] [Towards a mathematical theory of superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27540)
*Michael I. Ivanitskiy,John Jasper,Emily J. King,Dustin G. Mixon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用框架理论和压缩感知工具，建立了神经网络中超位置（superposition）现象的数学理论。模型将稀疏二值特征向量通过过完备字典编码，并用ReLU(W^TWx+b)进行特征恢复，证明了随机支持设定和 worst-case 支持设定下的多种恢复定理，包括对实等角紧框架的精确恢复阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超位置现象是神经网络中一个重要的经验观测，即网络可以用少量神经元表示远多于神经元数量的特征。然而，以往对该现象缺少严格的数学理论支撑，本文旨在为这一现象提供严谨的数学基础，回答"稀疏特征在何种条件下可以被可靠恢复"这一核心问题。

Method: 作者将特征编码建模为稀疏二值向量x通过过完备字典W进行线性编码，恢复过程使用ReLU(W^TWx+b)，其中b为适当选择的偏置。在随机支持设定下，利用低相干性、近紧框架字典建立高概率恢复保证（期望稀疏度可达d/log n量级）；在 worst-case 设定下，给出一个尖刻且可计算的恢复判据，并将其应用于高斯随机矩阵和等角紧框架。对实等角紧框架，通过一个新颖的 Gram 矩阵符号分布刻画推导出精确恢复阈值。

Result: 在随机支持设定下，对近紧、低相干性字典建立了高概率的支持恢复定理。在 worst-case 设定下，给出了可计算的恢复判据；对实等角紧框架（n>d+1），以相干性形式确定了精确的恢复阈值。此外，证明了关于实等角紧框架 Gram 矩阵符号分布的新刻画，该结果对框架理论具有独立意义。

Conclusion: 本文为神经网络的超位置现象提供了严格的数学理论基础，证实了在适当的字典条件下，过完备表示中的稀疏特征可以被可靠恢复，并给出了精确的恢复阈值。该工作连接了神经网络、框架理论和压缩感知三个领域，为后续研究提供了理论工具和开放方向。

Abstract: We develop a mathematical theory of superposition in neural networks using tools from frame theory and compressed sensing. In our model, a sparse binary vector \(x\) of active features is encoded through an overcomplete dictionary \(W\), and feature recovery is performed by applying \(\operatorname{ReLU}(W^\top W x+b)\) with an appropriate bias vector \(b\). We prove several recovery theorems for this model. In the random-support setting, we establish high-probability support recovery for nearly tight, low-coherence dictionaries, with guarantees when the expected sparsity is up to order \(d/\log n\). In the worst-case support setting, we give a sharp and computable criterion for which sparsity levels permit support recovery. We apply this criterion to Gaussian random matrices and equiangular tight frames. For real equiangular tight frames with \(n>d+1\), we determine the exact recovery threshold in terms of the coherence. The proof of this result for real equiangular tight frames relies on a novel characterization---which should be of independent interest to frame theorists---of the distribution of signs in the Gram matrix.

</details>


### [128] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Optimized Certainty Equivalent Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28179)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究风险规避决策问题，采用优化确定性等价（OCE）指标来衡量不确定性下的风险，涵盖均值-方差风险和条件风险价值（CVaR）等常见准则。作者刻画了已知分布下的最优策略，并证明其可简化为基于预测集的CVaR解决方案，为共形预测类型预测集提供了操作化解释。对于未知分布，提出基于合成似然模型和留出校准数据的数据驱动校准策略，实现了对OCE风险的高概率控制，并在两个无线波束赋形场景中验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际决策中，代理常面临系统状态不确定的情况，需要同时考虑风险规避。现有风险度量（如均值-方差、CVaR）各有局限，缺乏统一框架；同时如何在分布未知时可靠地进行数据驱动的风险控制仍具挑战。

Method: 采用OCE指标统一风险度量框架；在已知分布下推导最优策略并揭示其与预测集（共形预测）的联系；在未知分布下，提出基于合成似然模型与留出校准数据的两阶段校准策略，以保证OCE风险的高概率上界。

Result: 理论结果表明CVaR最优策略具有预测集结构，为共形预测提供决策论解释；所提校准方法在无线波束赋形实验中展示了对OCE风险的有效控制。

Conclusion: 论文建立了风险规避决策与共形预测之间的理论桥梁，并提供了一个在分布不确定下可操作、可验证的OCE风险控制框架，拓展了数据驱动决策的理论与实践边界。

Abstract: We study risk-averse decision making, in which an agent selects actions while being uncertain about the true system state. The risk is measured via optimized certainty equivalent (OCE) metrics, which generalize popular criteria such as mean-variance risk and conditional value-at-risk (CVaR). We characterize the optimal policy under known distributions, and show that it reduces to a prediction set-based solution for the CVaR. This provides an operational interpretation of conformal prediction-type prediction sets. For unknown distributions, we develop a data-driven calibration strategy, based on a synthetic model for the likelihood and held-out calibration data, yielding high-probability control of the OCE risk. The approach is evaluated on two wireless beamforming settings.

</details>


### [129] [I-FLOP: Fast Learning of Order and Parents from Interventional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28245)
*Liuting Chen,Alex Markham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出I-FLOP算法，将FLOP算法从观测数据扩展到干预数据，通过适配Hauser和Bühlmann的干预BIC评分使其兼容迭代Cholesky评分更新，在样本极限下能恢复数据生成DAG所属的干预马尔可夫等价类，在真实和模拟干预数据上均取得较好的性能和运行时间表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLOP算法在观测数据上表现出色，但现有因果结构学习算法在处理干预数据时往往效率不高或效果不佳。作者希望将FLOP的高效性扩展到干预数据场景，以应对实际中常见的介入式实验数据。

Method: 将FLOP算法从观测数据扩展到干预数据；采用Hauser和Bühlmann的干预BIC评分，并通过适配使其兼容FLOP中基于迭代Cholesky的评分更新机制，从而保持算法的计算速度优势。

Result: 在样本极限下，I-FLOP能够恢复与真实数据生成DAG相同的干预马尔可夫等价类中的DAG。在真实和模拟干预数据上，I-FLOP在性能（准确性）和运行时间方面均优于现有因果结构学习算法。

Conclusion: I-FLOP成功将FLOP的高效学习能力推广到干预数据场景，既保持了理论上的正确性（恢复正确的干预马尔可夫等价类），又在实证中展现出优越的性能与计算效率，是干预数据因果结构学习的有效工具。

Abstract: We extend the FLOP (fast learning of order and parents) algorithm recently proposed by Wienöbst et al. (2026) from observational to interventional data. In particular, we use the interventional BIC score of Hauser and Bühlmann (2012), adapting it to be used with the iterative Cholesky-based score updates that are partly responsible for FLOP's speed. We show that, in the sample limit, I-FLOP recovers a DAG in the same interventional Markov equivalence class as the data-generating DAG. We compare I-FLOP to existing causal structure learning algorithms on real and simulated interventional data, where it performs favorably in terms of both performance and run time.

</details>


### [130] [Localizing Global Discrepancies: Marginal Contributions and Contextual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28375)
*Tommaso dorigo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个框架，通过将每个观测值分配其在不同随机统计背景下的条件或边际贡献，来解决全局拟合优度检验中的局部化（定位）问题，即将"样本偏离参考分布"这一全局现象定位到具体观测值上。该框架连接了重采样诊断、数据估值、投影理论和事件级异常检测，并在理论推导和LHC奥林匹克异常检测基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局拟合优度和离散统计量可以确认样本偏离参考分布，但无法识别是哪些观测值驱动了这种偏离。现有方法缺乏一个系统的框架来"定位"这种全局偏差的来源，因此需要开发一种将全局不一致性归因到个体观测值的理论框架。

Method: 作者提出通过在每个随机统计背景中计算每个观测值的条件或边际贡献来定位偏差。关键理论结果包括：(1) 对称统计量的固定大小替换等价于中心条件定位；(2) U-统计量的加法分数等于一阶Hoeffding/Hájek贡献；(3) 平滑分布泛函的加法分数在leading order上与影响函数相关；(4) 无偏已知背景MMD的情形精确归结为MMD witness。此外还提出了匹配上下文减法等更高效的估计器，以及配对MMD的事件包含项简单定位器。

Result: 在LHC奥林匹克异常检测基准上，配对估计器以预测的1/(Rm^2)缩放收敛到直接经验MMD witness（m为批次大小，R为批次数量）。在m=1000、R=5×10^6时达到0.9993的相关性，AUC几乎完全相同。在共享隐变量玩具模型中，单事件信号与背景分布相同（孤立事件AUC=0.5），但跨事件依赖保留了判别信息，集成方法成功恢复该信息，而独立隐变量对照则不能。

Conclusion: 该框架区分了上下文的两种角色：(1) 高效定位全局偏差（局部化效率）；(2) 当备择假设包含共享结构时，提供额外的类别信息。这为异常检测、数据估值和重采样诊断提供了一个统一的理论视角。

Abstract: Global goodness-of-fit and discrepancy statistics can establish that a sample departs from a reference distribution without identifying which observations drive the departure. We develop a framework for this localization problem by assigning to each observation its conditional or marginal contribution across random statistical contexts. This connects resampling diagnostics and data valuation to projection theory and event-level anomaly detection. For symmetric statistics, fixed-size replacement is exactly equivalent to centered conditional localization. For U-statistics, the addition score equals the first Hoeffding/Hájek contribution; for smooth distributional functionals it is related at leading order to the influence function; and for unbiased known-background MMD it reduces exactly to the MMD witness.
  This viewpoint also yields more efficient estimators. Matched-context subtraction removes fluctuations unrelated to the observation, while for pairwise MMD the event-containing terms give a simple localizer. On the LHC Olympics anomaly-detection benchmark, the pair estimator converges to the direct empirical MMD witness with the predicted 1/(Rm^2) scaling, where m is batch size and R the number of batches. At m=1000 and R=5x106 it reaches correlation 0.9993 with essentially identical AUC.
  We also ask when context contains information beyond an event's own features. In a shared-latent toy model, the full single-event signal and background distributions are identical by construction, forcing isolated-event AUC=0.5. Discriminating information survives only in cross-event dependence induced by the shared latent parameter; the ensemble recovers this information, whereas an independent-latent control does not. This separates two roles of context: efficient localization of a global discrepancy and genuinely additional class information when the alternative contains shared structure.

</details>


### [131] [Learning between the peaks: sharp asymptotics for kernel ridge regression under power-law anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28564)
*Lorenzo Rizzi,Arie Wortsman Zurich,Bruno Loureiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了各向异性高斯数据下核岭回归的渐近行为，推导出核谱和泛化误差的精确表达式，揭示了输入协方差的幂律衰减如何重塑学习曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法理论研究大多假设输入数据是各向同性的，但实际数据往往具有各向异性结构（协方差按幂律衰减）。理解输入几何如何影响核特征和泛化性能是重要的理论问题。

Method: 在多项式高维机制n=Θ(d^κ)下，针对多项式内积核，解析推导各向异性高斯数据下的核谱和泛化误差渐近精确表达式，并根据各向异性强度α分类讨论弱各向异性(0<α<1)和强各向异性(α>1)两种情形。

Result: 弱各向异性下，方差仍在整数样本复杂度处峰值但被逐渐抑制，偏差在分数复杂度处发生转变；强各向异性下，有效维数为常数，方差不随样本量变化，偏差经历由目标衰减率控制的尖锐转变，可恢复经典source和capacity率。

Conclusion: 输入几何从根本上塑造核特征并影响泛化性能，各向异性通过目标与数据主方向的对齐程度决定学习行为。

Abstract: We study kernel ridge regression under anisotropic Gaussian data, where the input covariance decays as a power law with exponent $α\geq 0$ for polynomial inner-product kernels. We derive asymptotically sharp expressions for the kernel spectrum and the generalization error in the polynomial high-dimensional regime $n=Θ(d^κ)$, revealing how anisotropy reshapes the learning curves. For weak anisotropy ($0<α<1$), the problem remains effectively high-dimensional and retains some features of the isotropic case, while departing from it in others: the variance still peaks at integer sample complexities $κ\in\mathbb{N}$, but these peaks are progressively damped as $α$ grows; meanwhile, for targets strongly aligned with the data's principal directions, the bias drops at fractional sample complexities, decoupling the bias transitions from the interpolation peaks. For strong anisotropy ($α> 1$), the effective dimension of the problem is constant, and the variance stops depending on sample size altogether, plateauing under ridgeless interpolation or vanishing at an explicit rate under fixed ridge penalty. The bias undergoes a sharp transition governed by the target's decay rate: below a threshold, learning is abrupt rather than gradual; above it, the bias decays as a power law that recovers the classical source and capacity rates. We finally specialize these results to single-index targets, showing how the alignment of the index with the data's principal directions determines the effect of anisotropy on learning. Together, our results clarify how the input geometry shapes the kernel features and fundamentally impacts its generalization properties.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Competitive One-Step-Ahead Control of Friedkin--Johnsen Networks: Potential Games, Stability, and the Price of Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27623)
*Gabriel Gentil,Amit Bhaya*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了Friedkin-Johnsen网络中具有重叠玩家影响力的竞争性一步超前控制问题，将其建模为精确势博弈并证明存在唯一纳什均衡，分析了序贯与并行最佳响应算法的收敛条件、稳定性判据、反馈几何以及可达均衡的特征化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在社交网络等Friedkin-Johnsen网络中，多个参与者（玩家）可能同时对网络状态施加影响，且这种影响存在重叠。这就需要研究竞争性一步超前控制下的均衡性质与算法收敛性，但目前对此类重叠影响力场景下的博弈论分析尚不充分。

Method: 作者将一步交互建模为精确势博弈，通过对称正定系统刻画唯一纳什均衡；分别证明序贯与并行最佳响应扫描的收敛条件（并行扫描满足精确Jacobi条件）；对边际网络给出符号化左右阻尼稳定性条件；利用预解恒等式阐明反馈几何，并引入控制感知中心性识别目标冲突；刻画无约束、凸约束和稀疏目标约束下的可达均衡集合。

Result: 证明了序贯最佳响应扫描在任意冻结网络状态下均收敛；并行扫描满足精确Jacobi条件且在两玩家情形下必然收敛；给出了单次扫描实现所需的增广状态-动作稳定性测试；符号化左右阻尼条件既是精确均衡稳定性的充分条件，其符号反转时又构成尖锐的一阶不稳定测试；给出了竞争导致的同状态福利损失的闭式解。

Conclusion: 通过数值实验验证了稳定性阈值、反馈几何和福利预测，为Friedkin-Johnsen网络中的竞争性控制提供了完整的博弈论分析与可操作的设计指导。

Abstract: This paper studies competitive one-step-ahead control of Friedkin-Johnsen networks with overlapping player influence. The one-step interaction is an exact potential game with a unique Nash equilibrium obtained from a symmetric positive-definite system. Sequential best-response sweeps converge for every frozen network state, parallel sweeps obey an exact Jacobi condition (and always converge with two players), and one-sweep implementations require an augmented state-action stability test. For marginal networks, a signed left-right damping condition is sufficient for exact-equilibrium stability and becomes a sharp first-order instability test when its sign is reversed. A resolvent identity clarifies the feedback geometry, while a control-aware centrality identifies the goal conflicts that matter most. We characterize attainable equilibria under unconstrained, convex, and sparse goal restrictions and give a closed form for the same-state welfare loss caused by competition. Numerical examples verify the stability thresholds, geometry, and welfare predictions.

</details>


### [133] [From Generation to Discovery: Diffusion Mutation Kernels for Circuit and Physical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27649)
*Dinithi Jayasuriya,Aravind Saravanan,Nilesh Ahuja,Amanda Rios,Amit Trivedi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于扩散的"发现"框架（区别于传统"生成"），通过学习转换算子将现有工件变换为新候选，利用部分重新加噪-去噪定义扩散变异核，在保持结构可行性的同时探索设计空间，并在共形风险预算下监控中间轨迹以提前丢弃无候选。在三个电子设计空间取得突破：形式验证等价且延迟/面积显著优于Kogge-Stone的32位前缀加法器、训练语料外的新型放大器拓扑、以及显著优于工业放置器的宏布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成模型只能复现已观察分布，而真正"发现"需要产出超出语料库之外、满足硬结构约束、并在模型无法影响的评估指标上超越现有设计的工件。生成与发现是本质上不同的问题，需要新的框架。

Method: 提出扩散式发现框架：学习转换算子（transition operators）而非采样分布；通过受控的部分重新加噪后去噪定义"扩散变异核"（diffusion mutation kernel），在可行设计区域间移动并保持结构规律；模型只提供可行性结构，正确性与性能判断交给外部工程工具；在共形风险预算下监控中间扩散轨迹，提前丢弃无望候选以节省昂贵的评估成本。

Result: 在三个电子设计空间中验证：(1)形式验证等价于所有2^64输入对的32位前缀加法器，延迟降低17%、面积减少18%（相对Kogge-Stone，在布局布线流程下）；(2)七个不在训练语料中的独立重仿真放大器拓扑，增益21.9-66.1 dB，带宽72.9 kHz-207 MHz；(3)保留网表上的宏布局达到工业布局器线长的0.68倍。

Conclusion: 该框架证明学习转换而非分布、外部评估器把关、共形风险监控相结合的范式，能够有效实现超越训练语料的工程设计发现，在电子设计自动化领域展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Generation and discovery are different problems. A generative model trained on valid artifacts reproduces a distribution, whereas discovery must produce artifacts that lie outside the observed corpus, satisfy hard structural constraints, and improve on established designs under evaluation that the model cannot influence. We introduce a diffusion-based discovery framework. Unlike conventional generative models that sample from learned distributions, it learns transition operators that transform existing artifacts into new candidates. Controlled partial re-noising followed by denoising defines a diffusion mutation kernel, a learned transition distribution that preserves the structural regularities of feasible designs while moving between regions of the design space. The learned model supplies feasibility structure only, and all correctness and performance judgments remain with external engineering tools. Intermediate diffusion trajectories are additionally monitored under a conformal risk budget so that unpromising candidates are discarded before expensive evaluation. We evaluate the framework on three electronic design spaces, an environment that supplies rigorous non-differentiable evaluators in the form of simulation, formal equivalence checking, and industrial physical implementation. The framework discovers 32-bit prefix adders that are formally verified equivalent to addition over all 2^64 input pairs and reduce delay by 17% and area by 18% relative to Kogge-Stone under a placed-and-timed flow; seven independently re-simulated amplifier topologies absent from the training corpus, spanning gains of 21.9-66.1 dB and bandwidths of 72.9 kHz-207 MHz; and macro placements on held-out netlists reaching 0.68x wirelength of an industrial placer.

</details>


### [134] [Exact Decomposition of Value Functions for Two-Player Games in Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27654)
*Dylan Hirsch,William Sharpless,Sylvia Herbert*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Hamilton-Jacobi可达性（HJR）框架中，将复合任务值函数分解为基础任务值函数的代数方法从单玩家设置推广到双玩家设置。作者证明了在满足关键单调性假设的条件下，前作中关于"在满足约束的同时到达多个目标"的关键结果在双玩家设置下仍然成立，并提供了与单玩家情况结构不同的证明、反例以及该结果在多种任务分析中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HJR是安全控制理论中的重要框架，前作提出了代数框架将复合任务分解为HJR中传统研究的基础任务，但许多分解结果无法直接推广到双玩家设置。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索这些分解结果在更具挑战性的双玩家对抗设置下是否仍然成立。

Method: 本文在连续时间、有限时域（而非前作的离散时间、无限时域）下，研究双玩家HJR设置。作者：(1) 证明前作中关于"到达多个目标同时满足约束"的关键结果在双玩家设置下成立，前提是满足一个关键的单调性假设；(2) 提供了与单玩家情况结构不同的详细证明；(3) 通过反例展示当单调性假设不满足时会出现的错误；(4) 展示了该结果在多种任务分析中的应用。

Result: 证明了一个关键的分解结果在双玩家连续时间有限时域设置下仍然成立（在单调性假设下）；通过反例明确了单调性假设的必要性；展示了该结果可应用于多种复合任务的分析。

Conclusion: 尽管许多单玩家的分解结果无法直接推广到双玩家设置，但"多目标到达+约束满足"这一关键结果在满足单调性假设的条件下仍然成立。这为双玩家安全控制中的复合任务分析与值函数分解提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi reachability (HJR) is an important framework in safe control theory. HJR provides theoretical tools and numerical methods to obtain value-functions for tasks involving target-reaching and obstacle-avoidance. A recent work proposed an algebraic framework to scale to complex, composite tasks via decomposing the value functions of the composite tasks into value functions for the fundamental tasks traditionally studied in HJR, all in the one-player setting. Many of these decomposition results, however, do not directly translate to the two-player setting. In this technical note, we nevertheless show that one of the previous work's key results for analyzing various tasks involving reaching multiple targets while obeying constraints still holds in the two-player setting, so long as a critical monotonicity assumption is satisfied. In particular, we detail the analogous result and its proof (which structurally differs from the one-player case), we show via a counter-example what can go wrong if this monotonicity assumption is not satisfied, and we show how the analysis of a variety of tasks can be performed using this result. We note that, whereas the prior work considered discrete-time, infinite-horizon tasks, which are more standard in reinforcement learning, we here consider continuous-time, finite-horizon tasks, which are more standard in HJR.

</details>


### [135] [Horizon-Dependent Tube MPC for Elliptical-Orbit Rendezvous Under Mass Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27659)
*Omer Burak Iskender,Keck-Voon Ling*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对航天器交会对接中的安全控制问题，推导了控制保证失效的临界距离，并将其作为设计准则。该准则基于预测模型的线性化误差与扰动集合的比较，仅需采样周期、轨道和扰动界即可在仿真前评估。在火星采样返回任务中，该方法判定交会段不合格（该段消耗大部分推进剂），通过改用相对轨道要素重新建模恢复保证；在Monte Carlo仿真中相比基准控制器节省29%（圆轨道）和40%（偏心轨道）推进剂，中位脱靶量约5cm，满足0.20m捕获要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器从300公里逼近目标到接触，飞行跨越5个量级的距离范围。在近距离被证明安全的控制器，在远距离可能完全丧失保证却仍然照常飞行，这是危险的。现有方法无法提前预知保证何时失效，因此需要推导保证失效的临界距离作为设计规则。

Method: 推导保证失效的临界范围：将预测模型自身的线性化误差与控制器设计用于抑制的扰动集合进行比较。该界限仅需要采样周期、轨道和扰动界，可在任何仿真之前评估。对不合格阶段改用相对轨道要素重新建模以恢复保证。此外将约束收紧与预测时域可行性联系起来，使时域搜索上限成为任务参数而非求解器设置。

Result: 在火星采样返回任务中，判定交会段（消耗大部分推进剂）不合格，清除其他两阶段；重述不合格阶段可恢复保证。对已通过阶段的更高精度重构仅使推进剂变化不到0.1%，验证了规则的可证伪性。在500多次Monte Carlo转移仿真中（使用匹配随机种子再现基准推进剂至1%以内），相比基准节省29%（圆目标轨道）和40%（偏心轨道）的推进剂，几乎所有样本均满足0.20m捕获要求，中位脱靶约5cm。代价是飞行时间和计算量增加，且节省来源于保证对终端条件的要求而非更好的扰动抑制。不声称递推可行性和渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的设计规则能够在仿真前判定控制器安全保证是否失效，并指导重新建模以恢复保证。该方法在火星采样返回场景中显著节省推进剂，且通过可证伪性验证增强了规则的可信度。但需要权衡飞行时间和计算成本，且不提供递推可行性和渐近稳定性保证。

Abstract: A spacecraft closing on a target from three hundred kilometres to contact flies one guidance law across five orders of magnitude of range, and a controller that is provably safe at close range can lose that guarantee completely at long range while continuing to fly as though nothing were wrong. This paper derives the range at which the guarantee lapses and uses it as a design rule. The bound compares the prediction model's own linearisation error against the disturbance set the controller was built to reject, and needs only the sampling period, the orbit and that disturbance bound, so it can be evaluated before any simulation. On a Mars Sample Return approach it disqualifies the homing phase, where most of the propellant is spent, and clears the other two. Re-posing the disqualified phase in relative orbital elements restores the guarantee; re-posing a phase the rule already clears, in a frame two orders of magnitude more accurate, changes propellant by under a tenth of one per cent, and it is that second prediction that makes the rule falsifiable rather than descriptive. The constraint tightening also ties the prediction horizon to feasibility, so the horizon search limit becomes a mission parameter rather than a solver setting. Against a reimplementation of a published benchmark that reproduces its propellant to within one per cent, over five hundred dispersed Monte Carlo transfers per case on matched seeds, the controller saves 29% of the propellant on a circular target orbit and 40% on an eccentric one, docking inside the 0.20 m capture requirement on essentially every draw at a median miss near 5 cm. Two results run the other way: the saving is bought with time of flight and computation, and it comes from what the guarantee demanded of the terminal condition rather than from better disturbance rejection. Recursive feasibility and asymptotic stability are not claimed.

</details>


### [136] [Explicit Separators for Consecutive Levels of Parrilo's Sum-of-Squares Hierarchy over the Copositive Cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27743)
*Jiachen Shen,Hui Zhong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了Parrilo锥族K(5,r)中前三对相邻层次之间的严格包含关系的显式分离矩阵，即K(5,1)⊊K(5,2)⊊K(5,3)⊊K(5,4)。这些矩阵通过对Horn矩阵沿正内方向进行对角缩放平移得到，每个分离由精确有理Gram矩阵和对偶矩泛函证明，并通过整数运算的独立程序验证。进一步结合已有缩放定理和完备性定理，证明严格相邻包含关系在任意大层次上重复出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parrilo锥K(n,r)是协同正锥COP_n的半定可表示内逼近序列。虽然n=5时所有层次的并集等于COP_5，但没有任何单一层次能达到整个锥。关键问题是相邻层次是否真正不同：除了经典的第一步（K(5,1)⊊K(5,2)已知）之外，对于t≥2和n≥5，从未有显式矩阵被发表在K(n,t)\K(n,t-1)中。该问题一直悬而未决。

Method: 作者通过对Horn矩阵进行对角缩放并沿正内方向平移，构造了显式有理矩阵。每个分离通过精确有理Gram矩阵（证明属于内逼近）和精确有理对偶矩泛函（证明不属于更小层次）双重认证，并由独立整数运算程序验证。分离具有鲁棒性：一个固定证书对覆盖宽度超过3×10^-3的平移区间。此外，利用Dickinson-Dür-Gijben-Hildebrand的缩放定理和Schweighofer-Vargas的完备性定理，将前三层的结果推广到任意大层次。关键工具是阈值ε_r(M)：沿内方向将M带入K(5,r)的最小平移量，该量关于r非增，每次严格下降都标志着一组分离矩阵的存在。

Result: 成功构造了三个显式有理矩阵分别位于K(5,2)\K(5,1)、K(5,3)\K(5,2)和K(5,4)\K(5,3)，从而证明了三个严格包含关系K(5,1)⊊K(5,2)⊊K(5,3)⊊K(5,4)。这些分离在数值上鲁棒（K(5,2)\K(5,1)具有非空内部），且严格相邻包含关系在任意大层次上反复出现。

Conclusion: Parrilo锥序列在n=5的前四层是严格逐级递增的，第一组三个相邻分离已完全解决。该工作不仅提供了第一个公开发表的t≥2的显式分离矩阵，还通过鲁棒性和鲁棒性证明显示了这些分离不是退化情形，并通过已有的缩放与完备性定理将结果推广到无穷多个层次。

Abstract: Parrilo's cones $\Kc{n}{r}$ form a nested sequence of semidefinite-representable inner approximations of the copositive cone $\COP_n$. For $n=5$ their union is all of $\COP_5$, yet no single level attains it, and whether consecutive levels actually differ had remained open beyond the classical first step. No explicit matrix in $\Kc{n}{t}\setminus\Kc{n}{t-1}$ had, to our knowledge, been published for any $t\ge2$ and $n\ge5$. We settle the first three cases. Explicit rational matrices, obtained from diagonal scalings of the Horn matrix shifted along a positive interior direction, lie in $\Kc{5}{2}\setminus\Kc{5}{1}$, in $\Kc{5}{3}\setminus\Kc{5}{2}$, and in $\Kc{5}{4}\setminus\Kc{5}{3}$, giving three consecutive strict inclusions $\Kc{5}{1}\subsetneq\Kc{5}{2}\subsetneq\Kc{5}{3}\subsetneq\Kc{5}{4}$. Each is certified by an exact rational Gram matrix and an exact rational dual moment functional, re-verified by a standalone program in integer arithmetic. The separations are robust. One fixed certificate pair covers an interval of shifts of width exceeding $3\cdot10^{-3}$, and $\Kc{5}{2}\setminus\Kc{5}{1}$ has nonempty interior. Combining a scaling theorem of Dickinson, Dür, Gijben and Hildebrand with the completeness theorem of Schweighofer and Vargas shows further that strict adjacent inclusions recur at arbitrarily large levels. All separators were located by one threshold device: the least shift $\eps_r(M)$ carrying $M$ into $\Kc{5}{r}$ along an interior direction is nonincreasing in $r$, and each strict drop between levels marks a window of separators.

</details>


### [137] [Design of a Fast Crowbar Protection Circuit for Single Event Effect Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27772)
*Collin Burt,Joseph D'Amico,Joseph Van Grinsven,Tommy Aldaz,Nathaniel Dodds*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文描述了一种定制的撬棍（crowbar）电路设计，当电流超过可编程阈值时能快速切断电源，用于在单粒子闩锁/烧毁测试中保护器件免遭永久损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单粒子闩锁（SEL）和单粒子烧毁（SEB）测试中，器件可能因过流而遭受永久性损坏，因此需要一种能够快速响应并切断电源的保护电路。

Method: 设计了一种定制的撬棍电路，通过可编程的电流阈值检测机制，在电流超过设定阈值时迅速移除电源供应。

Result: 该电路能够在超过可编程电流阈值时快速切断电源，有效保护被测器件在辐射测试中免受永久损伤。

Conclusion: 该定制撬棍电路为单粒子效应测试提供了一种可靠的过流保护方案，有助于保障测试过程中器件的安全。

Abstract: We describe the design of a custom crowbar circuit that quickly removes power when a programmable current threshold is exceeded. This protects devices from permanent damage during single event latchup/burnout tests.

</details>


### [138] [Observability Analysis for Fusion of Doppler Measurements in Multistatic Radar Near the Tx-Rx Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27838)
*Rong Yang,Yaakov Bar-Shalom,Leung Hong Huat Michael*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究目标位于发射-接收基线模糊区内的多静态测量融合问题，重点针对两个紧密放置的静态收发对配置。在此区域内，距离和方位测量精度急剧下降，多普勒测量成为唯一可靠信息源。论文分析了目标轨迹的可观测性，并提出了一种仅利用多普勒测量的融合方法，采用多初始点最大似然非线性估计器进行初始状态估计，再结合扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行动态状态更新。仿真结果与可观测性分析一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Tx-Rx基线模糊区内，两个紧密放置的静态收发对虽能提供重叠探测区域并扩展有效探测范围，但距离和方位测量精度急剧退化，仅有多普勒测量可靠，导致目标轨迹估计的可观测性边际化甚至完全丧失，这是一个亟待解决的关键挑战。

Method: 1) 在不同条件下分析目标轨迹的可观测性，使系统设计者能够提前评估系统性能；2) 开发仅基于多普勒测量的融合方法，采用多初始点最大似然（ML）非线性估计器进行初始状态估计；3) 使用扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）进行动态状态更新。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提出的多普勒融合方法有效，且仿真结果与所进行的可观测性分析结论一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为基线模糊区内的多静态系统提供了可观测性分析框架和仅多普勒测量的目标跟踪方案，使系统设计者能够预先评估性能，并为后续目标轨迹估计提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: This paper studies multistatic measurement fusion when a target lies within the Tx--Rx (Transmitter-Receiver) baseline ambiguity zone, with particular emphasis on configurations involving two closely spaced stationary Tx--Rx pairs. Such configurations provide overlapping detectable regions and extend the effective detection range compared with sparsely spaced multistatic systems. However, in this region, the accuracy of range and bearing measurements degrades rapidly, and Doppler measurements often remain the only reliable information source. As a result, target trajectory estimation becomes highly challenging, with observability being marginal or even completely lost.
  To address this problem, the observability of target trajectories is analyzed under various conditions, enabling system designers to assess system performance in advance. A Doppler-only measurement fusion approach is then developed, employing a multiple-initial-point Maximum Likelihood (ML) nonlinear estimator for initial state estimation, followed by dynamic state updates using an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). Simulation results are presented and shown to be consistent with the observability analysis.

</details>


### [139] [Graphon Design for Human-Machine Coordination under Bounded Rationality: Optimality of Stochastic Block Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27851)
*Zhewei Wang,Vu Anh Phi,Marcos M. Vasconcelos*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统中如何设计网络拓扑以最大化协作指标。在异质智能体（如机器和人）参与猎鹿博弈的背景下，智能体具有不同层级的有界理性，导致决策过程中的不确定性和错误倾向。由于在离散有限图空间上优化通常计算上不可行，作者采用平均场方法将问题提升到图极限（graphon）空间，分析智能体遵循对数线性学习动力学。通过变分法证明，对于双峰理性分布的系统，只需在随机块模型系综中搜索最优图极限，并提出水填充算法寻找局部最优图极限，最后可从优化后的图极限采样有限图，绕过离散图优化的组合复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，协作是一个理想的特性，但异质智能体（如机器与人）之间的协作促进面临挑战。智能体具有不同层级的有界理性，导致学习和决策过程中的不确定性。设计能最大化全局协作指标的网络拓扑是一个关键问题，但直接优化离散有限图空间在计算上不可行，需要新的数学框架来克服这一困难。

Method: 采用平均场方法将离散图优化问题提升到连续图极限（graphon）空间；分析智能体在对数线性学习动力学下的行为；使用变分法证明对于双峰理性分布，最优图极限可在随机块模型系综中找到；提出水填充（water-filling）算法来求解局部最优图极限；最后从优化后的图极限中采样有限图。

Result: 证明了对于具有双峰理性分布的系统，搜索最优图极限只需限制在随机块模型系综中即可，从而大幅缩小搜索空间；提出的水填充算法能够有效地找到局部最优图极限，且通过从图极限采样有限图可以规避离散图优化的组合复杂性。

Conclusion: 通过平均场方法和图极限理论，成功将网络拓扑优化的离散组合问题转化为连续优化问题，为促进异质智能体间的协作提供了一种计算可行且具有理论保证的框架。

Abstract: Coordination is a desirable feature in multi-agent systems, ranging from robotic swarms to socioeconomic networks. This paper is concerned with promoting coordination among heterogeneous agents, e.g., machines and humans, interacting in a stag-hunt game. In our model the agents exhibit bounded rationality at different levels, which leads to uncertainty and a propensity for errors during learning and decision-making processes. This paper addresses the problem of designing a network topology that maximizes a global metric of coordination under such constraints. While optimizing over the discrete space of finite graphs is generally computationally intractable, we employ a mean-field approach to lift the problem into the space of graphons. Within this framework, we analyze agents following a logit learning dynamics. Using calculus of variations, we show that for systems with a bimodal rationality profile, it suffices to search for optimal graphons in the ensemble of stochastic block models. We then propose a water-filling algorithm to find a locally optimal graphon. Finite graphs can then be sampled from the optimized graphon, bypassing the inherent combinatorial complexities of discrete graph optimization.

</details>


### [140] [AutoDRI: Bridging the Semantic Gap for Automated Design Rule Integration in CP-SAT-Based Cell Synthesis under Multi-Patterning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27891)
*Yuhao Ren,Yucheng Wang,Zihao Chen,Chung-Kuan Cheng,Zhiang Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: AutoDRI是一个多智能体框架，用于自动化标准单元综合中的设计规则集成（DRI），通过几何语义库、标准化冲突集编码、构造性多色切割建模和反馈驱动多智能体流程，弥合自然语言设计规则与可执行CP-SAT约束之间的语义鸿沟。实验表明在41个单元基准和10+复杂规则下达到近乎完美的规则集成正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计规则集成（DRI）仍然是基于CP-SAT的标准单元综合和先进节点快速技术启用的主要瓶颈，严重依赖人工和领域专家知识，且现有低层规则编码无法表达新兴约束（如多图案化技术下的切割规则）。

Method: AutoDRI结合了四个关键组件：几何语义库、标准化冲突集编码、构造性多色切割建模方法，以及反馈驱动的多智能体流程，用于将自然语言设计规则转化为可执行的CP-SAT约束。

Result: 在41个单元基准和10+复杂规则（包括彩色切割掩模间距规则）下，AutoDRI在Gemini-3-pro上达到33/33的正确集成，在GPT-5.4上达到32/33，运行时间与手动硬编码相当，并通过KLayout DRC和Cadence LVS验证。

Conclusion: AutoDRI成功实现了自动化设计规则集成，解决了多图案化技术下新兴约束的表达能力问题，为先进节点的标准单元综合提供了高正确率、高效率的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Design-rule integration (DRI) remains a major bottleneck for scalable (Constraint Programming with SAT) CP-SAT-based standard cell synthesis and rapid technology enablement at advanced nodes. It still depends heavily on manual effort and domain expertise. Moreover, existing low-level rule encodings are not expressive enough for emerging constraints such as cut-based rules under multi-patterning technology. This paper presents \textbf{AutoDRI}, a multi-agent framework for automated design-rule integration in standard cell synthesis. AutoDRI combines a geometric semantic library, a standardized conflict-set encoding, a constructive multicolor-cut modeling method, and a feedback-driven multi-agent flow to bridge the semantic gap between natural-language design rules and executable CP-SAT constraints. In the reported experiments, AutoDRI achieves near-perfect rule-integration correctness across 41 cell benchmarks under 10+ complex rules, including colored cut-mask spacing rules, reaching 33/33 correct integrations with Gemini-3-pro and 32/33 with GPT-5.4, while maintaining runtime comparable to manual hard-coding and passing KLayout DRC and Cadence LVS.

</details>


### [141] [Backup Control Barrier Function Synthesis using Sum-of-Squares Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27916)
*Jungbae Chun,Shima Sadat Mousavi,David E. J. van Wijk,Ersin Daş,Aaron D. Ames,Felix Biertümpfel*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过有限时域平方和（SOS）反向可达集来合成更不保守的备用控制对（backup pair）的方法，并将其集成到备用控制屏障函数（bCBF）框架中，从而认证更大的安全集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的备用控制屏障函数（bCBF）依赖于预先给定的备用集合和控制器，其性能强烈取决于这一预设对的选择。如果预设的备用对过于保守或选择不当，会导致认证的安全集合较小，从而限制系统的安全性能。

Method: 从初始备用集合出发，利用有限时域平方和（SOS）反向可达性计算，构造一个经SOS认证的有限时域反向可达集和控制器，该集合满足安全约束和输入约束，同时能将轨迹引导至原始备用集合。随后给出该认证集合成为分段备用控制器有效备用集合的条件，并将得到的备用对集成到bCBF框架中。

Result: 所构造的有限时域SOS反向可达集在满足安全与输入约束的前提下，可认证比传统备用集合更大的安全集合，从而降低备用对选择的保守性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种构建性的备用对合成方法，能够有效扩大bCBF框架下可认证的安全集合，减少了备用对预设对性能的依赖。

Abstract: Backup control barrier functions (bCBFs) enforce safety for input-constrained nonlinear systems using a pre-certified backup set and controller, but their performance depends strongly on this prescribed pair. This letter develops a constructive method for synthesizing a less conservative backup pair via finite horizon sum-of-squares (SOS) backward reachability. Starting from an initial backup set, we compute an SOS-certified finite horizon backward reachable set and controller that satisfy safety and input constraints while steering trajectories to the original backup set. We then provide conditions under which this certified set becomes a valid backup set for a piecewise backup controller. The resulting backup pair is integrated into the bCBF framework to certify larger safe sets.

</details>


### [142] [Securing Cooperative Sensing in UAV Swarms Against Conformity-Driven Byzantine Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28017)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Qiuping Li,He Fang,Jiamin Li,Dongming Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对ISAC赋能的6G无人机蜂群网络中，模仿性从众合作机制被拜占庭攻击者利用制造虚假共识的问题，提出了一种融合进化博弈论与最大后验估计的从众感知拜占庭弹性融合框架。该框架刻画了正常无人机在有界理性意见动力学下的策略更新，推导了错误信息比例的演化动力学及其进化稳定状态（ESS），并建立了三个理论结果。通过将预测的错误动力学嵌入逐节点MAP规则，该融合机制在不同网络拓扑、攻击强度、网络规模和感知误差分布下均能达到接近100%的情境推断准确率，且在±20%参数失配下仍保持99%以上准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ISAC赋能的6G无人机蜂群网络中，模仿性从众合作机制容易被拜占庭攻击者利用来制造虚假共识，导致正常无人机的有效错误概率动态演化并远超其固有感知误差，使得基于独立性假设的传统融合方法失效。现有方法忽视了从众机制导致的错误概率相关性，亟需一种能够感知并建模从众共识演化的拜占庭弹性融合方案。

Method: 本文提出的框架将进化博弈论与最大后验（MAP）估计相结合。具体地：(1) 用有界理性意见动力学刻画正常无人机的策略更新；(2) 在死亡-出生更新规则下推导错误信息比例的演化动力学及进化稳定状态（ESS）；(3) 建立了三个理论结果：零阶ESS仅通过均值依赖误差分布、一阶弱选择修正的闭式解及任意选择强度下精确的均场不动点、攻击概率超过1/2时蜂群级错误信息可淹没多数（该阈值与感知误差和恶意比例无关）；(4) 将预测的错误动力学嵌入逐节点MAP规则实现融合。

Result: 所提出的融合机制在不同网络拓扑、攻击强度、网络规模和感知误差分布下实现接近100%的情境推断准确率，在±20%参数失配下保持99%以上准确率。相比之下，多数投票、信誉加权和独立融合方法在跨过多数翻转阈值后完全失效。

Conclusion: 本文首次将从众共识的演化动力学纳入拜占庭弹性融合设计，揭示了攻击概率1/2作为多数翻转的关键阈值，并证明了所提出的从众感知MAP融合方法在广泛条件下具有强健性，为ISAC使能的6G无人机蜂群网络安全融合提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: In integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enabled 6G unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarm networks, the widely adopted imitation-based conformity cooperation mechanism can be exploited by Byzantine attackers to fabricate false consensus, causing the effective error probability of normal UAVs to evolve dynamically and far exceed their inherent sensing errors, which invalidates conventional fusion methods built on the independence assumption. This paper proposes a conformity-aware Byzantine-resilient fusion framework that couples evolutionary game theory with maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation. First, the strategy updates of normal UAVs are characterized by bounded-rational opinion dynamics, and the evolution dynamics of the misinformation ratio together with its evolutionarily stable state (ESS) are derived under death birth updating. Three theoretical results are then established: under heterogeneous per-node sensing errors, the zeroth-order ESS depends on the error distribution only through its mean; a closed-form first-order weak-selection correction to the ESS is obtained, together with an exact mean-field fixed point valid for arbitrary selection intensity; and it is revealed that swarm level misinformation can overwhelm the majority if and only if the attack probability exceeds one half, with this threshold independent of both the sensing error and the malicious ratio. Embedding the predicted error dynamics into a per-node MAP rule, the resulting fusion mechanism achieves nearly 100% situation-inference accuracy under different network topologies, attack intensities, network scales, and sensing-error distributions, and maintains accuracy above 99% under +-20% parameter mismatch. In contrast, majority voting, reputation weighting, and independent fusion collapse completely once the majority-flip threshold is crossed.

</details>


### [143] [Anytime Primal--Dual Certification of the Maximum Disturbance Radius in Robust MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28056)
*Wenqi Cai,Muhammad Bakr Abdelghany,Kyriakos G. Vamvoudakis,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了可调集鲁棒模型预测控制（MPC）中，在有限步外部（out-of-set）扰动后剩余鲁棒储备量的问题，提出了一种由独立原始和对偶校正层级构成的双侧储备消耗包络，并基于其有限步精确性设计了基数优先的自适应算法，可在任意时刻返回有效证书，比暖启动完全重优化快约5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可调集鲁棒MPC方法主要关注在集内扰动下优化和传播鲁棒储备（即状态相关的最大扰动半径），但尚未研究在有限步集外扰动后还剩余多少储备，且无需立即重新求解完整优化问题。这在实际场景中具有重要意义，因为系统可能遭遇超出预设扰动的意外干扰。

Method: 提出一种双侧储备消耗包络，由独立的原始校正层级和其对偶校正层级构成，分别提供单调的下界和上界。该包络在活动集（active-set）变化时仍然有效，具有在线可计算的宽度，并在子空间扩展下单调收缩。基于其有限步精确性，进一步提出基数优先的自适应算法，以任意时间（anytime）方式运行：每次完成的降阶求解都返回一个有效证书，一旦达到预定容差即可提前终止。

Result: 在测试案例中，数值实验未报告任何证书违规，且在2%证书宽度容差下，相较暖启动完全重优化实现了最高4.97倍的中位数加速。

Conclusion: 该研究为鲁棒MPC在有限集外扰动后如何高效评估剩余鲁棒储备提供了理论框架和高效算法，其任意时间属性与有限步精确性使该方法兼具实用性和可拓展性，显著减少了重新优化所需计算成本。

Abstract: Adjustable-set robust model predictive control (MPC) characterizes a state-dependent maximum disturbance radius, which can be interpreted as a certified robustness reserve. Existing methods primarily focus on optimizing and propagating this reserve under in-set disturbances. This letter investigates how much reserve remains after a finite out-of-set disturbance without immediately re-solving the full optimization problem. To this end, we propose a two-sided reserve-depletion envelope formed by independent primal and dual correction hierarchies, which supply monotone lower and upper bounds, respectively. The envelope remains valid across active-set changes, has an online-computable width, and contracts monotonically under subspace expansion. Leveraging its finite-step exactness, we present a basis-first adaptive algorithm that operates in an anytime manner: every completed reduced solve returns a valid certificate, enabling early termination once a prescribed tolerance is reached. Across the tested cases, numerical studies report no certificate violations and median speedups of up to 4.97x over warm-started full re-optimization at a 2% certificate-width tolerance.

</details>


### [144] [Managing Inherent Risk: On the Conceptualization of Risk in Defense Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28093)
*NIklas Braun,Leon J. Brettin,Marvin Loba,Markus Maurer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了部署于民用环境中的国防系统所面临的独特风险格局，指出与传统民用安全工程不同，国防系统既需造成伤害以发挥防御功能，又面临外部威胁带来的物理安全风险，并对比了民用与军事领域标准中的风险概念，主张将这一复杂风险格局纳入公共沟通以促进建设性讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国防系统部署于民用环境中时，其风险与民用安全工程的风险格局截然不同——国防系统必须造成伤害才具有效用，且不能消除所有风险，这给公众接受度带来挑战。现有标准中的风险概念可能不足以涵盖这种复杂性，需要重新审视。

Method: 采用概念比较的方法，对民用领域和军事领域的现有标准中的风险概念进行对比分析，识别出需要额外考虑的扩展风险格局，特别是外部威胁对物理安全造成的风险。

Result: 发现国防系统需要纳入一个扩展的风险格局，不仅要考虑系统自身运行的风险，还要考虑外部威胁带来的物理安全风险，这与传统民用安全标准所涵盖的风险范围有本质区别。

Conclusion: 通过将这种复杂的风险格局纳入国防系统的公共沟通中，可以激发更富建设性的公众讨论，从而促进公众对这些系统的接受与理解。

Abstract: Certain defense systems are, by nature, deployed in a civilian environment in order to serve a defensive function for that environment. However, the risk involved with their deployment and operation poses a challenge for the public acceptance of these systems. Compared to safety engineering for civilian systems, the risk constellation is quite different. While reducing risk of safety-critical systems is generally desirable, defense systems are required to cause harm in order to be useful. In both cases, not all risk can be eliminated. The complex risk constellation of defense systems has implications for the systems' designs. We compare the concepts of risk in existing standards from both the civilian and the military domains. An extended constellation of risks needs to be considered, including risk caused by external threats to physical security. By including this risk constellation in public communication about defense systems, we aim to stimulate a productive debate.

</details>


### [145] [Distributed Model Predictive Control for Optimal Consensus of Constrained Heterogeneous Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28180)
*Nan Bai,Tao Liu,Qishao Wang,Zhisheng Duan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了模型预测控制（MPC）框架下受约束异构多智能体系统的分布式最优一致性控制问题，同时优化控制输入序列和动态可行的共识平衡点，并提出分布式原始-对偶算法求解，建立了递归可行性和渐近一致性的充分终端条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多智能体系统在分布式控制中面临约束条件和一致性性能冲突的挑战，现有方法通常未同时优化控制输入和共识平衡点，导致一致性能受限，因此需要在MPC框架下联合优化二者以提升整体表现。

Method: 在MPC框架内同时优化控制输入序列和动态可行的共识平衡点，形成每个预测时刻的耦合约束优化问题；开发分布式原始-对偶算法求解该优化问题，并推导局部可验证的收敛条件；建立充分终端条件以保证递归可行性和闭环系统的渐近一致。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提方法的有效性，证明了分布式原始-对偶算法的收敛性以及MPC框架下闭环异构多智能体系统的递归可行性与渐近一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPC框架能够有效解决受约束异构多智能体系统的分布式最优一致性问题，在保证收敛性的同时提升了一致性能，为异构系统的分布式协同控制提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the distributed optimal consensus control problem of constrained heterogeneous multi-agent systems within a model predictive control (MPC) scheme. Both the control input sequence and the dynamically feasible consensus equilibrium are optimized simultaneously within the proposed MPC framework to improve consensus performance, yielding a coupled constrained optimization problem at each prediction time. A distributed primal--dual algorithm is developed to solve the resulting optimization problem, and locally verifiable conditions are derived to guarantee its convergence. Furthermore, sufficient terminal conditions are established for the proposed MPC framework to guarantee the recursive feasibility and asymptotic consensus of the closed-loop heterogeneous multi-agent systems. Finally, numerical simulations verify the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [146] [SafeLink-Agent: Agentic Maintenance for Adaptive Bitrate Controllers over Dynamic Starlink Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28194)
*Hongjun Xie,Bowen Zhang,Genke Yang,Pengcheng Luo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeLink-Agent，一个基于智能体（agentic）的维护框架，用于在动态星链（Starlink）低轨卫星网络上维护自适应码率（ABR）控制器。该框架利用大语言模型（LLM）生成候选补丁，并通过回放验证决定是否安全提交，同时支持基于规则和基于学习的控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starlink等低轨卫星网络使高清视频流在固定地面覆盖之外成为可能，但其接入链路随时间和区域变化，导致吞吐量尾部、延迟、波动性和切换条件不断变化。现有ABR控制器通常针对特定网络条件设计、调优或训练，难以适应星链网络的动态性和复杂性，需要一种能够持续维护和修复ABR控制器的框架。

Method: SafeLink-Agent包含三个关键组件：1）故障总结模块，汇总暴露的失败情况；2）基于LLM的智能体补丁提议器，生成候选补丁；3）回放验证机制，判断每个补丁是否能安全提交。该框架统一了基于规则和基于学习的控制器的维护工作流。
