This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [A Design Concept of Forecasting Software for Normalized Vector Autoregressions with Fat Tails and Stochastic Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28087)
*Fei Shang,Xiaolei Wang,Tomasz Woźniak*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一套用于宏观经济预测的R语言软件包套件，该套件利用先进的贝叶斯、结构化、多元、动态、分层、非线性和非高斯模型，支持结构分析与预测分析，并适用于不同类型、维度和采样频率的时间序列数据。软件设计通过精选模型、C++高效算法、先进计量经济方法、复杂输入输出对象处理和标准化工作流，兼顾计算效率与R语言的数据操作便利性。作者通过实例证明该套件能促进预测领域的原创研究贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观经济预测需要处理高级贝叶斯、结构化、多元、动态等复杂模型，每个新增功能都会增加计算复杂度。作者旨在开发一套兼顾计算效率（C++）与数据操作便利性（R）的软件工具，以支持结构性和预测性分析，适用于多种类型和频率的时间序列数据。

Method: 该方法的核心是设计一套R软件包套件，通过以下策略解决计算复杂性挑战：(1) 精心挑选的模型集合；(2) 用C++实现高效算法；(3) 采用先进的计量经济学和数值方法；(4) 稳健处理复杂输入输出对象；(5) 标准化工作流程。这样在保留C++计算效率的同时，提供R语言的数据处理便利性。

Result: 作者展示了该套件能促进预测领域的原创研究贡献。具体示例表明，使用非中心参数化的随机波动率（non-centred stochastic volatility）的向量自回归模型，相比中心参数化的随机波动率模型，能提升密度预测和点预测的准确性。

Conclusion: 该R软件包套件成功地将高效计算（C++）与易用性（R）相结合，为复杂宏观经济模型的预测分析提供了标准化、稳健的工具支持，并通过实例验证了其在实际预测研究中的价值，特别是在改进随机波动率建模方面展示了实质性贡献。

Abstract: We present a suite of R packages for macroeconomic forecasting that leverages advanced Bayesian, structural, multivariate, dynamic, hierarchical, non-linear, and non-Gaussian models. The suite enables both structural and predictive analyses, and is adapted to time series data across various types, dimensions, and sampling frequencies. Each additional feature increases computational complexity. To address this challenge, our software design incorporates a carefully curated selection of models, efficient algorithms implemented in C++, advanced econometric and numerical methods, robust handling of complex input and output objects, and standardised workflows. This approach combines the computational efficiency of C++ with the convenience of working with data in R. We demonstrate that our packages facilitate original research contributions in forecasting, as illustrated by our example in which vector autoregressions with non-centred stochastic volatility enhance density and point predictions relative to models with centred stochastic volatility.

</details>


### [247] [Causal Non-causal State Space Models and the Modelling of Financial Bubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28115)
*Frederik Bjerg Krabbe*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究因果非因果状态空间模型，用于建模具有局部爆炸性上涨后急剧下跌特征的时间序列（如股票价格）。论文证明了Gourieroux和Zakoian（2017）提出的因果非因果卷积自回归模型与理性预期股票价格模型相容，并探讨了该模型中的状态推断和参数推断问题，最后通过估计互联网泡沫规模验证了模型的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的因果状态空间模型难以刻画金融时间序列中"快速上涨后急剧下跌"的局部爆炸性特征。作者希望引入能够同时捕捉因果和非因果动态关系的模型，并为其提供理论基础和推断方法。

Method: 论文首先建立因果非因果卷积自回归模型与理性预期股票价格模型之间的一致性联系；其次，讨论因果非因果状态空间模型中的状态和参数推断问题；最后，深入研究了因果非因果卷积自回归模型，提供新的理论结果，并使用该模型结合稳定非因果自回归模型作为基准，对互联网泡沫规模进行实时和事后估计。

Result: 论文为因果非因果状态空间模型提供了系统的推断框架，给出了因果非因果卷积自回归模型的新理论结果，并通过互联网泡沫大小的实时和事后估计展示了该模型的实用价值。

Conclusion: 因果非因果状态空间模型是建模具有局部爆炸性增长后急剧下降特征时间序列的有效工具，在金融泡沫检测等应用中具有显著优势，为未来相关研究提供了理论和推断基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study causal non-causal state space models to model time series characterised by a local explosive increase followed by a sharp decrease such as stock prices. To motivate the use of causal non-causal state space models, we show that the causal non-causal convolution autoregressive model introduced by Gourieroux and Zakoian (2017) can be consistent with the rational expectations stock price model. As in a causal state space model, a central question is how to perform state and parameter inference in the causal non-causal state space model, which we discuss in the paper. We also study the causal non-causal convolution autoregressive model in more detail, providing some new results for the model. To illustrate the usefulness of causal non-causal state space models, we use the causal non-causal convolution autoregressive model to estimate the size of the dot-com bubble in both real time and a posteriori with the stable non-causal autoregressive model considered also by Gourieroux and Zakoian (2017) as a benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [It Takes Three to Converse: Empirical Observations on How the Developer, the Convener and the Participant Shaped 119 Polis Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28368)
*Lodewijk Gelauff*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究民主创新工具Polis平台上的异步公民参与过程，基于119个对话（100+参与者）分析平台开发者、召集者和参与者三方如何共同影响流程输出结果。研究发现平台设计选择（如分组数量限制、聚类算法）限定了结果范围，召集者的初始声明和政策设定基调，而参与者在有限信息下基于感知的可完成声明数量决定回复量。由于热路径聚类等选择，基于投票数据无法复现导出的聚类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polis作为一种流行的民主创新工具被广泛使用，但学术界对其输出结果的决定因素缺乏系统理解。理解平台设计选择、召集者设定和参与者行为如何共同塑造最终结果，对平台改进和民主实践有重要意义。

Method: 收集并分析了119个参与者≥100人的Polis对话，来自更广泛的271个流程数据导出。通过综合分析平台设计限制、召集者初始设定（初始声明、接受/拒绝策略、邀请机制）以及参与者投票行为模式来探究产出决定因素。

Result: 研究发现：(1)平台设计选择（报告组数限制为2-5组、声明优先级排序）限制了可能结果；(2)召集者的初始声明和政策设定为流程定调；(3)参与者仅接触不到一半的声明，当对话的声明显得可完成时回复更多；(4)由于热路径聚类等设计选择，导出的聚类结果无法基于投票数据重现。

Conclusion: Polis的输出结果由平台开发者、召集者和参与者三方共同塑造。建议召集者利用完整数据重新分析自己的对话，使其分析优先级显式化，以弥补平台设计选择带来的局限。

Abstract: Polis is a popular democratic innovation tool that allows asynchronous citizen engagement through atomic statements: short statements that together describe a complex question, inviting the citizen to vote Agree or Disagree on each. This paper uses 119 conversations with 100 or more participants and an extensive data export, drawn from a wider set of 271 collected processes.
  The paper asks what determines the output of such a process. Three parties shape the result. The developer of the platform has made important design choices that restrict the outcome: the number of groups the platform is able to report (restricted to 2--5) and which statements are prioritized.
  The convener defines the assignment: the initial statements that set the tone, the policy that accepts or rejects new statements and who can be invited. Finally, the participant works within these boundaries. With access to less than half of the generated statements, they end up responding to more statements when their conversation seems to have an achievable number of statements to complete, than when they are presented with more statements.
  Due to choices such as warm path clustering, the exported resulting clustering cannot be reproduced based on the voting data. Conveners may want to re-analyse their own conversations once the process is closed, to consider the data in its entirety, and make their own analysis priorities explicit.

</details>


### [249] [A Guided Inquiry Approach to Students Co-Designing Generative AI Course Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28501)
*Ashish Hingle,Aditya Johri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究探索了一项引导式探究活动，让学生共同设计生成式AI（GenAI）课程政策。学生先独立提出政策提案，再通过整合多方利益相关者视角进行协作完善。分析发现学生优先关注培训、AI使用披露标准和机构支持，并希望更多参与GenAI决策。政策共同设计活动能够呈现学生的价值观、关切和对GenAI的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学生中生成式AI使用日益增加，教育工作者面临如何在解决伦理和机构关切的同时支持学习的挑战。教育者需要了解学生对于GenAI使用的真实态度与优先考量，但目前缺乏对学生参与政策制定过程的实证研究。

Method: 采用探索性研究设计，实施引导式探究活动：学生先完成阅读材料，使用GenAI头脑风暴政策想法；随后通过书面作业和独立设计的课程政策表达个人观点；最后与同伴协作整合多方利益相关者视角，形成集体政策。通过对学生作品和小组讨论进行分析来回答研究问题。

Result: 参与者优先关注：1）为学生和教师提供培训；2）标准化AI使用披露程序；3）更强大的机构支持。参与者还希望更深入地参与GenAI相关决策，并将政策设计过程描述为一种接触多元视角及治理AI使用固有权衡的方式。

Conclusion: 本研究为政策共同设计活动如何揭示学生在教育情境中对GenAI的价值观、关切和意义建构提供了教学见解，表明让学生参与政策制定有助于促进更有效的GenAI治理。

Abstract: As generative AI (GenAI) use among students increases, educators face growing questions about how to support learning while addressing ethical and institutional concerns. This exploratory study examines a guided inquiry activity in which students co-designed a GenAI course policy. Students first developed individual policy proposals focused on appropriate and ethical use of GenAI, then collaboratively refined them by incorporating diverse stakeholder perspectives. The following research questions guided the study: 1) what practical factors do students prioritize in their GenAI use policies, and how do they justify these choices? and 2) how do participants reflect on the policy design process? Participants first completed readings, then used GenAI to brainstorm initial policy ideas. Next, they articulated their own perspectives through a written assignment and a course policy they designed individually. Finally, they incorporated diverse stakeholder perspectives by collaborating with peers to develop a collective policy. Analysis of student artifacts and group discussions showed that participants prioritized training for students and instructors, standardized procedures for disclosing AI use, and stronger institutional support. Participants also wanted greater involvement in GenAI-related decision-making. They described the policy design process as a way to engage with multiple perspectives and the inherent trade-offs involved in governing AI use. This study offers pedagogical insights into how policy co-design activities can surface student values, concerns, and sensemaking about GenAI in educational contexts.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [An Adaptive Augmented Lagrangian Method for Deterministic and Stochastic Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27676)
*Tianzhu Liu,Michael J. O'Neill*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非精确增广拉格朗日算法，用于求解非线性非凸优化问题。该方法采用自适应罚参数更新和完整对偶步长，在三类场景（函数与约束均确定、函数随机而约束确定、两者均随机）下均能达到已知最佳的增广拉格朗日方法最坏情况复杂度（最多相差对数因子）。在CUTEst测试问题和机器学习随机约束优化问题上的实验验证了该方法的实际优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来提出的具有最坏情况复杂度保证的增广拉格朗日方法通常采用固定或保守的罚参数更新策略以及较短的对偶步长，这虽然有利于理论分析，但在实践中可能表现欠佳。作者希望设计一种既能保持最优复杂度保证，又能在实际中更高效的方法。

Method: 提出非精确增广拉格朗日算法，核心创新在于：(1) 采用自适应罚参数更新机制，而非固定或预设的更新策略；(2) 使用完整的对偶步长（而非缩短的步长）。算法在确定性、函数随机、两者均随机三种设定下进行了复杂度分析。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法在三种场景下均匹配已知最佳的增广拉格朗日方法最坏情况复杂度（至多差对数因子）。实验上，在CUTEst标准测试问题上，该方法优于使用非自适应罚参数和/或短对偶步长的增广拉格朗日方法；在机器学习中的随机约束优化问题上的数值结果也验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 自适应罚参数更新配合完整对偶步长能够在保持最优理论复杂度保证的同时，显著提升增广拉格朗日方法在实际问题中的表现，为非线性非凸优化提供了一种理论与实践兼顾的求解方案。

Abstract: We present an inexact Augmented Lagrangian algorithm for solving nonlinear, non-convex optimization problems. Unlike most recently proposed Augmented Lagrangian methods with worst-case complexity guarantees, we utilize adaptive penalty parameter updates and full dual stepsizes. We show that the method matches the best known worst-case complexity results for Augmented Lagrangian methods (up to logarithmic factors) when both the function and constraints are deterministic, when the function is stochastic and the constraints are deterministic, and when both are stochastic. Experiments on CUTEst test problems confirm the practical advantages of the proposed approach over Augmented Lagrangian methods with non-adaptive penalty parameters and/or short dual step sizes in the deterministic setting. Numerical results on stochastic constrained optimization problems in machine learning also confirm these findings.

</details>


### [251] [Solving Quasi-Variational Inequalities Using the Progressive Decoupling of Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27694)
*Manoel Jardim,Claudia Sagastizábal,Mikhail Solodov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文受优化与变分不等式领域中"链接渐进解耦"方法论的启发，提出了一种将拟变分不等式（QVI）分解为一系列变分不等式（VI）来求解的算法，并在多种假设条件下证明了收敛性，同时在大型瓦尔拉斯均衡问题等应用中展现了良好的计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟变分不等式（QVI）比传统变分不等式（VI）更复杂，因为其可行集依赖解本身。现有的求解方法在处理大规模问题时效率有限，作者希望通过将QVI转化为一系列更易求解的VI来改进求解效率。

Method: 借鉴优化与变分不等式中"链接渐进解耦"的方法论，将拟变分不等式问题分解为一系列变分不等式子问题，通过迭代求解这些子问题来逼近原始QVI的解。

Result: 在正则性条件下证明了局部收敛性；当条件在全局成立时证明了全局收敛性。另外在"移动集"类QVI的假设下建立了线性收敛速率的全局收敛结果。在大型瓦尔拉斯均衡问题（广义纳什均衡的特例）及其他文献中的实例上展现了优越的计算性能。

Conclusion: 该算法为求解拟变分不等式提供了一种有效且可在多种条件下保证收敛的新途径，尤其在大规模经济均衡问题中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Inspired by the progressive decoupling of linkages methodology for optimization and variational inequalities, we propose an algorithm for solving quasi-variational inequalities as a sequence of variational inequalities. Our method is shown to converge locally under some regularity conditions and globally when such conditions hold throughout the entire domain. Separately, under other type of assumptions, global convergence with linear rate is also established for the class of quasi-variational inequalities said to have a moving set. Advantageous computational performance is shown for large-scale Walrasian equilibrium problems, a special case of generalized Nash equilibria, as well as for some other instances encountered in related literature.

</details>


### [252] [On the Computational and Statistical Efficiency of the Empirical Maximum Entropy on the Mean Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27705)
*Matthew King-Roskamp,Gabriel Rioux,Rustum Choksi,Tim Hoheisel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究经验最大熵均值（empirical MEM）方法的统计与计算效率。作者证明了经验MEM的参数化收敛速率从之前的O(n^{-1/4})提升到O(n^{-1/2})（期望意义下），并揭示了MEM对偶问题可重写为期望风险最小化问题，从而使其可以纳入现代随机优化的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEM方法通过熵正则化结合数据保真度解决逆问题，但实际中先验分布未知，需从数据中估计，形成经验MEM方法。此前King-Roskamp等人给出的收敛速率为O(n^{-1/4})，仍有改进空间；且MEM与随机优化的联系尚未被建立，限制了其在大规模逆问题中的应用。

Method: 作者通过对原问题和对偶问题在概率测度扰动下的稳定性分析（仅依赖凸分析和概率论的基础工具），建立了经验MEM的期望收敛速率为O(n^{-1/2})。此外，通过对偶问题的重新表述，将其转化为期望风险最小化问题，从而支持使用随机梯度算法进行大规模求解。

Result: 建立了经验MEM方法O(n^{-1/2})的参数化期望收敛速率，显著改进了此前O(n^{-1/4})的结果；同时证明了MEM对偶问题等价于期望风险最小化问题，使其可以兼容可扩展的随机梯度方法。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同表明经验MEM是一种在统计上高效且计算上可扩展的数据驱动逆问题求解方法，兼具良好的收敛保证和现代随机优化的适用性。

Abstract: The Maximum Entropy on the Mean (MEM) method provides a flexible computational framework for solving inverse problems by combining data fidelity with entropy-based regularization. In practice, however, the prior distribution is typically unknown but can be estimated from data, giving rise to the empirical MEM method. We establish a parametric convergence rate of $O(n^{-1/2})$ in expectation for empirical MEM, improving upon the previously established $O(n^{-1/4})$ guarantee by King-Roskamp et al. (2026). Our proof is based on a novel stability analysis of the primal and dual optimization problems under perturbations of the underlying probability measure, relying only on foundational tools from convex analysis and probability. We further show that the MEM dual problem admits a reformulation as an expected risk minimization problem, thereby placing MEM within the modern framework of stochastic optimization and enabling scalable stochastic gradient algorithms for large-scale inverse problems. Together, these results place empirical MEM as a statistically and computationally efficient methodology for data-driven inverse problems.

</details>


### [253] [Solution Methods for Infinite-Dimensional Generalized Disjunctive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27707)
*Daniel Nguyen,Joshua L. Pulsipher*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了广义析取规划（GDP）的求解方法到无限维优化领域，泛化了四种现有GDP求解方法（多重big-M、P-split、切平面重构和基于逻辑的外逼近算法），并提出了一种新的高斯过程变体MBM-GP，可高效学习无限域上的big-M函数，最终在Julia包中实现并验证了这些方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的InfiniteGDP抽象层将GDP建模扩展到连续域（如时间、空间、不确定性），但仅有big-M和hull两种松弛谱端点方法被推广到无限维设置，中间地带的多种GDP求解方法尚未被泛化，存在方法学空白。

Method: 将四种GDP求解方法（多重big-M重构、P-split重构、切平面重构、基于逻辑的外逼近算法）推广至无限维优化；提出MBM-GP算法，利用高斯过程从少量子问题求解中学习无限域上的big-M函数；在Julia包InfiniteDisjunctiveProgramming.jl中实现所有方法。

Result: 在动态和随机优化的案例研究中验证了泛化方法可优于big-M和hull方法，且MBM-GP能够在保持多重big-M紧度的同时大幅降低重构成本。

Conclusion: 这项工作填补了无限维GDP求解方法谱系中的空白，提供了完整的求解方法工具箱，并展示了高斯过程辅助学习策略在降低计算成本方面的潜力。

Abstract: Generalized disjunctive programming (GDP) expresses mixed discrete-continuous decisions through Boolean indicators and disjunctions, and can be systematically solved via a library of methods proposed in the literature. The recent InfiniteGDP abstraction lifts this modeling layer to continuous domains such as time, space, and uncertainty, but only the big-M and hull reformulations, the two endpoints of the relaxation spectrum, have been generalized to the infinite setting. This work closes this gap by generalizing four other GDP solution methods to infinite-dimensional optimization: the multiple big-M reformulation, P-split reformulation, cutting plane reformulation, and the logic-based outer approximation algorithm. It further proposes MBM-GP, a novel Gaussian-process variant of multiple big-M that learns the big-M function over the infinite domain from a small subset of the subproblem solves. Moreover, these approaches are implemented in the Julia package InfiniteDisjunctiveProgramming.jl. The methods are benchmarked on case studies arising in dynamic and stochastic optimization. The results demonstrate how the generalized solution methods can outperform big-M and hull, with MBM-GP retaining the tightness of multiple big-M at a fraction of its reformulation cost.

</details>


### [254] [Beyond Procrustes distances: a multilinear Gromov-Wasserstein distance capturing chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27774)
*Clément Soubrier,Geoffrey Woollard,Andrew Warren,Khanh Dao Duc*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多线性Gromov-Wasserstein目标的推广，能够区分形状及其镜像，从而解决现有形状分析度量无法检测手性（chirality）的问题。特别地，对于SO(d)对称群，作者引入了对手性敏感的手性Gromov-Wasserstein（CGW）距离，并建立了鲁棒性保证，同时开发了高效算法（包括局部和近似全局解），实现了完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。数值实验验证了CGW作为手性物体形状度量的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状数据分析在许多科学领域至关重要，手性是分子科学等众多应用中的基本属性，但现有的形状分析度量无法区分形状与其镜像，这是一个关键的研究空白。

Method: 提出多线性Gromov-Wasserstein目标的推广，将形状表示为概率分布并按对称群G取商；对G=SO(d)引入手性Gromov-Wasserstein（CGW）距离；通过将耦合投影到低维空间来重新表述优化问题；推导了局部解和近似全局解的算法，实现完全多项式时间近似方案。

Result: 在温和假设下，该目标在商空间上产生距离；建立了多线性Gromov-Wasserstein距离的鲁棒性性质；数值实验证明了CGW作为手性物体形状度量的有效性。

Conclusion: CGW距离成功填补了形状分析中对手性敏感度量的空白，具有理论保证（距离性质、鲁棒性）和高效算法（FPTAS），为手性物体的形状分析提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficiently and robustly analyzing shape data is critical across many scientific disciplines. While chirality is a fundamental property in numerous applications - most notably in molecular science - existing shape analysis metrics fail to distinguish between a shape and its mirror image. To address this gap, we introduce a multilinear generalization of the Gromov-Wasserstein objective. Under mild assumptions, this objective yields a distance between shapes, represented as probability distributions quotiented by a symmetry group $G$. In particular, for $G = SO(d)$, we introduce the Chiral Gromov-Wasserstein ($\mathrm{CGW}$) distance, sensitive to chirality. We establish robustness properties for the multilinear Gromov-Wasserstein distances and develop efficient algorithms to compute them, reformulating the underlying optimization problem by projecting couplings onto a low-dimensional space. We derive algorithms for both local and approximate global solutions, yielding a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme for these problems. We validate the framework through numerical experiments that demonstrate the effectiveness of $\mathrm{CGW}$ as a shape metric for chiral objects.

</details>


### [255] [Optimal control of fractional diffusion with Dirac measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27776)
*Enrique Otarola,Abner J. Salgado*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一个以谱分数阶拉普拉斯算子椭圆方程和狄拉克测度线性组合为强制项的PDE约束优化问题，控制变量是奇异源的振幅。作者证明了最优解的存在唯一性，推导了二阶充分条件（一阶最优性条件），提出基于有限元的离散化方法（由于控制变量是有限维的无需离散化），并给出了先验误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于需要控制带有奇异源（如点源/狄拉克测度）的分数阶椭圆方程，这类问题在实际应用中出现（如点源控制），然而此前缺乏对控制变量为奇异源振幅的理论分析和数值方法研究。

Method: 1) 理论上证明最优解的存在唯一性并推导一阶最优性条件；2) 采用有限元方法对状态变量进行离散化，由于容许控制集是有限维的，控制变量无需离散化；3) 推导离散解的先验误差界。

Result: 证明了最优解的存在唯一性，建立了最优性条件，并获得了基于有限元离散化的先验误差估计。

Conclusion: 本文为带奇异源的分数阶拉普拉斯方程提供了完整的理论分析与数值离散框架，控制变量因有限维性无需离散化，有限元方法能获得可证明的误差界，为后续算法设计和实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study a PDE-constrained optimization problem for an elliptic equation with the spectral fractional Laplacian and a linear combination of Dirac measures as the forcing term; the controls are the amplitudes of these singular sources. We prove existence and uniqueness of an optimal solution and derive first-order optimality conditions. We then propose a discretization based on finite elements. Since the set of admissible controls is finite dimensional, the control variable itself does not require discretization. We conclude by deriving a priori error bounds

</details>


### [256] [Anchored Scenario Coverage for Failure-Aware First-Hit Batch Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27873)
*Chuhan Yang,Chenxi Wang,Linhan Wu,Yuyang Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC-SC（Anchored Risk-Constrained Scenario Coverage），一种批量采集方法，通过锚定强边缘候选并在风险支持约束下最大化预测目标场景的互补覆盖，改善失败闭环逆向设计中早期有效发现的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在失败的闭环逆向设计中，早期发现至少一个满足目标需求的有效设计是核心目标。现有的批量基线按乘积形式的边缘有效命中分数对候选项排名，但在预测不确定性下独立选择最高分候选项会产生冗余推荐，浪费实验预算。

Method: ARC-SC将强边缘候选保留为锚点，并将其余批量位置分配给在风险支持约束下最大化预测目标场景互补覆盖的候选，从而实现锚定的场景感知批量选择。

Result: 在超导和JARVIS材料性质基准的冻结预言机闭环模拟中，ARC-SC在首次命中发现上取得统计显著改进，并在更具挑战性的设计空间上保持具有方向性优势的首次命中性能。

Conclusion: ARC-SC作为一种POF锚定的、场景感知的批量策略，能够有效改善结构化实验失败下的早期有效目标发现。

Abstract: Early discovery of at least one valid design satisfying a target requirement is a central objective in failure-prone closed-loop inverse design. A natural batch baseline ranks candidates by a product-form marginal valid-hit score, but selecting the highest-ranked candidates independently can produce redundant recommendations under predictive uncertainty and waste the experiment budget. We introduce ARC-SC(Anchored Risk-Constrained Scenario Coverage), a batch acquisition method that preserves strong marginal candidates as anchors and allocates the remaining batch positions by maximizing complementary coverage over predictive target scenarios under a risk-support constraint. In frozen-oracle closed-loop simulations on superconductivity and JARVIS materials-property benchmarks, ARC-SC yields a statistically supported improvement in first-hit discovery and remains competitive with directionally favorable first-hit performance on more challenging design space. These results establish ARC-SC as a POF-anchored, scenario-aware batch strategy for improving early valid-target discovery under structured experimental failure.

</details>


### [257] [On Solving Continuous-Discrete Projection Filters via Sum-of-Squares Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28002)
*Muhammad F. Emzir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续-离散投影滤波器中预测阶段的数值不稳定问题，提出利用平方和（SOS）松弛方法约束自然参数在容许域内演化。通过log-Cholesky映射参数化SOS矩阵，推导出能天然保持正定性约束的投影常微分方程（ODE），避免了先前半无限规划方法中昂贵的在线优化检查，并给出了高斯情形下的显式SOS松弛演化方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续-离散投影滤波器虽为非线性状态估计提供了严格框架，但在预测阶段存在数值不稳定性：积分误差会使自然参数超出其容许域，导致滤波发散或失效。已有的半无限规划方法虽能处理此问题，但需要在线优化检查，计算成本高昂。

Method: 采用平方和（SOS）松弛技术约束自然参数的演化路径；利用log-Cholesky映射对SOS矩阵进行参数化，从而将正定性约束转化为内在的结构性保证；最终推导出天然保持正定性约束的投影ODE，并提供高斯情形下显式的SOS松弛演化方程及理论推导。

Result: 得到了一个无需在线优化检查即可天然保持正定性约束的投影ODE演化方案，并给出了高斯情形下的显式SOS松弛演化方程。

Conclusion: 所提出的SOS松弛方法有效解决了投影滤波器预测阶段的数值不稳定性问题，在保持正定性约束的同时大幅降低了计算开销，为连续-离散投影滤波器的实际应用提供了更稳健且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The continuous-discrete projection filter offers a rigorous framework to approximate the solution of the nonlinear state estimation problems. However, it suffers from numerical instability during the prediction phase when integration errors force the natural parameters outside their admissible domain. To address this issue, we introduce the sum-of-squares (SOS) relaxation to constrain the evolution of the natural parameters within the admissible domain. By parameterizing the underlying SOS matrix using the log-Cholesky map, we derive a projected ordinary differential equation (ODE) that inherently preserves the necessary positivity constraints without requiring the computationally expensive online optimization checks associated with previous semi-infinite programming approach. We provide a theoretical derivation of this positivity-preserving propagation scheme and present the explicit SOS-relaxed evolution equations for the Gaussian case.

</details>


### [258] [The AHI family of sum of squares polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28015)
*Shaon Naskar,Kanchan Rajwar,Sujeet Kumar Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的非负多项式族——AHI多项式（基于算术-调和不等式构造），证明其非负锥与SOS锥一致，同时属于SONC锥。将AHI族在乘法下封闭得到锥Π AHI，该锥仍为SOS但位于SONC和SDSOS锥之外，且成员资格具有无需求解半定规划的闭式证书。数值实验表明利用AHI稀疏性可比稠密SOS松弛快300倍以上，并能求解标准方法无法处理的40次多项式优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的SOS松弛虽然强大但计算代价高昂，尤其在高次多项式优化问题中受限于计算能力。作者希望构造一类非负多项式族，既能保持SOS的可验证性与非负性保证，又能显著降低计算复杂度，并探索其与现有非负性证书（SOS、SONC、SDSOS）之间的关系。

Method: （1）通过算术-调和不等式（AHI）构造一类新的非负多项式（AHI多项式），并给出其显式代数条件； （2）证明AHI多项式的非负锥与SOS锥一致，且每个AHI多项式同时是SOS和SONC； （3）对AHI族进行乘法封闭，构造更大锥Π AHI，证明其仍为SOS但位于SONC与SDSOS锥之外，并给出无需SDP的闭式成员证书； （4）提出基于AHI稀疏性的分解层次算法，将高次多项式优化分解为独立子问题。

Result: （1）AHI多项式同时属于SOS与SONC锥，且包含关系严格； （2）锥Π AHI是SOS但不在SONC和SDSOS之内； （3）数值实验表明AHI稀疏性带来超过300倍的加速，能求解高达40次的多项式优化问题； （4）因子化层次可将乘积分解为独立小子问题，通用稀疏性技术无法检测到该结构。

Conclusion: AHI多项式族为非负多项式提供了新的结构化证书，在计算效率上显著优于传统SOS方法，尤其在处理高阶多项式优化问题时展现了巨大潜力。该工作在非负多项式理论、锥论与全局优化之间建立了新的联系。

Abstract: We introduce a new family of non-negative polynomials, constructed via the arithmetic harmonic inequality, called AHI polynomials. We derive explicit algebraic conditions for this family and prove that, for AHI polynomials, the cone of non-negative polynomials coincides with the cone of sum of squares (SOS) polynomials. We then study their convexity, showing that although AHI polynomials are generally nonconvex, certain monomial substructures are SOS convex. We further locate the family precisely among the standard nonnegativities certificates; every AHI polynomial is simultaneously SOS and a sum of non negative circuit polynomials (SONC), and the containment in the intersection of these two cones is strict. By closing this family under multiplication, we obtain a cone Pi AHI that is, by construction, still SOS, yet we prove that it lies outside both the SONC cone and the smaller SDSOS cone. Moreover, membership in this cone admits a closed form certificate that does not require solving any semidefinite programs. Finally, we demonstrate the usefulness of these structures in optimization, numerical experiments indicate that exploiting AHI sparsity yields a computation time over 300 times faster than dense SOS relaxations and enables solving high degree polynomial optimization problems (up to degree 40) that standard methods cannot handle due to computational limits, and a factorized hierarchy for Pi AHI decomposes products into independent small subproblems that generic sparsity techniques do not detect.

</details>


### [259] [A Refined Parameter Condition in the Lyapunov Analysis of IGAHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28088)
*Samir Adly*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文改进了带Hessian阻尼的惯性梯度算法（IGAHD）的Lyapunov分析，通过保留两个此前被下界替换的系数，得到了更宽松的充分条件，使得对于任意固定的β>0，足够小的步长s>0都能满足新条件，从而保持原论文的结论有效。此外，对凸二次函数进行了谱分析，得到了Schur稳定性的充要条件以及目标和梯度的几何衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文中IGAHD算法的Lyapunov分析给出的条件0≤β<2√s排除了固定β>0时足够小的步长s>0的情况，这限制了算法的适用性。作者希望放宽这一条件，使算法在更广泛的参数范围内有效，特别是允许固定阻尼参数下使用足够小的步长。

Method: 1) 通过细化Lyapunov分析中的一个估计，保留了两个此前被下界替换的系数，推导出更宽松的充分条件。2) 对有限维凸二次函数进行单独的谱分析，通过模态矩阵的Schur稳定性分析，得到参数区域的充要条件及几何衰减性质。

Result: 得到新的更宽松的充分条件：0<βL√s<1+√(1+sL(1-sL))，其中0<s≤1/L。最简单的特殊情况是0<β<2/(L√s)。对于固定β>0和L>0，该条件在s足够小时满足。此外，对凸二次函数的谱分析给出了更大的参数区域，并得到了Schur稳定性的充要条件以及目标和梯度的几何衰减。

Conclusion: 通过细化的Lyapunov分析，本文成功放宽了IGAHD算法的充分条件，使其适用于固定阻尼参数下足够小的步长。对于二次函数，谱分析得到了更大的参数可行域，并提出了一个开放问题：是否存在不同的Lyapunov函数能够将扩展的谱域（部分或全部）推广到非二次目标函数。

Abstract: In a recent paper, Attouch, Chbani, Fadili and Riahi introduced the inertial gradient algorithm with Hessian-driven daming, called (IGAHD). Under the condition $0\leqβ<2\sqrt{s}$, where $β$ is the Hessian driven damping parameter and $s>0$ is the gradient step size, their Lyapunov analysis shows an accelerated estimate of the objective function as well as a weighted summability of the gradient for the case $β>0$. For any fixed value of $β>0$, this condition excluded sufficiently small step sizes $s>0$. The present note refines one of the estimates in this Lyapunov analysis by retaining two coefficients that were previously replaced by lower bounds. This leads to the following less restrictive and sufficient condition: $$0<βL\sqrt{s}<1+\sqrt{1+sL(1-sL)},$$ where $L>0$ is the constant Lipschitz of the gradient objective function and $0<s\leq1/L$. In particular, the simplest condition $0<β<\frac{2}{L\sqrt{s}}$ is sufficient. For fixed values of $β>0$ and $L>0$, this new condition is satisfied for sufficiently small $s>0$. Consequently, under this new refined sufficient condition, the conclusions in the original paper stay valid. For convex quadratic functions in finite dimensions, a separate spectral analysis leads to a larger region parameters. It gives a necessary and sufficient condition for Schur stability of the limit modal matrices, as well as the geometrical decay of the objective residuals and the gradients. This modal analysis raises the question of whether a different Lyapunov function would allow us to recover part (or all) of this extended spectral domain for non-quadratic objective functions.

</details>


### [260] [First-order approximations of multifunctions defined implicitly by "split" feasibility problems with applications to optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28319)
*Amos Uderzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类参数化"分裂"可行性问题解映射的一阶微分性质，给出了其图形（切触）导数的内外逼近，并将其应用于具有分裂可行性约束的数学规划最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "分裂"可行性问题在工程和系统生物学等众多领域有广泛应用，但关于其解映射的微分性质（如导数、灵敏性）的研究尚不充分，这限制了该类问题在优化理论和实际应用中的进一步发展。

Method: 针对由参数化"分裂"可行性问题定义的解映射（多值函数），利用问题数据的导数来刻画其图形（切触）导数的内逼近和外逼近，从而建立解映射的一阶微分性质。

Result: 成功提供了解映射图形导数的内外逼近表达式，这些表达式完全由问题数据的导数表示；并进一步讨论了这些结果在具有"分裂"可行性约束的数学规划最优性条件表述中的应用前景。

Conclusion: 该研究为分裂可行性问题解映射的灵敏度分析提供了理论基础，并为求解带分裂可行性约束的优化问题铺平了道路，具有工程和系统生物学领域的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: In the present paper, a study is made of first-order differential properties of certain multifunctions, which are defined as a solution map associated with a family of parameterized ``split" feasibility problems. The latter are a class of convex feasibility problems, whose specific structure makes them suitable for well-established applications in several areas of engineering and systems biology. As a result, inner and outer approximations of the graphical (contingent) derivative of the solution map are provided, which are expressed in terms of derivatives of the problem data. As an application, a perspective is also discussed for employing some of these achievements in the formulation of optimality conditions for mathematical programs with ``split" feasibility constraints.

</details>


### [261] [On Nonsmooth and Relatively Weakly Convex Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28530)
*Chen Jiang,Jiawen Bi,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个分量均为非光滑且非凸的复合优化问题，提出了确定性的Bregman近端次梯度方法，并将其扩展到基于模型的随机最小化方法，建立了O(ε⁻⁴)的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合优化在现代机器学习和信号处理中至关重要，但现有方法通常要求正则项为凸函数。本文旨在放宽这一标准假设，使方法能适用于两个分量均非光滑且非凸的更广泛场景。

Method: 1) 提出确定性Bregman近端次梯度方法，在次梯度上界条件下收敛，放宽了正则项凸性要求；2) 扩展到随机场景，开发基于模型的极小化方法，在相对Lipschitz条件下建立O(ε⁻⁴)收敛率；3) 进一步扩展框架，支持距离生成函数及其梯度仅通过随机oracle访问的情况。

Result: 确定性方法在次梯度上界条件下保证收敛；随机方法在相对Lipschitz条件下达到O(ε⁻⁴)的收敛率；框架可扩展到随机oracle访问距离生成函数及梯度的场景。

Conclusion: 本文通过放宽凸性假设和引入随机扩展，为一般非光滑非凸复合优化问题提供了统一的确定性及随机求解框架，显著扩大了Bregman近端方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Composite optimization plays a central role in modern machine learning and signal processing, as it offers a natural balance between data fidelity and structural properties. In this paper, we study composite optimization in the setting where both components are nonsmooth and nonconvex. We start with a deterministic Bregman proximal subgradient method that converges under subgradient upper-bound conditions. This approach relaxes the standard requirement on the convexity of the regularization term, thus accommodating a broader range of applications. To extend this to the stochastic regime, we develop a model-based minimization method under a relative Lipschitz condition and establish a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$. We also extend the framework with convergence guarantees to the setting where the distance generating function and its gradient are accessible only through a stochastic oracle.

</details>


### [262] [Some quartic control results for scalar-input systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28582)
*Karine Beauchard,Frédéric Marbach*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了四次项（quartic terms）在标量输入系统小时间局部可控性中的作用，提出了新的充分条件并识别了一族四次障碍，同时回应了Kawski 1987年的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于可控性的结果未能充分利用更多"好的"四次李括号，且缺乏对四次障碍的系统性识别；此外Kawski 1987年提出的关于构造能分离好与坏四次括号的Hall基的开放问题一直悬而未决。

Method: 1) 证明了一个新的可控性充分条件，同时利用比之前结果更多的好的四次李括号；2) 识别了一族四次可控性障碍，基于第二类坐标是控制的正定泛函的李括号；3) 证明了这些结果的互补性可作为Kawski开放问题的一个回答。

Result: 获得了新的四次李括号可控性充分条件，识别了一族四次障碍，并展示了结果与Kawski开放问题之间的联系，辅以示例和评注阐明这些概念的微妙之处。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对标量输入系统可控性中四次李括号的理解，建立了充分条件与障碍之间的互补关系，为Kawski开放问题提供了一种解答框架。

Abstract: We investigate the role of quartic terms in the small-time local controllability of scalar-input systems. First, we prove a new sufficient condition for controllability, which exploits simultaneously more good quartic Lie brackets than previous results. Second, we identify a family of quartic obstructions to controllability, relying on Lie brackets whose coordinates of the second kind are positive-definite functionals of the control. Third, we show that the complementarity of these results can be seen as an answer to Kawski's 1987 open problem concerning the construction of a Hall basis which somehow separates good and bad quartic brackets. We give examples and remarks illustrating some of the intricacies of these notions.

</details>
